        )
        assert response.status_code == 422


def test_incremental_package_mechanism_is_not_silently_ignored() -> None:
    with _context(asset_count=1) as fixture:
        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            package = db.get(MaterialPackage, fixture["package"].id)
            prompt_a = db.scalar(select(PromptVersion).where(PromptVersion.stage == "A"))
            assert package is not None and prompt_a is not None
            definition = WorkflowDefinition(
                workflow_key="space_image.incremental-prompt-c",
                name="增量提示词 C 机制",
                description="",
                owner="platform",
                allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]',
                status="active",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(definition)
            db.flush()
            manifest = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "lane_key": "incremental",
                "run_modes": ["incremental"],
                "nodes": [
                    {
                        "node_instance_id": "prompt_c",
                        "kind": "prompt",
                        "display_name": "提示词 C",
                        "config": {
                            "prompt_version_id": prompt_a.id,
                            "component_snapshot": {"content": {"stage": "C"}},
                        },
                    }
                ],
                "edges": [],
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
            }
            version = WorkflowVersion(
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                version="1",
                status="active",
                workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
                step_manifest_json=json.dumps(manifest["nodes"]),
                edge_manifest_json="[]",
                input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
                output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
                resource_policy_json=json.dumps(
                    {
                        "max_parallel": 1,
                        "queue_class": "production_batch",
                        "category_key": "space_image",
                        "lane_key": "incremental",
                        "run_modes": ["incremental"],
                    }
                ),
                canonical_hash="a" * 64,
                validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(version)
            db.flush()
            workflow_snapshot = {
                "workflow": {
                    "workflow_version_id": version.id,
                    "manifest": manifest,
                }
            }
            frozen_package = _copy_package_with_frozen_mechanism(
                db,
                package=package,
                workflow_version_id=version.id,
                mechanism_snapshot_json=json.dumps(
                    {
                        "schema_version": (
                            "category-incremental-mechanism-snapshot-v1"
                        ),
                        "category_key": "space_image",
                        "workflow_version_id": version.id,
                        "snapshot_hash": hashlib.sha256(
                            canonical_json(workflow_snapshot).encode("utf-8")
                        ).hexdigest(),
                        "workflow_snapshot": workflow_snapshot,
                    }
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            package_id = frozen_package.id

        response = _create(
            fixture["client"], package_id, "incremental-prompt-c"
        )

        assert response.status_code == 422, response.text
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "stock_mechanism_legacy_projection_unsupported"
        )


def test_incremental_package_rejects_tampered_frozen_mechanism_snapshot() -> None:
    with _context(asset_count=1) as fixture:
        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            package = db.get(MaterialPackage, fixture["package"].id)
            model = db.get(ModelConfig, fixture["model"].id)
            assert package is not None and model is not None
            _version, _prompt_a, _prompt_b, frozen_package = (
                _bind_compatible_incremental_mechanism(
                    db,
                    package=package,
                    model=model,
                )
            )
            db.commit()
            frozen = json.loads(frozen_package.mechanism_snapshot_json)
            frozen["workflow_snapshot"]["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0][
                "display_name"
            ] = "被篡改的读取步骤"
            # This test intentionally simulates storage corruption below the
            # database invariant; normal application writes cannot do this.
            db.execute(text("DROP TRIGGER trg_material_packages_no_metadata_update"))
            db.execute(
                text(
                    "UPDATE material_packages SET mechanism_snapshot_json=:snapshot "
                    "WHERE id=:package_id"
                ),
                {
                    "snapshot": json.dumps(frozen),
                    "package_id": frozen_package.id,
                },
            )
            db.commit()
            package_id = frozen_package.id

        response = _create(
            fixture["client"],
            package_id,
            "incremental-tampered-snapshot",
        )

        assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
        assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == (
            "incremental_mechanism_snapshot_hash_mismatch"
        )


def test_incremental_package_uses_frozen_mechanism_instead_of_profile_defaults() -> None:
    with _context(asset_count=1) as fixture:
        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            package = db.get(MaterialPackage, fixture["package"].id)
            model = db.get(ModelConfig, fixture["model"].id)
            profile = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                    EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "space_image"
                )
            )
            assert package is not None and model is not None and profile is not None
            _version, prompt_a, prompt_b, frozen_package = (
                _bind_compatible_incremental_mechanism(
                    db,
                    package=package,
                    model=model,
                )
            )
            # Deliberately disagree with the legacy profile. The frozen workflow
            # is the production authority and must not be rejected by these old
            # defaults before its own prompts/model are applied.
            profile.rubric_version = "legacy-profile-rubric"
            profile.prompt_a_id = None
            profile.prompt_b_id = None
            db.commit()
            prompt_a_id = prompt_a.id
            prompt_b_id = prompt_b.id
            package_id = frozen_package.id

        response = _create(
            fixture["client"],
            package_id,
            "incremental-frozen-authority",
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            jobs = db.scalars(select(EvaluationJob)).all()
            assert len(jobs) == 1
            assert jobs[0].prompt_a_id == prompt_a_id
            assert jobs[0].prompt_b_id == prompt_b_id
            snapshot = json.loads(jobs[0].category_profile_snapshot_json)
            assert snapshot["rubric_version"] == "mechanism-rubric-v1"
            assert snapshot["model_config"]["max_tokens"] == 1024
            assert snapshot["model_config"]["thinking_mode"] == "enabled"
            assert "reasoning_effort" not in snapshot["model_config"]


def _complete_job(
    db: Session,
    *,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    model: ModelConfig,
    prompt_a: PromptVersion,
    prompt_b: PromptVersion,
    needs_review: bool,
) -> EvaluationResult:
    policy = db.get(SamplingPolicy, 1)
    if policy is None:
        policy = SamplingPolicy(id=1, revision=1)
        db.add(policy)
        db.flush()
    bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        prompt_b=prompt_b,
        rubric_version="rubric-v2.1",
        engine_version="engine-test-v1",
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=policy,
    )
    aesthetic = {"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3", "dimensions": {}}
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=job.asset_id,
        job_id=job.id,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        strategy_snapshot_json=build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            db=db,
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
            sampling_policy=policy,
            aesthetic=aesthetic,
        ),
        precheck_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "classification": {
                    "scope_status": "in_scope",
                    "primary_category": "住宅",
                },
                "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "normal"},
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        aesthetic_json=json.dumps(aesthetic, ensure_ascii=False),
        scoring_json='{"formal":true}',
        raw_response_a='{"provider":"raw-a"}',
        raw_response_b='{"provider":"raw-b"}',
        score=80,
        level="L2",
        confidence=0.6 if needs_review else 0.95,
        needs_review=needs_review,
        review_stage="initial" if needs_review else "completed",
        model_id=bundle.model_id,
        prompt_a_version=prompt_a.version,
        prompt_b_version=prompt_b.version,
        rubric_version=bundle.rubric_version,
        engine_version=bundle.engine_version,
    )
    job.status = "completed"
    job.stage = "done"
    job.progress = 100
    db.add(result)
    db.flush()
    return result


def test_create_is_idempotent_and_creates_one_job_per_available_asset() -> None:
    with _context(asset_count=3) as fixture:
        first = _create(fixture["client"], fixture["package"].id)
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        body = first.json()
        assert body["duplicate"] is False
        assert body["status"] == "queued"
        assert body["job_counts"]["total"] == 3
        assert len(body["job_ids"]) == 3
        assert body["evaluation_package_id"] is None
        assert len(body["category"]["configuration_hash"]) == 64
        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            run = db.get(EvaluationProductionRun, body["id"])
            assert run.category_profile_hash == body["category"]["configuration_hash"]
            assert "encrypted-reference-must-not-escape" not in run.category_profile_snapshot_json

        duplicate = _create(fixture["client"], fixture["package"].id)
        assert duplicate.status_code == 200
        assert duplicate.json()["duplicate"] is True
        assert duplicate.json()["id"] == body["id"]
        assert duplicate.json()["job_ids"] == body["job_ids"]

        changed = fixture["client"].post(
            "/api/evaluation-production-runs",
            json={
                "material_package_id": fixture["package"].id,
                "category_key": "material_image",
                "idempotency_key": "production-request-1",
            },
        )
        assert changed.status_code == 409


def test_rejects_cross_category_empty_materials_and_incomplete_configuration() -> None:
    with _context(asset_count=1) as fixture:
        cross = fixture["client"].post(
            "/api/evaluation-production-runs",
            json={
                "material_package_id": fixture["package"].id,
                "category_key": "material_image",
                "idempotency_key": "cross-category",
            },
        )
        assert cross.status_code == 422

        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            empty = MaterialPackage(
                package_key="empty-package",
                name="空包",
                category_key="space_image",
                source="manual_upload",
                created_by=fixture["user"].username,
            )
            db.add(empty)
            db.commit()
            empty_id = empty.id
        rejected = _create(fixture["client"], empty_id, "empty-materials")
        assert rejected.status_code == 409
        assert "可用素材" in rejected.text

    with _context(asset_count=1, include_model=False) as fixture:
        missing_model = _create(
            fixture["client"], fixture["package"].id, "missing-model"
        )
        assert missing_model.status_code == 409
        assert "主评测模型" in missing_model.text

    with _context(asset_count=1, include_prompts=False) as fixture:
        missing_prompt = _create(
            fixture["client"], fixture["package"].id, "missing-prompt"
        )
        assert missing_prompt.status_code in {400, 409}
        assert "提示词" in missing_prompt.text


def test_reconcile_uses_job_review_and_automation_facts_and_recovers_from_blocker() -> None:
    with _context(asset_count=2) as fixture:
        created = _create(fixture["client"], fixture["package"].id)
        run_id = created.json()["id"]
        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            run = db.get(EvaluationProductionRun, run_id)
            jobs = db.scalars(
                select(EvaluationJob).where(EvaluationJob.batch_key == run.batch_key)
            ).all()
            jobs[0].status = "processing"
            db.commit()
        evaluating = fixture["client"].post(
            f"/api/evaluation-production-runs/{run_id}/reconcile"
        )
        assert evaluating.status_code == 200
        assert evaluating.json()["status"] == "evaluating"

        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            run = db.get(EvaluationProductionRun, run_id)
            jobs = db.scalars(
                select(EvaluationJob)
                .where(EvaluationJob.batch_key == run.batch_key)
                .order_by(EvaluationJob.id.asc())
            ).all()
            results = [
                _complete_job(
                    db,
                    job=job,
                    model=db.get(ModelConfig, fixture["model"].id),
                    prompt_a=db.get(PromptVersion, fixture["prompt_a"].id),
                    prompt_b=db.get(PromptVersion, fixture["prompt_b"].id),
                    needs_review=index == 0,
                )
                for index, job in enumerate(jobs)
            ]
            panel = ReviewPanel(
                evaluation_id=results[0].id,
                required_reviewers=1,
                status="collecting",
            )
            db.add(panel)
            db.commit()
            panel_id = panel.id
            reviewed_result_id = results[0].id
        first_review = fixture["client"].get(
            f"/api/evaluation-production-runs/{run_id}"
        )
        assert first_review.status_code == 200
        assert first_review.json()["status"] == "first_review"
        assert first_review.json()["pending_first_review_count"] == 1

        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            panel = db.get(ReviewPanel, panel_id)
            result = db.get(EvaluationResult, reviewed_result_id)
            final_review = HumanReview(
                evaluation_id=result.id,
                reviewer_name="一审定案",
                stage="initial",
                decision="corrected",
                corrected_level="L2",
                corrected_score=80,
                note="纠偏完成",
                corrections_json="[]",
            )
            db.add(final_review)
            db.flush()
            panel.status = "completed"
            panel.final_review_id = final_review.id
            panel.final_truth_json = '{"decision":"corrected"}'
            result.review_stage = "completed"
            result.needs_review = False
            case = OptimizationCaseQueue(
                category_key="space_image",
                idempotency_key=f"production-case:{run_id}",
                evaluation_id=result.id,
                final_review_id=final_review.id,
                source_type="human_review",
                prompt_version=result.prompt_b_version,
                severity="P2",
                case_json='{"schema_version":"optimization-case-v1"}',
                status="pending",
            )
            db.add(case)
            db.commit()
            case_id = case.id
        blocked = fixture["client"].post(
            f"/api/evaluation-production-runs/{run_id}/reconcile"
        )
        assert blocked.status_code == 200
        assert blocked.json()["status"] == "blocked"
        assert blocked.json()["blockers"][0]["code"] == "automation_disabled"

        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            automation = AutomationOptimizationRun(
                category_key="material_image",
                run_key=f"production-automation:{run_id}",
                base_prompt_version="production-B-v1",
                policy_revision=1,
                status="processing",
                dry_run=False,
                trigger_reason="case_threshold",
                case_ids_json=json.dumps([case_id]),
                frozen_input_json="{}",
                result_json="{}",
                created_by="worker",
            )
            db.add(automation)
            db.flush()
            case = db.get(OptimizationCaseQueue, case_id)
            case.automation_run_id = automation.id
            case.status = "processing"
            db.commit()
        mismatched = fixture["client"].post(
            f"/api/evaluation-production-runs/{run_id}/reconcile"
        )
        assert mismatched.status_code == 200
        assert mismatched.json()["status"] == "failed"
        assert mismatched.json()["error"]["code"] == "automation_category_mismatch"

        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            db.get(AutomationOptimizationRun, automation.id).category_key = "space_image"
            db.commit()
        recovered = fixture["client"].post(
            f"/api/evaluation-production-runs/{run_id}/reconcile"
        )
        assert recovered.status_code == 200
        assert recovered.json()["status"] == "optimizing"
        assert recovered.json()["blockers"] == []
        assert recovered.json()["automation_run_id"] == automation.id
        assert recovered.json()["automation"]["lifecycle_status"] == "processing"


def test_reconcile_links_real_final_package_without_approving_or_publishing() -> None:
    from test_evaluation_packages import _close, _fixture

    fixture = _fixture()
    client = fixture["client"]
    db = fixture["db"]
    try:
        source_item = fixture["sample_items"][0]
        material = MaterialPackage(
            package_key="production-final-source",
            name="最终包生产来源",
            category_key="space_image",
            source="manual_upload",
            created_by=fixture["user"].username,
        )
        db.add(material)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            MaterialPackageItem(
                package_id=material.id,
                asset_id=source_item.asset_id,
                original_name=source_item.asset.original_name,
                duplicate=False,
                position=1,
            )
        )
        profile = db.scalar(
            select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "space_image"
            )
        )
        if profile is None:
            profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                category_key="space_image",
                display_name="空间图片",
                status="active",
                allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                pipeline_config_json=json.dumps(
                    default_pipeline("space_image"), ensure_ascii=False
                ),
                created_by=fixture["user"].username,
            )
            db.add(profile)
        profile.rubric_version = "rubric-package-v1"
        profile.prompt_a_id = fixture["prompt_a"].id
        profile.prompt_b_id = fixture["base_b"].id
        profile.model_config_id = fixture["model"].id
        profile.dimension_schema_key = SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY
        profile.dimension_schema_version = ACTIVE_V13_VERSION
        add_active_v3_contract(db)
        db.commit()
        created = _create(client, material.id, "production-final-package")
        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        run_id = created.json()["id"]
        run = db.get(EvaluationProductionRun, run_id)
        job = db.scalar(select(EvaluationJob).where(EvaluationJob.batch_key == run.batch_key))
        source = db.get(EvaluationResult, source_item.source_result_id)
        production_result = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=source.asset_id,
            job_id=job.id,
            strategy_bundle_id=source.strategy_bundle_id,
            strategy_snapshot_json=source.strategy_snapshot_json,
            preprocess_json=source.preprocess_json,
            precheck_json=source.precheck_json,
            aesthetic_json=source.aesthetic_json,
            scoring_json=source.scoring_json,
            raw_response_a='{"production":"raw response"}',
            raw_response_b='{"production":"raw response"}',
            score=source.score,
            level=source.level,
            confidence=source.confidence,
            needs_review=False,
            review_stage="completed",
            model_id=source.model_id,
            prompt_a_version=source.prompt_a_version,
            prompt_b_version=source.prompt_b_version,
            rubric_version=source.rubric_version,
            engine_version=source.engine_version,
            risk_review_version=source.risk_review_version,
        )
        job.status = "completed"
        job.stage = "done"
        job.progress = 100
        db.add(production_result)
        db.flush()
        final_review = HumanReview(
            evaluation_id=production_result.id,
            reviewer_name="一审定案",
            stage="initial",
            decision="corrected",
            corrected_level=production_result.level,
            corrected_score=production_result.score,
            corrections_json="[]",
            note="进入自动优化",
        )
        db.add(final_review)
        db.flush()
        db.add(
            OptimizationCaseQueue(
                category_key="space_image",
                idempotency_key=f"production-final-case:{run_id}",
                evaluation_id=production_result.id,
                final_review_id=final_review.id,
                source_type="human_review",
                prompt_version=fixture["automation"].base_prompt_version,
                severity="P2",
                case_json='{"schema_version":"optimization-case-v1"}',
                status="completed",
                automation_run_id=fixture["automation"].id,
            )
        )
        db.commit()

        reconciled = client.post(
            f"/api/evaluation-production-runs/{run_id}/reconcile"
        )
        assert reconciled.status_code == 200, reconciled.text
        body = reconciled.json()
        assert body["status"] == "awaiting_review"
        assert body["evaluation_package_id"] is not None
        package = db.get(EvaluationPackage, body["evaluation_package_id"])
        assert package.status == "awaiting_review"
        assert package.reviewed_by is None
        assert package.published_at is None
        assert package.automation_run_id == fixture["automation"].id
        assert package.regression_run_id == fixture["regression"].id

        serialized = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).lower()
        for forbidden in (
            "raw response",
            "encrypted-reference",
            "api_key",
            "authorization",
        ):
            assert forbidden not in serialized
    finally:
        _close(fixture["engine"], fixture["db"])

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_allowance_settlement.py =====
"""The daily allowance must reserve what a material costs, not the context ceiling.

Measured on the gray: the frozen worst case prices the model's full 200k context
at the most expensive tier, so a material reserved 808,476 micros while actually
charging about 15,000. A day of 200 CNY therefore bought 247 materials instead of
thousands. Two changes fix that, and both have to stay safe in the same direction:
the reservation may only ever settle downwards, and an estimate may only ever
lower a reservation below the frozen cap, never raise it above.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.factory_material_stream import DailyAllowance

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 15, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
LIMIT = 200_000_000


def _allowance(tmp_path):
    return DailyAllowance(tmp_path / "allowance.sqlite", LIMIT)


def test_settlement_lowers_the_reservation_to_what_was_charged(tmp_path):
    allowance = _allowance(tmp_path)
    assert allowance.reserve(1, 808_476, NOW)
    assert allowance.usage(NOW) == 808_476

    allowance.finish(1, now=NOW, settled_cost=15_000)
    assert allowance.usage(NOW) == 15_000


@pytest.mark.parametrize("settled", [808_476, 900_000, 0, -1, None, "15000", 15_000.0])
def test_settlement_never_raises_or_accepts_a_non_integer(tmp_path, settled):
    # Only a strictly smaller positive integer may replace the reservation. A
    # larger one was never checked against the daily limit, and an unknown or
    # zero actual must not make the work look free.
    allowance = _allowance(tmp_path)
    assert allowance.reserve(1, 808_476, NOW)
    allowance.finish(1, now=NOW, settled_cost=settled)
    assert allowance.usage(NOW) == 808_476


def test_settlement_without_a_cost_keeps_the_old_behaviour(tmp_path):
    allowance = _allowance(tmp_path)
    assert allowance.reserve(1, 808_476, NOW)
    allowance.finish(1, now=NOW)
    assert allowance.usage(NOW) == 808_476


def test_settling_twice_does_not_lower_it_again(tmp_path):
    allowance = _allowance(tmp_path)
    assert allowance.reserve(1, 808_476, NOW)
    allowance.finish(1, now=NOW, settled_cost=15_000)
    allowance.finish(1, now=NOW, settled_cost=1)
    assert allowance.usage(NOW) == 15_000


def test_settling_an_unknown_task_is_a_no_op(tmp_path):
    allowance = _allowance(tmp_path)
    allowance.finish(999, now=NOW, settled_cost=1)
    assert allowance.usage(NOW) == 0


def test_a_settled_reservation_still_counts_against_the_same_day(tmp_path):
    # The contract keeps today's spend on today's books; settling lowers the
    # amount, it does not move the debit to another day.
    allowance = _allowance(tmp_path)
    assert allowance.reserve(1, 808_476, NOW)
    allowance.finish(1, now=NOW, settled_cost=15_000)
    assert allowance.usage(NOW) == 15_000
    tomorrow = NOW + timedelta(days=1)
    assert allowance.usage(tomorrow) == 0


def test_settlement_frees_room_for_more_materials_the_same_day(tmp_path):
    allowance = _allowance(tmp_path)
    for task in range(1, 248):
        assert allowance.reserve(task, 808_476, NOW), task
    # 247 worst-case reservations exhaust the day.
    assert not allowance.reserve(248, 808_476, NOW)
    for task in range(1, 248):
        allowance.finish(task, now=NOW, settled_cost=15_000)
    assert allowance.reserve(248, 808_476, NOW)


def test_a_reservation_cannot_change_amount_while_outstanding(tmp_path):
    # Estimates are deterministic per material, so a second reserve at another
    # amount means the quote moved underneath us and must fail closed.
    allowance = _allowance(tmp_path)
    assert allowance.reserve(1, 90_756, NOW)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="factory_reserved_cost_changed"):
        allowance.reserve(1, 90_757, NOW)


def test_the_ledger_accepts_the_raised_ceiling(tmp_path):
    from app.factory_material_stream import MAX_DAILY_ALLOWANCE_MICROS

    # 2026-09-16 再上调一个数量级：旧的 1000 元/天比业务目标本身还小。
    assert MAX_DAILY_ALLOWANCE_MICROS == 10_000_000_000
    DailyAllowance(tmp_path / "a.sqlite", MAX_DAILY_ALLOWANCE_MICROS)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="factory_daily_allowance_invalid"):
        DailyAllowance(tmp_path / "b.sqlite", MAX_DAILY_ALLOWANCE_MICROS + 1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="factory_daily_allowance_invalid"):
        DailyAllowance(tmp_path / "c.sqlite", 0)


def test_an_existing_ledger_may_be_raised_but_never_lowered(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "allowance.sqlite"
    DailyAllowance(path, LIMIT)
    # Raising is safe: every past reservation passed a tighter check.
    raised = DailyAllowance(path, 1_000_000_000)
    assert raised.limit == 1_000_000_000
    # Lowering would make days that were within budget look retroactively over.
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="factory_allowance_configuration_changed"):
        DailyAllowance(path, LIMIT)


def test_raising_the_ceiling_keeps_every_recorded_reservation(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / "allowance.sqlite"
    allowance = DailyAllowance(path, LIMIT)
    assert allowance.reserve(1, 90_760, NOW)
    allowance.finish(1, now=NOW, settled_cost=15_000)
    raised = DailyAllowance(path, 1_000_000_000)
    assert raised.usage(NOW) == 15_000
    assert raised.recorded() == {1}

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_claim_contract.py =====
"""Offline factory wire contract. No network client or deployment credentials."""
import hashlib
import importlib
import importlib.util

import pytest


def contract():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec('app.factory_claim_contract'), '缺少工厂领取报文校验边界'
    return importlib.import_module('app.factory_claim_contract')


def reply():
    return {'code': 1, 'msg': '请求成功', 'request_id': 'fixture-request', 'data': [
        {'task_id': 10001, 'res_type': 6, 'll_id': 20001, 'img_id': 30001,
         'class_id_1': 10, 'image_url': 'https://example.com/image.jpg'}]}


def test_form_preserves_explicit_service_case_and_signs_exact_wire_values():
    c = contract()
    form = c.claim_form(service='ResInternalLlm', timestamp=1760000000, limit=1)
    assert form == {'service': 'ResInternalLlm', 'action': 'claim', 'time': '1760000000',
                    'body': '{"limit":1}',
                    'key': hashlib.md5(b'0000000671mialcmlLlanretnIseR').hexdigest()}
    assert c.claim_form(service='ResInternalLLM', timestamp=1760000000, limit=1)['key'] != form['key']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('limit', [0, 101, True, '1'])
def test_invalid_claim_limits_are_rejected(limit):
    c = contract()
    with pytest.raises(c.FactoryContractError):
        c.claim_form(service='ResInternalLLM', timestamp=1760000000, limit=limit)


def test_response_preserves_identity_without_inventing_sla_time_or_source_version():
    c = contract()
    task, = c.parse_claim_response(reply(), requested_limit=1, allowed_res_types={6})
    assert (task.task_id, task.res_type, task.ll_id, task.img_id, task.class_id_1) == (10001, 6, 20001, 30001, 10)
    assert task.image_url == 'https://example.com/image.jpg'
    assert task.upstream_created_at is None
    assert task.source_version is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change', ['code', 'boolean_id', 'unknown_type', 'missing_image', 'duplicate', 'too_many', 'request_id', 'url'])
def test_unsafe_or_ambiguous_responses_fail_closed(change):
    c = contract()
    data = reply()
    if change == 'code': data['code'] = True
    if change == 'boolean_id': data['data'][0]['task_id'] = True
    if change == 'unknown_type': data['data'][0]['res_type'] = 99
    if change == 'missing_image': del data['data'][0]['image_url']
    if change == 'duplicate': data['data'] *= 2
    if change == 'too_many': data['data'].append(dict(data['data'][0], task_id=10002))
    if change == 'request_id': del data['request_id']
    if change == 'url': data['data'][0]['image_url'] = 'file:///private/image.jpg'
    with pytest.raises(c.FactoryContractError):
        c.parse_claim_response(data, requested_limit=2 if change == 'duplicate' else 1, allowed_res_types={6})


def test_empty_queue_is_valid_and_error_text_never_echoes_remote_payload():
    c = contract()
    data = reply(); data['data'] = []
    assert c.parse_claim_response(data, requested_limit=1, allowed_res_types={6}) == ()
    data['code'] = 401; data['msg'] = 'sensitive-remote-value'
    with pytest.raises(c.FactoryContractError) as error:
        c.parse_claim_response(data, requested_limit=1, allowed_res_types={6})
    assert 'sensitive-remote-value' not in str(error.value)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_classification.py =====
import pytest
from app.factory_classification import factory_class_name, unique_class_label
from app.incremental_input import _with_upstream_material_type
from app.incremental_correction import upstream_class_label
from app.upstream_type_short_circuit import resolve_upstream_type
from app.model_3d_su_category_seed import build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit

@pytest.mark.parametrize("kind,cls,label,level", [(1,11,"全景图","L2"), (6,2124,"家具模型",None), (1,1,"家具模型",None), (6,11,None,None), (1,999999,None,None)])
def test_factory_ids_freeze_into_panorama_input_without_cross_type_guessing(kind, cls, label, level):
    frozen = {"source_system": "factory-test-material", "source_content_id": f"factory-prod:{kind}:10:20:task:30", "source_version": "prod-task:30"}
    payload = {"material_type": "全景图", "factory_task": {"task_id":30, "res_type":kind, "ll_id":10, "img_id":20, "class_id_1":cls}}
    result = _with_upstream_material_type(frozen, payload)
    assert result.get("material_type") == label
    assert result["factory_class_mapping_version"] == "3dsu-class-id-1-20260921"
    decision = resolve_upstream_type(build_model_3d_su_upstream_type_short_circuit(), result)
    assert (decision or {}).get("level") == level
    assert factory_class_name(kind, cls) == label

def test_display_mapping_uses_authoritative_source_before_legacy_override(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("INCREMENTAL_UPSTREAM_CLASS_LABELS_JSON", '{"2124":"全景图"}')
    assert upstream_class_label(2124, 6) == "SU · 家具模型"
    assert upstream_class_label(11, 1) == "3D · 全景图"
    assert unique_class_label(2124) == "SU · 家具模型"
    assert upstream_class_label(11, 6) == "分类 11"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_confirm_contract.py =====
import pytest
from app import factory_claim_contract as contract


def test_confirmation_form_is_explicit_and_bounded():
    import json
    form = getattr(contract, 'confirm_form', None)
    assert form is not None, 'confirmation form missing'
    value = form(timestamp=1700000000, task_ids=[1, 2])
    assert value['service'] == 'ResInternalLLM'
    assert value['action'] == 'confirmClaim'
    assert json.loads(value['body']) == {'task_ids': [1, 2]}
    for ids in ([], [True], [1, 1], [0], list(range(1, 102))):
        with pytest.raises(contract.FactoryContractError):
            form(timestamp=1700000000, task_ids=ids)


def test_confirmation_requires_exact_disjoint_partition():
    parse = getattr(contract, 'parse_confirm_response', None)
    assert parse is not None, 'confirmation boundary missing'
    envelope = dict(code=1, request_id='synthetic', data=dict(
        confirmed_task_ids=[1], already_claimed_task_ids=[2], rejected_task_ids=[3]))
    assert parse(envelope, requested_task_ids=[1, 2, 3]) == {
        'confirmed_task_ids': (1,), 'already_claimed_task_ids': (2,), 'rejected_task_ids': (3,)}
    for data in [dict(confirmed_task_ids=[1], already_claimed_task_ids=[1], rejected_task_ids=[3]),
                 dict(confirmed_task_ids=[1], already_claimed_task_ids=[], rejected_task_ids=[3]),
                 dict(confirmed_task_ids=[1, 4], already_claimed_task_ids=[2], rejected_task_ids=[3]),
                 dict(confirmed_task_ids=[True], already_claimed_task_ids=[2], rejected_task_ids=[3])]:
        with pytest.raises(contract.FactoryContractError):
            parse(dict(envelope, data=data), requested_task_ids=[1, 2, 3])


def test_observed_test_success_code_200_still_requires_complete_task_structure():
    envelope = dict(code=200, msg='请求成功', request_id='synthetic', data=[dict(
        task_id=1,res_type=1,ll_id=2,img_id=3,class_id_1=4,image_url='https://example.invalid/a.jpg')])
    assert len(contract.parse_claim_response(envelope, requested_limit=1, allowed_res_types={1,6})) == 1
    with pytest.raises(contract.FactoryContractError):
        contract.parse_claim_response(dict(envelope, data=[{'task_id':1}]), requested_limit=1, allowed_res_types={1,6})

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_confirm_journal.py =====
import json
import sqlite3
import pytest
from app import factory_test_claim as client


def test_confirmation_switch_and_production_target_fail_before_io(tmp_path):
    for options in ({'enabled': False}, {'enabled': True, 'url': 'https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory'}):
        with pytest.raises(client.ClaimGateError):
            client.confirm_received_once(tmp_path/'absent.sqlite', **options,
                send=lambda *a: pytest.fail('forbidden send'))
    assert not (tmp_path/'absent.sqlite').exists()


def journal(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / 'private.sqlite'
    response = dict(code=1, request_id='synthetic', data=[dict(task_id=1, res_type=1,
        ll_id=2, img_id=3, class_id_1=4, image_url='https://example.invalid/a.jpg')])
    client.claim_once(path, enabled=True, send=lambda *a: json.dumps(response).encode())
    return path


@pytest.mark.parametrize('classification,expected', [('confirmed_task_ids','confirmed'),
    ('already_claimed_task_ids','requires_reconciliation'), ('rejected_task_ids','requires_reconciliation')])
def test_durable_confirmation_does_not_confuse_already_claimed_with_ownership(tmp_path, classification, expected):
    confirm = getattr(client, 'confirm_received_once', None)
    assert confirm is not None, 'durable confirm sender missing'
    path = journal(tmp_path)
    def send(url, fields):
        assert url == client.TEST_URL and fields['action'] == 'confirmClaim'
        with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
            assert db.execute('SELECT state FROM confirmations').fetchone() == ('sending',)
        data = {k: ([1] if k == classification else []) for k in
                ('confirmed_task_ids','already_claimed_task_ids','rejected_task_ids')}
        return json.dumps(dict(code=1, request_id='synthetic', data=data)).encode()
    assert confirm(path, enabled=True, send=send)['state'] == expected
    with pytest.raises(client.ClaimGateError):
        confirm(path, enabled=True, send=lambda *a: pytest.fail('must not resend'))


def test_confirmation_timeout_is_durable_and_not_automatically_retried(tmp_path):
    confirm = getattr(client, 'confirm_received_once', None)
    assert confirm is not None, 'durable confirm sender missing'
    path = journal(tmp_path)
    def timeout(*a): raise TimeoutError()
    with pytest.raises(client.ClaimGateError): confirm(path, enabled=True, send=timeout)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute('SELECT state FROM confirmations').fetchone() == ('unknown',)
    with pytest.raises(client.ClaimGateError):
        confirm(path, enabled=True, send=lambda *a: pytest.fail('must not resend'))


def test_live_stage_requires_confirmed_journal_and_remains_local(tmp_path):
    stage = getattr(client, 'stage_confirmed', None)
    assert stage is not None, 'confirmed staging gate missing'
    path = journal(tmp_path)
    target = tmp_path / 'local-source.sqlite'
    with pytest.raises(client.ClaimGateError):
        stage(path, target, local_asset_id=7, enabled=True)
    assert not target.exists()
    response = dict(code=1, request_id='synthetic', data=dict(confirmed_task_ids=[1],
        already_claimed_task_ids=[], rejected_task_ids=[]))
    client.confirm_received_once(path, enabled=True, send=lambda *a:json.dumps(response).encode())
    assert stage(path, target, local_asset_id=7, enabled=True)['staged'] == 1
    assert stage(path, target, local_asset_id=7, enabled=True)['staged'] == 0


def test_saved_listing_is_reparsed_without_network_and_without_confirming(tmp_path):
    recover = getattr(client, 'reconcile_saved_listing', None)
    assert recover is not None, 'offline listing recovery missing'
    path = journal(tmp_path)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        db.execute("UPDATE attempts SET state='unknown'")
        db.execute('DELETE FROM tasks')
    assert recover(path)['tasks'] == 1
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute('SELECT state FROM attempts').fetchone() == ('received',)
        assert db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE name='confirmations'").fetchone()[0] == 0

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_gray_init.py =====
"""The local gray installer must produce usable, closed, empty configuration."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys


def test_initializes_private_gray_without_any_runs_and_refuses_overwrite(tmp_path):
    root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    cmd=[sys.executable,str(root/'scripts/factory-local-gray-init.py'),'--directory',str(tmp_path)]
    env=dict(os.environ,PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE='1')
    result=subprocess.run(cmd,env=env,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=60)
    assert result.returncode==0,result.stderr
    config=json.loads((tmp_path/'factory-pump.json').read_text())
    assert config['confirm_enabled'] is False and config['max_pending']==1
    assert config['journal']==str(tmp_path/'source.sqlite')
    assert (tmp_path/'factory-pump.json').stat().st_mode & 0o077==0
    environment=json.loads((tmp_path/'environment.json').read_text())
    assert environment['DATABASE_URL']==f'sqlite:///{tmp_path/"platform.sqlite"}'
    assert environment['LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV']=='test'
    with sqlite3.connect(tmp_path/'platform.sqlite') as db:
        assert db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM production_runs').fetchone()[0]==0
        assert db.execute('SELECT model_execution_mode FROM category_incremental_mechanism_bindings').fetchone()==('simulated',)
        assert db.execute('SELECT enabled FROM incremental_automation_controls').fetchone()==(0,)
    with sqlite3.connect(tmp_path/'source.sqlite') as db:
        assert db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM tasks').fetchone()[0]==0
        assert db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM changes').fetchone()[0]==0
    assert (tmp_path/'images/placeholder.jpg').is_file()
    before=(tmp_path/'factory-pump.json').read_bytes()
    again=subprocess.run(cmd,env=env,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=10)
    assert again.returncode!=0
    assert (tmp_path/'factory-pump.json').read_bytes()==before

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_material_controls.py =====
from sqlalchemy import create_engine,select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from app.models import WorkflowVersion,ConnectorContractVersion,CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding
from app.connector_governance import set_connector_pause_state
from app.incremental_automation import set_automation_control
from test_factory_native_canary import test_received_task_reaches_local_sink_only_after_resume as setup_canary


def test_material_controls_require_exact_mode_hash_and_live_platform_gates(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    config=setup_canary(tmp_path,monkeypatch,1,pump_mode=True,configured=True,
        factory_projection=True,material_mode=True,setup_only=True)
    engine=create_engine('sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'platform.sqlite'))
    sessions=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
    from app.factory_material_intake import material_platform_controls
    with sessions() as db:
        version=db.get(WorkflowVersion,config['workflow_version_id'])
        digest=version.canonical_hash;definition=version.workflow_definition_id
        up=db.get(ConnectorContractVersion,config['upstream_contract_id'])
        upkey=up.contract_key
    scope={key:config[key] for key in ('journal','workflow_version_id','upstream_contract_id','downstream_contract_id')}
    scope.update(expected_workflow_hash=digest,expected_mode='simulated',confirm_enabled=True)
    read=lambda:material_platform_controls(sessions,**scope)
    assert read()==dict(poll=True,confirm=True,material=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        set_automation_control(db,category_key='model_3d_su',workflow_definition_id=definition,
            enabled=True,expected_revision=1,reason='test',actor='test');db.commit()
    assert read()==dict(poll=True,confirm=True,material=True)
    assert material_platform_controls(sessions,**{**scope,'expected_workflow_hash':'0'*64})==dict(poll=False,confirm=False,material=False)
    assert material_platform_controls(sessions,**{**scope,'expected_mode':'real'})==dict(poll=False,confirm=False,material=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        set_connector_pause_state(db,category_key='model_3d_su',mechanism_key='',contract_key=upkey,
            state='paused',expected_revision=None,reason='test',actor='test');db.commit()
    assert read()==dict(poll=False,confirm=False,material=False)
    engine.dispose()

import json,sqlite3

def test_material_cycle_never_stages_placeholder_and_obeys_lifetime_cap(env):
    journal,sessions=env
    from app.factory_material_intake import material_cycle
    def forbidden(*args):raise AssertionError('no new request after one owned task')
    result=material_cycle(journal,sessions=sessions,controls=lambda:dict(poll=True,confirm=True,material=True),
        max_tasks=1,send=forbidden)
    assert result['material_enqueued']==1
    assert material_cycle(journal,sessions=sessions,controls=lambda:dict(poll=True,confirm=True,material=True),
        max_tasks=1,send=forbidden)['material_enqueued']==0
    with sqlite3.connect(journal) as db:
        assert db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM changes').fetchone()[0]==0
        assert db.execute('SELECT state FROM tasks').fetchone()[0]=='material_enqueued'


def test_material_pause_retains_confirmed_task(env):
    journal,sessions=env
    from app.factory_material_intake import material_cycle
    result=material_cycle(journal,sessions=sessions,controls=lambda:{},max_tasks=1,
        send=lambda *_:(_ for _ in ()).throw(AssertionError('paused network')))
    assert result['material_enqueued']==0
    with sqlite3.connect(journal) as db:assert db.execute('SELECT state FROM tasks').fetchone()[0]=='confirmed'

def service():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec('app.factory_material_intake'), 'confirmed Factory tasks need a durable real-material intake'
    from app.factory_material_intake import enqueue_confirmed_materials
    return enqueue_confirmed_materials


@pytest.mark.parametrize('field,value', [('task_id',True),('res_type',2),('ll_id',0),('img_id','301'),('class_id_1',None),('task_id',902)])
def test_frozen_factory_identity_rejects_invalid_or_mismatched_fields(field,value):
    from app.incremental_input import IncrementalInputError,validate_factory_task_identity
    frozen=dict(source_content_id='factory-test:6:201:301:task:901',source_version='test-task:901')
    task=dict(task_id=901,res_type=6,ll_id=201,img_id=301,class_id_1=10)
    task[field]=value
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError):validate_factory_task_identity(frozen,task)

def test_confirmed_task_retains_identity_and_real_image_after_restart(env,monkeypatch):
    journal,sessions=env
    intake=service()
    assert intake(journal,sessions=sessions,enabled=False)=={'enqueued':0,'disabled':True}
    assert intake(journal,sessions=sessions,enabled=True)['enqueued']==1
    assert intake(journal,sessions=sessions,enabled=True)['enqueued']==0
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==1
        job=db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob));body=json.loads(job.manifest_json)
        assert body['material']=={'url':'https://images.example.test/real.png'}
        assert 'asset_id' not in body
        assert body['factory_task']=={'task_id':901,'res_type':6,'ll_id':201,'img_id':301,'class_id_1':10}
        assert body['source_time_kind']=='platform_first_observed'
    # Real decoder/storage/ingress, fake only the external byte download.
    data=io.BytesIO();Image.new('RGB',(8,8),'red').save(data,format='PNG');raw=data.getvalue()
    from app import incremental_materials
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials,'_download',lambda *_:raw)
    with sessions() as db:
        incremental_materials.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='test',now=datetime.now(timezone.utc));db.commit()
        job=db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob))
        assert job.status=='completed',job.error_code
        asset=db.scalar(select(Asset));assert asset.sha256==hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        event=db.get(ContentIngressEvent,job.event_id)
        assert json.loads(event.payload_json)['asset_id']==asset.id
        from app.models import ContentRecord
        from app.incremental_input import build_frozen_content_ingress_input
        frozen=build_frozen_content_ingress_input(db,event=event,record=db.get(ContentRecord,event.content_record_id))
        assert frozen.get('factory_task')=={'task_id':901,'res_type':6,'ll_id':201,'img_id':301,'class_id_1':10}, 'workflow snapshot must retain exact downstream task identity'
        from app.incremental_input import validate_frozen_content_ingress_input,frozen_input_hash,IncrementalInputError
        changed={**frozen,'factory_task':{**frozen['factory_task'],'class_id_1':11}}
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError,match='Factory'):
            validate_frozen_content_ingress_input(db,event=event,input_manifest=changed,
                input_manifest_hash=frozen_input_hash(changed),package_category_key='model_3d_su',package_item_asset_id=asset.id)

    journal,sessions=env

@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind',['already_claimed','foreign','unlisted_host'])
def test_bad_receipt_or_unapproved_image_cannot_enter_pipeline(env,kind):
    journal,sessions=env
    with sqlite3.connect(journal) as db:
        if kind=='unlisted_host':
            task=json.loads(db.execute('SELECT payload_json FROM tasks').fetchone()[0])
            task['image_url']='https://unapproved.example/real.png'
            db.execute('UPDATE tasks SET payload_json=?',(json.dumps(task),))
        else:
            response=json.loads(db.execute('SELECT response FROM confirmations').fetchone()[0])
            if kind=='already_claimed':
                response['data']['confirmed_task_ids']=[]
                response['data']['already_claimed_task_ids']=[901]
            else:response['data']['confirmed_task_ids']=[902]
            db.execute('UPDATE confirmations SET response=?',(json.dumps(response).encode(),))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):service()(journal,sessions=sessions,enabled=True)
    with sessions() as db:assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==0
    with sqlite3.connect(journal) as db:assert db.execute('SELECT state FROM tasks').fetchone()[0]=='confirmed'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_material_stream.py =====
"""Persistent allowance and stream effects; external HTTP only is replaced."""
import importlib.util
import io
import json
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import pytest
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import select, func
from test_factory_material_intake import env
from app.incremental_material_models import IncrementalMaterialJob

def api():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec('app.factory_material_stream'), 'continuous real-material intake needs persistent daily allowance'
    from app import factory_material_stream
    return factory_material_stream

def test_daily_reservation_survives_restart_and_midnight(tmp_path):
    cls=api().DailyAllowance
    now=datetime(2026,9,14,15,59,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    path=tmp_path/'budget.sqlite'
    q=cls(path,200)
    assert q.reserve(1,120,now)
    assert not cls(path,200).reserve(2,120,now)
    assert q.reserve(1,120,now)  # Replay is not another debit.
    tomorrow=now+timedelta(minutes=2)  # Beijing midnight, not UTC midnight.
    assert not q.reserve(2,120,tomorrow)  # Outstanding work carries over.
    q.finish(1,now=tomorrow)
    assert not q.reserve(2,120,now)  # Completing does not reset today's spending.
    assert not q.reserve(2,120,tomorrow)  # Prior-day work may have spent money today.
    assert q.reserve(2,120,tomorrow+timedelta(days=1))

def test_allowance_cannot_reprice_and_cannot_be_lowered_on_restart(tmp_path):
    # 2026-09-15: the ceiling may now be raised on restart, because every
    # reservation already recorded passed a tighter check than the new one and
    # the operator asked for the allowance to stop limiting a measurement.
    # Repricing a single material and lowering the ceiling stay refused: the
    # first would rewrite a debit, the second would make days that were within
    # budget look retroactively over it.
    cls=api().DailyAllowance
    q=cls(tmp_path/'budget.sqlite',200)
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    assert q.reserve(1,100,now)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):q.reserve(1,1,now)
    raised=cls(tmp_path/'budget.sqlite',201)
    assert raised.limit==201 and raised.usage(now)==100
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):cls(tmp_path/'budget.sqlite',200)

def run_cycle(env,monkeypatch,*,confirm_unknown=False,closed=False):
    journal,sessions=env
    journal=journal.parent/'stream.sqlite'
    image=io.BytesIO();Image.new('RGB',(8,8)).save(image,format='PNG')
    from app import incremental_materials
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials,'_download',lambda *_:image.getvalue())
    actions=[]
    def send(url,fields):
        assert url=='https://userapitest.3d66.com/api/factory'
        actions.append(fields['action'])
        if fields['action']=='claim':
            return json.dumps(dict(code=200,request_id='list',data=[dict(task_id=902,res_type=1,ll_id=202,img_id=302,class_id_1=10,image_url='https://images.example.test/a.png')])).encode()
        assert json.loads(fields['body'])=={'task_ids':[902]}
        if confirm_unknown:raise TimeoutError()
        return json.dumps(dict(code=200,request_id='confirm',data=dict(confirmed_task_ids=[902],already_claimed_task_ids=[],rejected_task_ids=[]))).encode()
    controls=lambda:{} if closed else dict(poll=True,confirm=True,material=True)
    kwargs=dict(sessions=sessions,controls=controls,daily_limit_micros=200,per_task_micros=120,max_pending=2,excluded_ids=[6,7],send=send)
    return journal,kwargs,actions

def test_stream_confirms_enqueues_once_and_budget_stops_new_list(env,monkeypatch):
    """开着日额度时，第二轮会被额度停住——这测的是额度账本，不是默认行为。"""
    import app.workflow_model_budget as budget
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget,'INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED',True)
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==1
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['stop']=='daily_allowance'
    assert actions==['claim','confirmClaim']
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==1
    with sqlite3.connect(journal) as db:
        assert db.execute('select count(*) from changes').fetchone()[0]==0
        assert db.execute('select task_id,state from tasks').fetchall()==[(902,'material_enqueued')]

def test_unknown_confirmation_is_never_retried(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch,confirm_unknown=True)
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['error']=='factory_confirmation_unknown'
    service(journal,**kwargs)
    # 要守的是「confirmClaim 只发过一次」：它会改上游归属，结果未知就更不能重发。
    # 断言写成 actions==['claim','confirmClaim'] 是过紧的——它其实靠日额度把第二轮
    # 停在发 claim 之前。额度取消后第二轮会再 claim 一次，而 claim 是只读的，
    # 不改任何归属，不违反这条不变量。
    assert actions.count('confirmClaim')==1
    assert actions[1]=='confirmClaim' and 'confirmClaim' not in actions[2:]
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==0

def test_closed_stream_has_no_external_effect(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch,closed=True)
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['stop']=='platform_paused'
    assert actions==[]

def test_bad_image_is_not_confirmed(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    from app import incremental_materials
    def missing(*_):raise incremental_materials.MaterialError('material_http_rejected')
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials,'_download',missing)
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['image_rejected']==1
    assert actions==['claim']

def test_repeated_listing_after_restart_does_not_reconfirm(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    kwargs['daily_limit_micros']=1000
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==1
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==0
    assert actions==['claim','confirmClaim','claim']

def test_excluded_previous_tasks_never_confirm(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    kwargs['excluded_ids']=[902]
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==0
    assert actions==['claim']

def test_pause_after_download_blocks_confirmation(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    from app import incremental_materials
    download=incremental_materials._download
    enabled=[True]
    def pausing(*args):
        enabled[0]=False
        return download(*args)
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials,'_download',pausing)
    kwargs['controls']=lambda:dict(poll=enabled[0],confirm=enabled[0],material=enabled[0])
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['stop']=='platform_paused'
    assert actions==['claim']

def test_lost_allowance_cannot_reset_spending(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    service(journal,**kwargs)
    from pathlib import Path
    Path(str(journal)+'.allowance.sqlite').unlink()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='allowance_missing'):service(journal,**kwargs)
    assert actions==['claim','confirmClaim']

def test_stream_cli_defaults_closed(tmp_path):
    import subprocess,sys
    from pathlib import Path
    script=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]/'scripts/factory-material-stream.py'
    result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(script),'--config',str(tmp_path/'missing')],capture_output=True,text=True)
    assert result.returncode==0
    assert json.loads(result.stdout)=={'enabled':False,'network_calls':0}

def test_terminal_job_after_crash_reconciles_without_reconfirm(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    service(journal,**kwargs)
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:
        job=db.scalar(select(IncrementalMaterialJob));job.status='failed';db.commit()
    with sqlite3.connect(journal) as db:
        db.execute("UPDATE tasks SET state='confirmed'")
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==1
    assert actions==['claim','confirmClaim']

def test_the_daily_allowance_no_longer_stops_intake(env,monkeypatch):
    """2026-09-16 老大裁决：进料侧这道日额度也取消。

    额度用完就停下来，停下来之后既不会自己恢复也没人处理——挡掉的是正常运行，
    不是误操作。预留和结算照常，账本一条不少，只是不再据此停止进料。
    """
    import app.workflow_model_budget as budget
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget,'INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED',False)
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    assert service(journal,**kwargs)['material_enqueued']==1
    # 同样的第二轮：额度早就超了（limit 200，per_task 120），但不再是 daily_allowance。
    assert service(journal,**kwargs).get('stop')!='daily_allowance'


def test_the_allowance_ledger_still_records_every_reservation(env,monkeypatch):
    import app.workflow_model_budget as budget
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget,'INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED',False)
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    service(journal,**kwargs)
    with sqlite3.connect(str(journal)+'.allowance.sqlite') as db:
        rows=db.execute('select task_id,cost from reservations').fetchall()
    assert rows==[(902,120)], '取消的是「到点就停」，不是记账'


def run_multi(env,monkeypatch,*,upstream_tasks):
    """每次 claim 依次吐出 upstream_tasks 里的下一条；吐完返回空列表。"""
    journal,sessions=env
    journal=journal.parent/'stream.sqlite'
    image=io.BytesIO();Image.new('RGB',(8,8)).save(image,format='PNG')
    from app import incremental_materials
    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_materials,'_download',lambda *_:image.getvalue())
    actions=[];queue=list(upstream_tasks)
    def send(url,fields):
        actions.append(fields['action'])
        if fields['action']=='claim':
            data=[dict(task_id=queue.pop(0),res_type=1,ll_id=1,img_id=1,class_id_1=10,image_url='https://images.example.test/a.png')] if queue else []
            return json.dumps(dict(code=200,request_id='list',data=data)).encode()
        ids=json.loads(fields['body'])['task_ids']
        return json.dumps(dict(code=200,request_id='confirm',data=dict(confirmed_task_ids=ids,already_claimed_task_ids=[],rejected_task_ids=[]))).encode()
    kwargs=dict(sessions=sessions,controls=lambda:dict(poll=True,confirm=True,material=True),daily_limit_micros=10_000_000,per_task_micros=120,max_pending=50,excluded_ids=[],send=send)
    return journal,kwargs,actions

def test_per_cycle_takes_several_materials_in_one_run(env,monkeypatch):
    """每 10 秒一轮、每轮一条，供给上限就是 6 条/分（2026-09-17 上午 490 轮只入队 366 条）。"""
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_multi(env,monkeypatch,upstream_tasks=[911,912,913,914])
    result=service(journal,per_cycle=3,**kwargs)
    assert result['material_enqueued']==3 and result['cycles']==3 and 'stop' not in result
    assert actions==['claim','confirmClaim']*3
    with kwargs['sessions']() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IncrementalMaterialJob))==3

def test_per_cycle_stops_when_upstream_runs_dry(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_multi(env,monkeypatch,upstream_tasks=[921])
    result=service(journal,per_cycle=5,**kwargs)
    assert result['material_enqueued']==1 and result['cycles']==2
    assert actions==['claim','confirmClaim','claim']  # 第二轮 claim 为空就收手，不再打第三次

def test_per_cycle_stops_at_the_pending_ceiling(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_multi(env,monkeypatch,upstream_tasks=[931,932,933])
    kwargs['max_pending']=2
    result=service(journal,per_cycle=5,**kwargs)
    assert result['material_enqueued']==2 and result['stop']=='pending_limit' and result['cycles']==3

def test_per_cycle_of_one_is_byte_identical_to_the_old_behaviour(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    single=service(journal,**kwargs)
    assert 'cycles' not in single and single['material_enqueued']==1

@pytest.mark.parametrize('value',[0,31,1.0,'3',True,None])
def test_per_cycle_is_validated(tmp_path,value):
    journal=tmp_path/'m.sqlite';journal.parent.chmod(0o700)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='factory_stream_per_cycle_invalid'):
        api().stream_cycle(journal,sessions=None,controls=lambda:{},daily_limit_micros=200,per_task_micros=120,max_pending=2,per_cycle=value)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('bucket,state',[('already_claimed_task_ids','elsewhere'),('rejected_task_ids','rejected')])
def test_claimed_elsewhere_or_rejected_releases_its_reservation(env,monkeypatch,bucket,state):
    # 复审 09-26：被他人认领/被拒的任务永远没有素材 job，旧结算只看 job，预留
    # 永不释放，攒满 max_pending 后进料永久停。确认结果为 elsewhere/rejected 的
    # 预留必须释放（与灵感图进料一致）；unknown 仍保留。
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    send=kwargs['send']
    def other(url,fields):
        if fields['action']=='confirmClaim':
            data=dict(confirmed_task_ids=[],already_claimed_task_ids=[],rejected_task_ids=[]);data[bucket]=[902]
            actions.append(fields['action'])
            return json.dumps(dict(code=200,request_id='confirm',data=data)).encode()
        return send(url,fields)
    kwargs['send']=other
    service(journal,**kwargs)
    with sqlite3.connect(journal) as db:
        assert db.execute('select state from tasks where task_id=902').fetchone()[0]==state
    allowance=api().DailyAllowance(str(journal)+'.allowance.sqlite',200)
    assert allowance.outstanding()==[902]
    service(journal,**kwargs)
    assert allowance.outstanding()==[]


def test_unknown_confirmation_keeps_its_reservation(env,monkeypatch):
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch,confirm_unknown=True)
    service(journal,**kwargs);service(journal,**kwargs)
    assert api().DailyAllowance(str(journal)+'.allowance.sqlite',200).outstanding()==[902]


def test_one_unusable_claim_row_does_not_stall_the_page(env,monkeypatch):
    # 复审 09-26：上游一页里只要一条 res_type 不在 {1,6} 或 URL 非法就整页抛错，
    # 上游反复返回同一页时进料永久卡住。非法行逐条跳过，合法行照常处理。
    service=api().stream_cycle
    journal,kwargs,actions=run_cycle(env,monkeypatch)
    send=kwargs['send']
    def page(url,fields):
        if fields['action']=='claim':
            actions.append('claim')
            return json.dumps(dict(code=200,request_id='list',data=[
                dict(task_id=900,res_type=3,ll_id=200,img_id=300,class_id_1=10,image_url='https://images.example.test/x.png'),
                dict(task_id=901,res_type=1,ll_id=201,img_id=301,class_id_1=10,image_url='ftp://images.example.test/y.png'),
                dict(task_id=902,res_type=1,ll_id=202,img_id=302,class_id_1=10,image_url='https://images.example.test/a.png'),
            ])).encode()
        return send(url,fields)
    kwargs['send']=page
    result=service(journal,**kwargs)
    assert result['material_enqueued']==1 and result['claim_invalid']==2
    with sqlite3.connect(journal) as db:
        assert db.execute('select task_id from tasks').fetchall()==[(902,)]


def test_claim_parser_skips_only_when_asked_and_keeps_page_level_checks():
    from app.factory_claim_contract import FactoryContractError, parse_claim_response
    good=dict(task_id=1,res_type=1,ll_id=1,img_id=1,class_id_1=1,image_url='https://a.test/1.png')
    bad_type=dict(good,task_id=2,res_type=9)
    bad_url=dict(good,task_id=3,image_url='javascript:x')
    bad_id=dict(good,task_id=0)
    page=dict(code=200,request_id='r',data=[bad_type,good,bad_url,bad_id])
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError,match='factory_resource_type_unmapped'):
        parse_claim_response(page,requested_limit=10,allowed_res_types={1,6})
    skipped=[]
    tasks=parse_claim_response(page,requested_limit=10,allowed_res_types={1,6},skipped=skipped)
    assert [t.task_id for t in tasks]==[1]
    assert [(s['task_id'],s['code']) for s in skipped]==[
        (2,'factory_resource_type_unmapped'),(3,'factory_claim_image_url_invalid'),(None,'factory_claim_identity_invalid')]
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError,match='factory_claim_duplicate_task'):
        parse_claim_response(dict(page,data=[good,dict(good)]),requested_limit=10,allowed_res_types={1,6},skipped=[])
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError,match='factory_claim_limit_exceeded'):
        parse_claim_response(page,requested_limit=3,allowed_res_types={1,6},skipped=[])

"""Mock 工厂套件：测试环境才允许的第三个工厂目标 + 明文素材源 + mock 服务本身。"""
from __future__ import annotations

    send_upstream_request,
)

MOCK = "http://mock.example.test:8089/api/factory"


def test_the_mock_target_only_exists_in_non_production_deployments():
    assert set(build_upstreams({})) == {TEST_URL, PRODUCTION_URL}
    assert mock_upstream_target({"LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV": "production", "LABEL_SYSTEM_FACTORY_MOCK_URL": MOCK}) is None
    assert set(build_upstreams({"LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV": "production", "LABEL_SYSTEM_FACTORY_MOCK_URL": MOCK})) == {TEST_URL, PRODUCTION_URL}
    targets = build_upstreams({"LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV": "test", "LABEL_SYSTEM_FACTORY_MOCK_URL": MOCK})
    assert targets[MOCK] == ("factory-mock", "mock-task:", "mock-task:"), "mock 任务号也从 1 起，前缀必须独立"
    assert targets[TEST_URL] == ("factory-test", "test-task:", "test-task:")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", [
    "https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory",   # 真域名不能当 mock
    "http://mock.3d66.com/api/factory",
    "http://mock.example.test/api/factory/",   # 结尾斜杠
    "http://user:pw@mock.example.test/api/factory",
    "http://mock.example.test/api/factory?x=1",
    "ftp://mock.example.test/api/factory",
])
def test_a_mock_target_that_could_be_confused_with_the_real_factory_is_refused(bad):
    with pytest.raises(ClaimGateError):
        mock_upstream_target({"LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV": "test", "LABEL_SYSTEM_FACTORY_MOCK_URL": bad})


def test_plain_http_material_sources_are_ignored_in_production(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON", json.dumps(
        {"factory-test-material": {"allowed_hosts": ["127.0.0.1"], "insecure_http": True}}))
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "production")
    policy = incremental_materials._policy("factory-test-material")
    assert policy["insecure_http"] is False
    with pytest.raises(incremental_materials.MaterialError):
        incremental_materials._url("http://127.0.0.1:8089/images/1.png", policy)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "test")
    policy = incremental_materials._policy("factory-test-material")
    assert policy["insecure_http"] is True
    assert incremental_materials._url("http://127.0.0.1:8089/images/1.png", policy).port == 8089
    with pytest.raises(incremental_materials.MaterialError):
        incremental_materials._url("http://evil.example/images/1.png", policy)


def _load_mock_module():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "mock-factory-server.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("mock_factory_server", path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


@pytest.fixture
def mock_factory(tmp_path):
    module = _load_mock_module()
    state = module.MockState(tmp_path / "state.json", "http://127.0.0.1:0")
    server = module.build_server("127.0.0.1", 0, state)
    port = server.server_address[1]
    state.public_base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    try:
        yield module, state, f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    finally:
        server.shutdown()
        server.server_close()


def test_the_mock_factory_serves_the_whole_claim_confirm_submit_loop(mock_factory, monkeypatch):
    module, state, base = mock_factory
    url = base + "/api/factory"
    monkeypatch.setitem(factory_test_claim.UPSTREAMS, url, factory_test_claim.Upstream("factory-mock", "mock-task:", "mock-task:"))
    with state.lock:
        created = state.seed(3, res_type=6, class_id=2124)
    now = int(time.time())
    tasks = parse_claim_response(json.loads(send_upstream_request(url, claim_form(service="ResInternalLLM", timestamp=now, limit=2))),
                                 requested_limit=2, allowed_res_types={1, 6})
    assert [t.task_id for t in tasks] == created[:2] and tasks[0].class_id_1 == 2124 and tasks[0].res_type == 6
    assert tasks[0].image_url == f"{base}/images/{created[0]}.png"
    outcome = parse_confirm_response(json.loads(send_upstream_request(url, confirm_form(timestamp=now, task_ids=[created[0], 999]))),
                                     requested_task_ids=[created[0], 999])
    assert outcome == {"confirmed_task_ids": (created[0],), "already_claimed_task_ids": (), "rejected_task_ids": (999,)}
    again = parse_confirm_response(json.loads(send_upstream_request(url, confirm_form(timestamp=now, task_ids=[created[0]]))),
                                   requested_task_ids=[created[0]])
    assert again["already_claimed_task_ids"] == (created[0],), "重复确认要报 already_claimed，不能再次确认"
    # 已领取的不再被列出
    listed = parse_claim_response(json.loads(send_upstream_request(url, claim_form(service="ResInternalLLM", timestamp=now, limit=10))),
                                  requested_limit=10, allowed_res_types={1, 6})
    assert [t.task_id for t in listed] == created[1:]
    body = {"task_id": created[0], "grade": 9, "score": 78, "title": "t", "seo_title": "s", "category": "c", "style": "s",
            "cons": "c", "design": "d", "tags": ["a", "b", "c", "d"], "reason": "r", "hwreason": "h"}
    accepted = parse_submit_response(json.loads(send_upstream_request(url, submit_form(timestamp=now, body=body))), task_id=created[0])
    assert accepted == {"task_id": created[0], "accepted": True, "request_id": accepted["request_id"], "status": 1}
    with state.lock:
        assert state.tasks[created[0]]["state"] == "submitted" and state.submissions[-1]["body"]["grade"] == 9
    # 注入一次失败：平台侧会当成投递失败进入重试/失败待处理
    with state.lock:
        state.fail_submit_remaining = 1
    from app.factory_claim_contract import FactoryContractError
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError):
        parse_submit_response(json.loads(send_upstream_request(url, submit_form(timestamp=now, body={**body, "task_id": created[1]}))), task_id=created[1])
    # 签名错误一律拒绝
    fields = claim_form(service="ResInternalLLM", timestamp=now, limit=1); fields["key"] = "0" * 32
    assert json.loads(send_upstream_request(url, fields))["code"] == 0
    # 素材图能按测试环境的明文策略下载，并且是合法 PNG
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "test")
    monkeypatch.setenv("INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON", json.dumps(
        {"factory-test-material": {"allowed_hosts": ["127.0.0.1"], "max_images": 1, "insecure_http": True}}))
    data = incremental_materials._download(tasks[0].image_url, incremental_materials._policy("factory-test-material"))
    with Image.open(io.BytesIO(data)) as image:
        image.verify()
    assert data[:8] == b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"
    # 状态落盘后重启还在
    reloaded = module.MockState(state.path, state.public_base)
    assert reloaded.tasks[created[0]]["state"] == "submitted" and len(reloaded.submissions) == 1


def _load_provider_module():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "mock-provider-server.py"
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("mock_provider_server", path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_the_mock_provider_answers_a_and_b_prompts_with_real_shaped_content():
    import urllib.request
    module = _load_provider_module()
    canned = json.loads((Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "mock-provider-canned.json").read_text())
    server = module.build_server("127.0.0.1", 0, canned)
    port = server.server_address[1]
    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True); thread.start()
    try:
        def call(system_prompt, path="/api/v3/chat/completions"):
            body = json.dumps({"model": "ep-bi-test", "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system_prompt},
                                                                     {"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "评一下"}]}]}).encode()
            req = urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}{path}", data=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": "Bearer x"})
            try:
                with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5) as r:
                    return r.status, json.loads(r.read())
            except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
                return e.code, json.loads(e.read())
        status, a = call("你是 3D/SU 模型素材的结构化识别专家")
        assert status == 200 and a["object"] == "chat.completion" and a["model"] == "ep-bi-test"
        parsed_a = json.loads(a["choices"][0]["message"]["content"])
        assert "classification" in parsed_a and a["usage"]["total_tokens"] > 0
        status, b = call("你是 3D/SU 模型素材的美感评分专家。", path="/api/v3/batch/chat/completions")
        parsed_b = json.loads(b["choices"][0]["message"]["content"])
        assert "aesthetic_score" in parsed_b, "B 提示按 system 里的「美感」识别"
        # 注入一次 429：平台侧应当按 RequestBurstTooFast 退避
        urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/mock/fail-next", data=b"times=1&status=429", method="POST"), timeout=5).read()
        status, err = call("你是 3D/SU 模型素材的结构化识别专家")
        assert status == 429 and err["error"]["type"] == "RequestBurstTooFast"
        status, again = call("你是 3D/SU 模型素材的结构化识别专家")
        assert status == 200
        state = json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/mock/state.json", timeout=5).read())
        assert state["counts"] == {"A": 2, "B": 1, "failed": 1}
    finally:
        server.shutdown(); server.server_close()


def test_the_provider_stub_redirect_only_applies_outside_production():
    from app.doubao import provider_stub_base_url
    stub = "http://host.docker.internal:8090/api/v3"

    assert provider_stub_base_url({"LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV": "test"}) is None
    assert provider_stub_base_url({"LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV": "test", "LABEL_SYSTEM_MODEL_PROVIDER_STUB_URL": stub}) == stub

def test_the_client_url_uses_the_stub_in_test_and_the_real_base_in_production(monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app import doubao
    client = SimpleNamespace(config=SimpleNamespace(base_url="https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3", api_path="/batch/chat/completions"))
    url_property = doubao.DoubaoClient.url if hasattr(doubao, "DoubaoClient") else None
    if url_property is None:
        pytest.skip("client class name differs")
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "test"); monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_MODEL_PROVIDER_STUB_URL", "http://host.docker.internal:8090/api/v3")
    assert url_property.fget(client) == "http://host.docker.internal:8090/api/v3/batch/chat/completions"
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "production")
    assert url_property.fget(client) == "https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3/batch/chat/completions"

"""Journal -> persisted source -> native simulated run -> SQL outbox readback.

Only the upstream HTTP boundary is fake. No copied dispatch/scoring logic.
"""
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import json
import socket

import pytest
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.models import Asset, CategoryEvaluationV3Config, ConnectorContractVersion, ProductionRun, RuntimeOutboxEvent, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.factory_test_claim import claim_once, confirm_received_once, stage_confirmed
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
