@pytest.mark.parametrize('res_type',[1,6])
def test_received_task_reaches_local_sink_only_after_resume(tmp_path,monkeypatch,res_type, journal_override=None, pump_mode=False, configured=False, setup_only=False, factory_projection=False, material_mode=False):
    def no_network(*a,**k):raise AssertionError('external connection forbidden')
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket,'connect',no_network)
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket,'create_connection',no_network)
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    import app.upstream_poll_tick as poll
    import app.outbox_delivery_tick as delivery
    import app.incremental_materials as materials
    from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_due_incremental_batches
    category='model_3d_su'
    engine=create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path/"platform.sqlite"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as conn:run_migrations(conn)
    sessions=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
    Image.new('RGB',(32,32),'white').save(tmp_path/'placeholder.jpg')
    raw=(tmp_path/'placeholder.jpg').read_bytes()
    mapping={k:k for k in ('content_id','source_version','category_key','occurred_at','event_type','asset_id')}|{'change_cursor':'seq'}
    sink_engine=create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path/"downstream.sqlite"}')
    with sink_engine.begin() as conn:
        conn.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE results (business_idempotency_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, mechanism_version_id INTEGER NOT NULL, payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL, external_record_key TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, review_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto', published_at DATETIME)")
    connector_overrides={}
    source_options={}
    if configured:
        from app.factory_pump import pump_once,platform_controls
        from app.external_datasources import compute_live_schema_fingerprint
        monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV','test')
        monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_SOURCE_REGISTRY_FIXTURE_3D',f'sqlite:///{tmp_path/"source.sqlite"}')
        monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_SOURCE_REGISTRY_FIXTURE_3D_TABLE','changes')
        monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_LOGICAL_MODEL_3D_SU_OUTPUT',f'sqlite:///{tmp_path/"downstream.sqlite"}')
        monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_LOGICAL_MODEL_3D_SU_OUTPUT_TABLE','results')
        # Initialize an empty journal, with every external-effect gate closed.
        if pump_mode:
            pump_once(tmp_path/'source.sqlite',asset_id=1,controls=lambda:{})
        else:
            assert journal_override is not None
            stage_confirmed(journal_override,tmp_path/'source.sqlite',local_asset_id=1,enabled=True)
        connector_overrides={'downstream':dict(adapter_key='sql-envelope-v1',schema_fingerprint=compute_live_schema_fingerprint('logical.model_3d_su.output',None,table_name='results'))}
        if material_mode:
            connector_overrides['upstream']={'adapter_key':'factory-test-list-confirm-v1'}
        source_options=dict(adapter_key='sql',schema_fingerprint=compute_live_schema_fingerprint('source-registry:fixture-3d',None,table_name='changes'))
    with sessions() as db:
        version=seed_incremental_versions(db,category_key=category,count=1,connector_overrides=connector_overrides)[0]
        nodes=json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
        for node in nodes:
            if node['kind']=='prompt':
                schema={'type':'object','properties':{'score':{'type':'integer','minimum':0,'maximum':100}},'required':['score'],'additionalProperties':False}
                if factory_projection:
                    field='aesthetic_score' if node['config']['asset_key']=='prompt_b' else 'production_fields'
                    schema['properties'][field]=({'type':'integer','const':81} if field=='aesthetic_score' else {
                        'type':'object','properties':{'title':{'type':'string','const':'测试模型'},
                        'tags':{'type':'array','items':{'type':'string'},'const':['木材']}},
                        'required':['title','tags'],'additionalProperties':False})
                    schema['required'].append(field)
                    if field=='aesthetic_score':
                        schema['properties']['aesthetic_evidence']={'type':'array','items':{'type':'string'},'const':['测试夹具：轮廓清晰、材质可辨']}
                        schema['required'].append('aesthetic_evidence')
                node['config']['output_contract']={'mode':'json_schema','strict':True,'schema':schema};node['output_schema']=schema
            elif factory_projection and node['kind']=='downstream_projection':
                node['config']['mapping']={'schema_version':'factory-result-v1',
                    'prompt_a_node_id':next(n['node_instance_id'] for n in nodes if n['kind']=='prompt' and n['config']['asset_key']=='prompt_a'),
                    'prompt_b_node_id':next(n['node_instance_id'] for n in nodes if n['kind']=='prompt' and n['config']['asset_key']=='prompt_b')}
        active=db.scalar(select(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).where(CategoryEvaluationV3Config.category_key==category))
        slices=split_legacy_revision({'category_key':category,'revision':1,'contract_hash':'c'*64,'contract':json.loads(active.contract_json),'classification_map':json.loads(active.classification_map_json),'subcategory_dimensions':json.loads(active.subcategory_dimensions_json)})
        policy=json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy['legacy_projection_snapshot']={'schema_version':'legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1','available':True,'workflow_fields':slices.workflow_fields,'presence':sorted(slices.presence)}
        version.resource_policy_json=json.dumps(policy);version.step_manifest_json=json.dumps(nodes)
        scope={'category_key':category,'workflow_definition_id':version.workflow_definition_id}
        set_incremental_mechanism(db,category_key=category,workflow_version_id=version.id,model_execution_mode='simulated',expected_lock_version=None,reason='test canary',actor='test')
        routing=configure_policy(db,**scope,expected_revision=0,mode='split',ratio_percent=100,ownership_contract='factory-test-only',reason='test',actor='test')
        set_admission(db,**scope,expected_revision=routing['revision'],paused=False,reason='test',actor='test')
        paused=set_automation_control(db,**scope,enabled=False,expected_revision=0,reason='test',actor='test')
        asset=Asset(original_name='placeholder.jpg',stored_name='placeholder.jpg',mime_type='image/jpeg',size_bytes=len(raw),sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),category_key=category)
        db.add(asset);db.flush();asset_id=asset.id
        source_contract=create_upstream_source_contract(db,**_contract_args(category_key=category,field_mappings=mapping,cursor_definition={'mode':'change','field':'change_cursor','failure_policy':'isolate'},poll_interval_seconds=1,page_size=1,**source_options))
        target=db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction=='downstream'))
        upstream=db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction=='upstream'))
        pump_config=dict(journal=tmp_path/'source.sqlite',source_contract_id=source_contract.id,upstream_contract_id=upstream.id,downstream_contract_id=target.id,workflow_version_id=version.id,confirm_enabled=True)
        target_scope={'category_key':category,'mechanism_key':'','contract_key':target.contract_key}
        sink_pause=set_connector_pause_state(db,**target_scope,state='paused',expected_revision=None,reason='test',actor='test')
        fingerprint=target.schema_fingerprint;db.commit()
    if setup_only:
        assert configured and pump_mode
        engine.dispose();sink_engine.dispose()
        return dict(pump_config,asset_id=asset_id,confirm_enabled=False,max_pending=1)
    response={'code':1,'request_id':'synthetic-canary','data':[{'task_id':10,'res_type':res_type,'ll_id':20,'img_id':30,'class_id_1':4,'image_url':'https://example.com/a.jpg'}]}
    journal = journal_override if journal_override is not None else tmp_path/'claim.sqlite'
    if pump_mode:
        from app.factory_pump import pump_once
        def send(url, fields):
            return json.dumps(response if fields['action']=='claim' else {
                'code':200,'request_id':'pump-confirm','data':{'confirmed_task_ids':[10],
                'already_claimed_task_ids':[],'rejected_task_ids':[]}}).encode()
        for _ in range(2):
            pump_once(tmp_path/'source.sqlite',asset_id=asset_id,
                controls=(lambda:dict(platform_controls(sessions,**pump_config),stage=False)) if material_mode else (lambda:platform_controls(sessions,**pump_config)) if configured else lambda:dict(poll=True,confirm=True,stage=True),send=send)
        if material_mode:
            import io
            from app.factory_material_intake import enqueue_confirmed_materials
            monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_MATERIAL_SOURCES_JSON',json.dumps({'factory-test-material':{'allowed_hosts':['example.com']}}))
            image=io.BytesIO();Image.new('RGB',(24,24),'red').save(image,format='JPEG')
            real_image=image.getvalue()
            monkeypatch.setattr(materials,'_download',lambda *_:real_image)
            assert enqueue_confirmed_materials(tmp_path/'source.sqlite',sessions=sessions,enabled=True)['enqueued']==1
            assert enqueue_confirmed_materials(tmp_path/'source.sqlite',sessions=sessions,enabled=True)['enqueued']==0
    elif journal_override is None:
        claim_once(journal,enabled=True,send=lambda *a:json.dumps(response).encode())
        confirmation={'code':1,'request_id':'synthetic-confirm','data':{'confirmed_task_ids':[10],
            'already_claimed_task_ids':[],'rejected_task_ids':[]}}
        confirm_received_once(journal,enabled=True,send=lambda *a:json.dumps(confirmation).encode())
    if not pump_mode:
        stage_confirmed(journal,tmp_path/'source.sqlite',local_asset_id=asset_id,enabled=True)
    source_engine=create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path/"source.sqlite"}')
    @contextmanager
    def readonly():
        with source_engine.connect() as conn:
            conn.exec_driver_sql('PRAGMA query_only=ON');yield conn
    source=SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter(connection_factory=readonly,table_name='changes',field_mappings=mapping,schema_fingerprint='f'*64,cursor_definition={'mode':'change','field':'change_cursor','failure_policy':'isolate'})
    sink=SqlEnvelopeDownstreamAdapter(connection_factory=sink_engine.begin,table_name='results',evidence=DownstreamSafetyEvidence(True,fingerprint,'isolated-factory-test'))
    if not configured:
        monkeypatch.setattr(poll,'resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter',lambda _:source)
        monkeypatch.setattr(delivery,'resolve_configured_downstream_adapter',lambda _:sink)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker,'SessionLocal',sessions)
    monkeypatch.setattr(materials,'_storage_dir',lambda:tmp_path)
    def tick():
        with sessions() as db:
            if material_mode:materials.process_due_material_jobs(db,actor='test');db.commit()
            result=poll.poll_due_upstream_sources(db,actor='test',now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)+timedelta(hours=1))
            assert not result['errors'],result
            dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db,actor='test',now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)+timedelta(hours=1));db.commit()
        for _ in range(40):
            if not worker.process_runtime_step_once('factory-simulated-canary'):break
        else:raise AssertionError('bounded simulated run did not settle')
        with sessions() as db:delivery.deliver_due_outbox_events(db,worker_id='factory-test');db.commit()
    tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==0
        set_automation_control(db,**scope,enabled=True,expected_revision=paused['revision'],reason='resume',actor='test');db.commit()
    if configured and pump_mode and not material_mode:
        assert asset_id==1
        staged=pump_once(tmp_path/'source.sqlite',asset_id=asset_id,controls=lambda:platform_controls(sessions,**pump_config),send=send)
        assert staged['staged']==1 and staged['confirmed']==0
    tick()
    with sessions() as db:
        run=db.scalar(select(ProductionRun));assert run.status=='succeeded' and run.model_execution_mode=='simulated'
        if material_mode:
            frozen=json.loads(run.snapshot_json)['runtime_context']['input_manifest']
            assert frozen['factory_task']==dict(task_id=10,res_type=res_type,ll_id=20,img_id=30,class_id_1=4)
            assert frozen['asset_id']!=asset_id
            assert frozen['asset_sha256']==hashlib.sha256(real_image).hexdigest()
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status=='queued'))==1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowModelInvocation))==0
        set_connector_pause_state(db,**target_scope,state='active',expected_revision=sink_pause.revision,reason='resume',actor='test');db.commit()
    with sink_engine.connect() as conn:assert conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT count(*) FROM results').scalar_one()==0
    tick();tick()
    with sink_engine.connect() as conn:
        rows=conn.exec_driver_sql('SELECT payload_hash,payload_json FROM results').all()
        assert len(rows)==1
        assert hashlib.sha256(rows[0][1].encode()).hexdigest()==rows[0][0]
        if factory_projection:
            projected=json.loads(rows[0][1])['result']['output']
            assert projected['schema_version']=='factory-result-v1'
            if material_mode:
                assert projected.get('factory_task')==dict(task_id=10,res_type=res_type,ll_id=20,img_id=30,class_id_1=4), 'final local delivery must retain confirmed task identity'
            assert projected['prompt_a_fields']['production_fields']=={'title':'测试模型','tags':['木材']}
            assert projected['prompt_b_aesthetic_score']==81
            assert projected['final_level']==projected['normalized_result']['level']
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun))==1
        assert db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent)).status=='delivered'
    engine.dispose();source_engine.dispose();sink_engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_projection_barrier.py =====
"""Factory terminal delivery waits for the material, not only its direct parent."""
import json
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.models import RuntimeDispatchItem
from app import workflow_runtime as runtime
from test_workflow_runtime import _engine, _seed_active_workflow, _latest_attempts_for_test


@pytest.mark.parametrize('schema', ['factory-result-v1', 'inspiration-factory-result-v1'])
def test_factory_projection_is_not_dispatched_while_other_branch_runs(tmp_path, schema):
    engine=_engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version=_seed_active_workflow(db,parallel_join=True)
            run=runtime.create_production_run(db,runtime.ProductionRunRequest(
                workflow_version_id=version.id,idempotency_key='factory-barrier',
                category_key='model_3d_su',queue_class='validation',runtime_context={},
                input_manifest={},owner='test'),actor='test')
            latest=_latest_attempts_for_test(db,run.id)
            for key,row in latest.items():
                row.status='pending' if key=='join' else 'running' if key=='right' else 'succeeded'
                row.output_manifest_json=json.dumps({'status':row.status,'output':{'ok':True}})
            # The projection is a terminal branch; its other material branch is
            # not a direct predecessor. No network or provider runs in this test.
            version.workflow_schema_version='workflow-v3'
            version.step_manifest_json=json.dumps([
                {'node_instance_id':key,'kind':'downstream_projection' if key=='join' else 'prompt',
                 'config':{'mapping':{'schema_version':schema}} if key=='join' else {}}
                for key in latest])
            version.edge_manifest_json=json.dumps([{'from':'identity','to':'left'},
                {'from':'identity','to':'right'},{'from':'left','to':'join'}])
            db.flush()
            assert runtime._ready_pending_steps(db,run)==[]
            assert db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id==latest['join'].id)) is None
            latest['right'].status='succeeded'
            latest['right'].output_manifest_json=json.dumps({'status':'succeeded','output':{'ok':True}})
            db.flush()
            assert [row.step_key for row in runtime._ready_pending_steps(db,run)]==['join']
            assert set(json.loads(latest['join'].input_manifest_json)['upstream'])=={'identity','left','right'}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_pump.py =====
"""Catch duplicate acknowledgement, crash replay and ineffective pause gates."""
import json
import sqlite3

import pytest
from app import factory_pump as pump


def controls(**changes):
    return dict(poll=True, confirm=True, stage=True, **changes)


def listing(task=10, image=30):
    return {'code':200,'request_id':'test-list','data':[dict(task_id=task,
        res_type=1,ll_id=20,img_id=image,class_id_1=4,image_url='https://example.com/a.jpg')]}


def confirmed(ids, *, elsewhere=False):
    return {'code':200,'request_id':'test-confirm','data':dict(
        confirmed_task_ids=[] if elsewhere else ids,
        already_claimed_task_ids=ids if elsewhere else [],rejected_task_ids=[])}


def rows(path, table):
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        return db.execute('SELECT * FROM '+table).fetchall()


def test_restart_deduplicates_confirmation_and_monotonic_local_change(tmp_path):
    path=tmp_path/'pump.sqlite'; calls=[]; item=[listing()]
    def send(url, fields):
        assert url == pump.TEST_URL
        calls.append(fields['action'])
        if fields['action']=='claim':return json.dumps(item[0]).encode()
        assert json.loads(fields['body'])=={'task_ids':[item[0]['data'][0]['task_id']]}
        # Confirmation intent must be committed BEFORE transmitting.
        with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
            assert db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM tasks WHERE state='sending'").fetchone()[0]==1
        return json.dumps(confirmed([item[0]['data'][0]['task_id']])).encode()
    for _ in range(2):pump.pump_once(path, controls=lambda:controls(), asset_id=7, send=send)
    assert calls == ['claim','confirmClaim','claim']
    assert len(rows(path,'changes'))==1
    item[0]=listing(2)  # IDs need NOT increase across pages.
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:controls(),asset_id=7,send=send)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute('SELECT seq,task_id,asset_id FROM changes ORDER BY seq').fetchall()==[(1,10,7),(2,2,7)]


@pytest.mark.parametrize('crash',[False,True])
def test_unknown_confirmation_never_retries_or_stages(tmp_path,crash):
    path=tmp_path/'pump.sqlite'; calls=[]
    def send(url,fields):
        calls.append(fields['action'])
        if fields['action']=='claim':return json.dumps(listing()).encode()
        if crash:raise KeyboardInterrupt()
        raise TimeoutError('do not expose transport details')
    if crash:
        with pytest.raises(KeyboardInterrupt):pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:controls(),asset_id=7,send=send)
    else:pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:controls(),asset_id=7,send=send)
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:controls(),asset_id=7,send=send)
    assert calls.count('confirmClaim')==1
    assert rows(path,'changes')==[]
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:assert db.execute('SELECT state FROM tasks').fetchone()==('unknown',)


def test_pause_between_list_and_confirm_then_resume(tmp_path):
    path=tmp_path/'pump.sqlite'; flags=controls(); calls=[]
    def send(url, fields):
        calls.append(fields['action'])
        if fields['action']=='claim':
            flags['confirm']=False
            return json.dumps(listing()).encode()
        return json.dumps(confirmed([10])).encode()
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags.copy(),asset_id=7,send=send)
    assert calls==['claim'] and rows(path,'changes')==[]
    flags.update(poll=False,confirm=True,stage=False)
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags.copy(),asset_id=7,send=send)
    assert calls==['claim','confirmClaim'] and rows(path,'changes')==[]
    flags.update(confirm=False,stage=True)
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags.copy(),asset_id=7,send=send)
    assert len(rows(path,'changes'))==1 and len(calls)==2


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind',['elsewhere','malformed','identity'])
def test_uncertain_or_conflicting_task_never_stages(tmp_path,kind):
    path=tmp_path/'pump.sqlite'
    def send(url,fields):
        if fields['action']=='claim':return json.dumps(listing()).encode()
        return json.dumps({} if kind=='malformed' else confirmed([10],elsewhere=True)).encode()
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:controls(),asset_id=7,send=send)
    if kind=='identity':
        def changed(url,fields):
            assert fields['action']=='claim'
            return json.dumps(listing(image=31)).encode()
        result=pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:controls(),asset_id=7,send=changed)
        assert result['error']=='factory_task_identity_conflict'
    assert rows(path,'changes')==[]


def test_disabled_and_full_queue_do_not_send(tmp_path):
    path=tmp_path/'pump.sqlite'; calls=[]
    def send(url,fields):calls.append(fields['action']);return json.dumps(listing()).encode()
    flags=dict(poll=False,confirm=False,stage=False)
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags,asset_id=7,send=send,max_pending=1)
    assert calls==[]
    flags['poll']=True
    for _ in range(2):pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags,asset_id=7,send=send,max_pending=1)
    assert calls==['claim']


def test_atomic_stage_survives_failure_without_duplicate(tmp_path):
    path=tmp_path/'pump.sqlite'
    def send(url,fields):
        return json.dumps(listing() if fields['action']=='claim' else confirmed([10])).encode()
    flags={'poll':True,'confirm':True,'stage':False}
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags,asset_id=7,send=send)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        db.execute("CREATE TRIGGER reject_stage BEFORE INSERT ON changes BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'stage offline'); END")
    flags.update(poll=False,confirm=False,stage=True)
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags,asset_id=7,send=send)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute('SELECT state FROM tasks').fetchone()==('confirmed',)
        db.execute('DROP TRIGGER reject_stage')
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags,asset_id=7,send=send)
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:flags,asset_id=7,send=send)
    assert len(rows(path,'changes'))==1


def test_changed_placeholder_fails_before_network(tmp_path):
    path=tmp_path/'pump.sqlite'
    pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:{},asset_id=7)
    with pytest.raises(pump.ClaimGateError,match='configuration_changed'):
        pump.pump_once(path,controls=lambda:controls(),asset_id=8,
            send=lambda *a:pytest.fail('must reject before network'))

import json,subprocess,sys

def test_cli_defaults_disabled_without_opening_configuration_or_network():

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_pump_controls.py =====
"""Platform gates use real persisted rows, not a copied state machine."""
from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine,select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from app.database import Base
from app.models import ConnectorContractVersion,UpstreamSourceContract,CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.connector_governance import set_connector_pause_state
from app.readonly_sources import create_upstream_source_contract
from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions
from test_readonly_sources import _contract_args
from app import factory_pump as pump


def test_actual_connector_pause_and_binding_change_close_pump(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    engine=create_engine('sqlite://');Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions=sessionmaker(bind=engine,expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        version=seed_incremental_versions(db,category_key='model_3d_su',count=1)[0]
        set_incremental_mechanism(db,category_key='model_3d_su',workflow_version_id=version.id,
            model_execution_mode='simulated',expected_lock_version=None,reason='test',actor='test')
        upstream=db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction=='upstream'))
        upstream_key=upstream.contract_key
        source=create_upstream_source_contract(db,**_contract_args(category_key='model_3d_su',
            connection_locator='factory-test-staged',poll_interval_seconds=10))
        target=db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.direction=='downstream'))
        db.flush()
        config=dict(source_contract_id=source.id,upstream_contract_id=upstream.id,
            workflow_version_id=version.id,downstream_contract_id=target.id)
        locator=target.logical_locator;db.commit()
    import re
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_FACTORY_TEST_STAGED','sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'pump.sqlite'))
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_FACTORY_TEST_STAGED_TABLE','changes')
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_DATASOURCE_'+re.sub('[^A-Za-z0-9]+','_',locator).upper(),
        'sqlite:///'+str(tmp_path/'downstream.sqlite'))
    read=lambda:pump.platform_controls(sessions,journal=tmp_path/'pump.sqlite',confirm_enabled=True,**config)
    assert read()==dict(poll=True,confirm=True,stage=True)
    with sessions() as db:
        paused=set_connector_pause_state(db,category_key='model_3d_su',mechanism_key='',
            contract_key=upstream_key,state='paused',expected_revision=None,reason='test pause',actor='test')
        rev=paused.revision;db.commit()
    assert read()==dict(poll=False,confirm=False,stage=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        set_connector_pause_state(db,category_key='model_3d_su',mechanism_key='',contract_key=upstream_key,
            state='active',expected_revision=rev,reason='resume',actor='test')
        binding=db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding));binding.model_execution_mode='real';db.commit()
    assert read()==dict(poll=False,confirm=False,stage=False)
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_result_projection.py =====
"""Factory output must retain A, B score and authoritative final grade separately."""
from copy import deepcopy
import pytest
from app.workflow_node_executors import NodeExecutionContext, NodeExecutorError, execute_native_node


def project(*, b_status='succeeded', b_score=81, terminal=True, short_circuit=False, other_pending=False, skipped_nodes=None):
    node={'node_instance_id':'downstream','kind':'downstream_projection','config':{
        'connector_key':'logical.test.output','mapping':{'schema_version':'factory-result-v1',
        'prompt_a_node_id':'prompt_a','prompt_b_node_id':'prompt_b'}},'output_schema':{'type':'object'}}
    manifest={'schema_version':'workflow-v3','category_key':'model_3d_su',
        'nodes':[{'node_instance_id':'prompt_a','kind':'prompt','config':{}},
                 {'node_instance_id':'prompt_b','kind':'prompt','config':{}},
                 {'node_instance_id':'final_rule','kind':'rule_module','config':{}},node],
        'edges':[{'from':'prompt_a','to':'prompt_b'},{'from':'prompt_b','to':'final_rule'},
                 {'from':'final_rule','to':'downstream'}],
        'resource_policy':{'legacy_semantics':'documented_php_chain'}}
    if not terminal:manifest['edges'].append({'from':'downstream','to':'prompt_b'})
    # Legacy-compatible frozen result is deliberately distinct from B's foundation score.
    a={'score':99,'production_fields':{'title':'测试模型','tags':['木材']},
       'model_3d_su_fields':{'watermark':'','new_fixed_field':'保留'},'api_key':'not-a-real-key'}
    outputs={'prompt_a':{'node_kind':'prompt','status':'succeeded','output':deepcopy(a)},
        'prompt_b':{'node_kind':'prompt','status':b_status,'output':{'aesthetic_score':b_score}},
        'final_rule':{'node_kind':'rule_module','status':'succeeded','output':{'score':73,'level':'L4'}}}
    if other_pending:
        manifest['nodes'].append({'node_instance_id':'other','kind':'prompt','config':{}})
        outputs['other']={'status':'running'}
    if short_circuit:
        outputs['prompt_a']['short_circuit_decision']={
            'schema_version':'short-circuit-decision-v1','rule_key':'quality-l2',
            'target_level':'L2','rule_version':'short-circuit-rule-v1',
            'skipped_nodes':['prompt_b'] if skipped_nodes is None else skipped_nodes,'evidence':{'priority':90,'skip_roles':['grading'],
            'matched_rule_keys':['quality-l2'],'matched_conditions':[]}}
    result=execute_native_node(NodeExecutionContext(
        run_snapshot={'workflow':{'manifest':manifest},'runtime_context':{'model_execution_mode':'real'}},
        node=node,input_manifest={'upstream':outputs},raw_fixture_output={},attempt_no=1,started_at=None))
    return result['output']


def test_factory_projection_separates_b_score_from_final_and_keeps_a_fields():
    out=project()
    assert out['schema_version']=='factory-result-v1'
    assert out['prompt_a_fields']['production_fields']=={'title':'测试模型','tags':['木材']}
    assert out['prompt_a_fields']['model_3d_su_fields']['new_fixed_field']=='保留'
    assert 'api_key' not in out['prompt_a_fields']
    assert out['prompt_b_aesthetic_score']==81
    assert out['final_score']==73 and out['final_level']=='L4'
    assert out['external_io_performed'] is False


@pytest.mark.parametrize('score',[None,True,-1,101,'81'])
def test_factory_projection_rejects_missing_or_invalid_successful_b_score(score):
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError):project(b_score=score)


def test_factory_projection_rejects_unfinished_b():
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError):project(b_status='running')


def test_factory_projection_cannot_be_an_intermediate_write():
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError):project(terminal=False)


def test_factory_projection_waits_for_other_material_nodes():
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError):project(other_pending=True)


def test_factory_projection_does_not_invent_b_score_for_proven_short_circuit():
    out=project(b_status='skipped',short_circuit=True)
    assert out['prompt_b_status']=='skipped'
    assert out['prompt_b_aesthetic_score'] is None
    assert out['final_level']=='L2'
