    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError):project(b_status='skipped')


def test_factory_projection_requires_short_circuit_to_cover_missing_b():
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError):project(b_status='skipped',short_circuit=True,skipped_nodes=['final_rule'])

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_stream_loop.py =====
"""进料单次调用内循环：在时间预算内反复取料，没料/到上限/stop 立刻收手。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.factory_material_stream import MAX_PER_CYCLE, run_stream_loop


class FakeClock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.t = 100.0
        self.slept = []

    def now(self):
        return self.t

    def sleep(self, seconds):
        self.slept.append(seconds)
        self.t += seconds


def test_loop_keeps_cycling_while_material_arrives_until_budget_runs_out():
    clock = FakeClock()
    calls = []

    def cycle():
        calls.append(clock.t)
        clock.t += 3  # 每轮本身耗时 3 秒
        return {'listed': 1, 'confirmed': 1, 'material_enqueued': 1, 'image_rejected': 0, 'claim_invalid': 0, 'cycles': 1}

    out = run_stream_loop(cycle, loop_seconds=20, interval_seconds=2, clock=clock.now, sleep=clock.sleep)
    # 100→103, 105→108, 110→113, 115→118, 120→123：第五轮后 123+2 超过 120 的预算才停
    assert out['loops'] == len(calls) == 5
    assert out['material_enqueued'] == 5 and out['cycles'] == 5
    assert clock.slept == [2, 2, 2, 2]


def test_loop_stops_immediately_when_upstream_is_dry_or_paused():
    clock = FakeClock()
    seen = []

    def dry():
        seen.append(1)
        return {'listed': 0, 'image_rejected': 0, 'claim_invalid': 0, 'material_enqueued': 0}

    out = run_stream_loop(dry, loop_seconds=50, interval_seconds=2, clock=clock.now, sleep=clock.sleep)
    assert out['loops'] == 1 and out['material_enqueued'] == 0 and clock.slept == []

    def paused():
        return {'stop': 'pending_limit', 'material_enqueued': 0}

    out = run_stream_loop(paused, loop_seconds=50, interval_seconds=2, clock=clock.now, sleep=clock.sleep)
    assert out['stop'] == 'pending_limit' and out['loops'] == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('loop_seconds,interval', [(-1, 2), (56, 2), (10, 0), (10, 11), ('5', 2)])
def test_loop_bounds_are_validated(loop_seconds, interval):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='factory_stream_loop_invalid'):
        run_stream_loop(lambda: {}, loop_seconds=loop_seconds, interval_seconds=interval)


def test_per_cycle_ceiling_is_thirty():
    assert MAX_PER_CYCLE == 30

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_stream_pending_ceiling.py =====
"""In-flight materials must not be capped tighter than the slots they feed.

max_pending bounds how many claimed materials may be outstanding at once. It was
10 while the worker runs 96 execution slots, so the intake could never fill the
machine: with batch calls holding a slot for up to 600 seconds and two calls per
material, ten in flight caps a day at roughly 720 materials however much budget
is left. It is not a spend limit - the daily allowance still bounds what a day
can cost, and the platform's own admission still bounds what actually runs.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import inspect
import re
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from app import factory_material_stream
from app.factory_pump import pump_once

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value,accepted", [
    (1, True), (10, True), (48, True), (96, True), (100, True), (144, True), (256, True),
    (0, False), (-1, False), (257, False), (1000, False),
])
def test_the_stream_accepts_up_to_the_pump_ceiling(tmp_path, value, accepted):
    journal = tmp_path / "material.sqlite"
    journal.parent.chmod(0o700)

    def controls():  # pragma: no cover - never reached for a rejected value
        return {}

    if accepted:
        # A valid ceiling gets past validation and fails later, on the journal.
        with pytest.raises(Exception) as raised:
            factory_material_stream.stream_cycle(
                journal, sessions=None, controls=controls,
                daily_limit_micros=200_000_000, per_task_micros=90_760,
                max_pending=value,
            )
        assert "factory_stream_pending_invalid" not in str(raised.value)
    else:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="factory_stream_pending_invalid"):
            factory_material_stream.stream_cycle(
                journal, sessions=None, controls=controls,
                daily_limit_micros=200_000_000, per_task_micros=90_760,
                max_pending=value,
            )


def test_a_non_integer_ceiling_is_still_refused(tmp_path):
    journal = tmp_path / "material.sqlite"
    journal.parent.chmod(0o700)
    for value in (10.0, "10", True, None):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="factory_stream_pending_invalid"):
            factory_material_stream.stream_cycle(
                journal, sessions=None, controls=lambda: {},
                daily_limit_micros=200_000_000, per_task_micros=90_760,
                max_pending=value,
            )


def test_the_stream_ceiling_matches_the_pump_it_drives():
    # Two ceilings that disagree would silently clamp one of them.
    stream = inspect.getsource(factory_material_stream.stream_cycle)
    pump = inspect.getsource(pump_once)
    assert "1<=max_pending<=256" in stream.replace(" ", "")
    assert "1<=max_pending<=256" in pump.replace(" ", "")


def test_every_pump_call_in_the_stream_forwards_the_ceiling():
    # A literal here would pin the pump at a different number than the stream.
    tree = ast.parse(inspect.getsource(factory_material_stream))
    literals = []
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if not isinstance(node, ast.Call) or getattr(node.func, "id", "") != "pump_once":
            continue
        keywords = {kw.arg: kw for kw in node.keywords}
        assert "max_pending" in keywords, node.lineno
        value = keywords["max_pending"].value
        if not (isinstance(value, ast.Name) and value.id == "max_pending"):
            literals.append(node.lineno)
    assert not literals, "pump_once pinned to a literal ceiling at " + repr(literals)


def test_the_cli_allows_the_same_ceiling_and_keeps_the_money_cap():
    source = (ROOT / "scripts" / "factory-material-stream.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    guard = re.search(
        r"config\['daily_limit_micros'\]>(\S+) or config\['max_pending'\]>(\d+)", source
    )
    assert guard, "the intake must still guard both ceilings"
    # Raised on 2026-09-15 and again on 2026-09-16 by explicit operator decision;
    # it is still a real cash ceiling and must keep matching the ledger's own.
    # 数值会再变，两处必须一致这件事不会——那才是这条断言要守的。
    from app.factory_material_stream import MAX_DAILY_ALLOWANCE_MICROS
    assert guard.group(1) == "10_000_000_000"
    assert int(guard.group(1).replace("_", "")) == MAX_DAILY_ALLOWANCE_MICROS
    assert int(guard.group(2)) == 256

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_submit_contract.py =====
"""The submit wire boundary: exact form, honest body, strict envelope.

Real 2026-09-15 gray output shapes drive these cases: 250 successful materials
had every level in L1-L4, tags always >= 4, title/seo_title/design inside their
limits, cons over 50 characters in 222 of them, and no final score in the 45
that a frozen short circuit graded without aesthetic scoring.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import pytest

from app import factory_submit_contract as c
from app.factory_claim_contract import FactoryContractError


def _output(**overrides):
    base = {
        'schema_version': 'factory-result-v1',
        'factory_task': {'task_id': 255, 'res_type': 1, 'll_id': 33996578, 'img_id': 46254640, 'class_id_1': 4},
        'final_level': 'L4', 'final_score': 10,
        'prompt_a_fields': {
            'production_fields': {
                'title': '弧形布艺沙发', 'seotitle': '现代简约弧形布艺沙发3D模型',
                'category': '沙发', 'style': '现代', 'design': '弧形',
                'cons': '整体为孤立资产预览式展示，无真实使用场景；模型细节明显简化，部分表面仅为基础光滑曲面；材质表现普通，大面积同反射塑料感，微表面细节不足；光照平光，光影层次弱，仅达到常规可用，未见突出专业亮点。',
                'tags': ['沙发', '布艺', '弧形', '现代', '客厅'], 'score': 61, 'reason': [],
            },
            'model_3d_su_fields': {'brand_name': '', 'brand_trademark': '', 'chinese': '', 'watermark': ''},
        },
        'prompt_b_aesthetic_score': 53,
    }
    base.update(overrides)
    return base


def test_form_uses_the_documented_signature_and_compact_json():
    form = c.submit_form(timestamp=1700000000, body={'task_id': 7, 'result_status': 'success'})
    assert form['service'] == 'ResInternalLLM' and form['action'] == 'submit' and form['time'] == '1700000000'
    assert form['key'] == hashlib.md5('ResInternalLLMsubmit1700000000'[::-1].encode()).hexdigest()
    assert form['body'] == '{"task_id":7,"result_status":"success"}'
    for bad in ({'result_status': 'success'}, {'task_id': 0}, {'task_id': True}, 'x'):
        with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_body_invalid'):
            c.submit_form(timestamp=1700000000, body=bad)
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_auth_input_invalid'):
        c.submit_form(timestamp=0, body={'task_id': 7})


@pytest.mark.parametrize('level,grade', [('L1', 7), ('L2', 9), ('L3', 6), ('L4', 2)])
def test_grade_follows_the_operator_mapping(level, grade):
    body, _ = c.build_submit_body(_output(final_level=level))
    assert body['grade'] == grade
    assert c.GRADE_LABELS[grade] in {'好', '中等', '中差', '极差'}


def test_body_carries_every_required_field_from_the_frozen_output():
    body, notes = c.build_submit_body(_output())
    assert body['task_id'] == 255 and body['result_status'] == 'success'
    assert body['title'] == '弧形布艺沙发' and body['seo_title'] == '现代简约弧形布艺沙发3D模型'
    assert body['category'] == '沙发' and body['style'] == '现代' and body['design'] == '弧形'
    assert body['tags'] == ['沙发', '布艺', '弧形', '现代', '客厅']
    assert body['score'] == 10 and body['grade'] == 2
    assert body['reason'] == '' and body['hwreason'] == ''
    # The final score is the mechanism's, never prompt A's reference 61.
    assert 'score:prompt_a_reference' not in notes


def test_cons_is_truncated_to_the_api_limit_and_reported():
    body, notes = c.build_submit_body(_output())
    assert len(body['cons']) == 50 and body['cons'].startswith('整体为孤立资产预览式展示')
    assert any(n.startswith('cons:truncated:') for n in notes)
    # Fields already inside their limits are untouched and unreported.
    assert not any(n.startswith(('title:', 'seo_title:', 'design:')) for n in notes)


def test_short_circuit_without_final_score_uses_prompt_a_reference_and_says_so(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'SCORE_FALLBACK', 'prompt_a_reference')
    body, notes = c.build_submit_body(_output(final_level='L3', final_score=None, prompt_b_aesthetic_score=None))
    assert body['score'] == 61 and body['grade'] == 6
    assert 'score:prompt_a_reference' in notes


def test_short_circuit_without_final_score_fails_closed_under_that_policy(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'SCORE_FALLBACK', 'fail_closed')
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_score_missing'):
        c.build_submit_body(_output(final_score=None))


def test_no_score_anywhere_is_never_invented(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'SCORE_FALLBACK', 'prompt_a_reference')
    out = _output(final_score=None)
    out['prompt_a_fields']['production_fields']['score'] = None
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_score_missing'):
        c.build_submit_body(out)


# A non-string tag cannot reach here: the prompt output contract is a strict JSON
# schema of string items, and the legacy projection stringifies defensively.
@pytest.mark.parametrize('tags', [[], ['a', 'b', 'c'], ['a', 'b', 'c', ' ']])
def test_fewer_than_four_real_tags_is_refused(tags):
    out = _output()
    out['prompt_a_fields']['production_fields']['tags'] = tags
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_tags_insufficient'):
        c.build_submit_body(out)


def test_missing_identity_or_unmapped_level_is_refused():
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_task_identity_missing'):
        c.build_submit_body(_output(factory_task={}))
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_level_unmapped'):
        c.build_submit_body(_output(final_level='L5'))
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_output_schema_invalid'):
        c.build_submit_body({'schema_version': 'other'})


def test_hwreason_and_reason_come_from_the_legacy_projection():
    out = _output()
    out['prompt_a_fields']['model_3d_su_fields'].update(brand_trademark='有知名品牌logo/商标', watermark='有水印')
    body, _ = c.build_submit_body(out)
    assert body['hwreason'] == '有知名品牌logo/商标' and body['reason'] == '有水印'


def test_response_envelope_is_strict():
    ok = {'code': 1, 'msg': '请求成功', 'data': {'task_id': 255, 'status': 2, 'accepted': True}, 'request_id': 'req-1'}
    assert c.parse_submit_response(ok, task_id=255) == {'task_id': 255, 'accepted': True, 'request_id': 'req-1', 'status': 2}
    assert c.parse_submit_response(dict(ok, code=200), task_id=255)['accepted'] is True
    dedup = dict(ok, data={'task_id': 255, 'status': 2, 'accepted': False})
    assert c.parse_submit_response(dedup, task_id=255)['accepted'] is False
    # 工厂自己写的那句原因：只取 msg，清洗截断，供审计用
    assert c.submit_diagnostic(dict(ok, code=0, msg='任务已结束，不可提交')) == '任务已结束，不可提交'
    assert c.submit_diagnostic({'code': 0}) == ''          # 没有 msg
    assert c.submit_diagnostic({'msg': 123}) == ''         # msg 不是字符串
    assert c.submit_diagnostic('not a dict') == ''
    assert c.submit_diagnostic({'msg': '  换\n行\t与控制符  '}) == '换行与控制符'
    long_msg = c.submit_diagnostic({'msg': '很' * 400})
    assert len(long_msg) == c.MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_CHARS + 1 and long_msg.endswith('…')
    for bad, code in [
        (dict(ok, code=0), 'factory_submit_not_successful_code_0'),
        (dict(ok, code=-1), 'factory_submit_not_successful_code_-1'),
        # 数值长到放不进 String(80) 的错误码列，就退回不带码的形状
        (dict(ok, code=10 ** 9), 'factory_submit_not_successful$'),
        # 根本没有 code 或不是整数时，没有码可带
        (dict(ok, code='0'), 'factory_submit_not_successful$'),
        (dict(ok, request_id=''), 'factory_submit_envelope_invalid'),
        (dict(ok, data=[]), 'factory_submit_envelope_invalid'),
        (dict(ok, data={'task_id': 256, 'accepted': True}), 'factory_submit_task_mismatch'),
        (dict(ok, data={'task_id': 255, 'accepted': 'yes'}), 'factory_submit_envelope_invalid'),
        ('not json', 'factory_submit_not_successful'),
    ]:
        with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match=code):
            c.parse_submit_response(bad, task_id=255)


# --- who overwrote a task the server already accepted ---------------------------

def _human_override(**overrides):
    base = {'schema_version': 'incremental-level-override-v1', 'field': 'final_level',
            'previous_level': 'L3', 'level': 'L1', 'actor': 'ops', 'reason': '这张实际是好图',
            'correction_event_id': 'corr-1'}
    base.update(overrides)
    return base


def _change(trigger_kind='human_correction', **overrides):
    base = {'schema_version': 'downstream-change-record-v1', 'trigger_kind': trigger_kind,
            'actor': 'ops', 'reason': '这张实际是好图', 'correction_event_id': 'corr-1'}
    base.update(overrides)
    return base


def test_the_policy_is_on_and_off_keeps_the_wire_byte_identical(monkeypatch):
    # Names agreed with the downstream owner on 2026-09-18, so the default emits.
    assert c.OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY == 'emit'
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'off')
    body, notes = c.build_submit_body(_output(human_override=_human_override()),
                                      change=_change())
    assert set(body) == {'task_id', 'result_status', 'title', 'seo_title', 'category', 'style',
                         'cons', 'design', 'tags', 'score', 'grade', 'reason', 'hwreason'}
    assert not any(n.startswith('override:') for n in notes)


def test_a_level_correction_says_a_person_made_this_write(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'emit')
    body, notes = c.build_submit_body(_output(final_level='L1', human_override=_human_override()),
                                      change=_change())
    assert body['override_source'] == 'human_correction'
    assert body['override_actor'] == 'ops' and body['override_reason'] == '这张实际是好图'
    assert 'override:human_correction' in notes


def test_a_replay_after_a_correction_says_so_from_the_change_record_alone(monkeypatch):
    # The replay recomputes the mechanism's own level, so nothing is frozen into
    # the result; the change record is the only place the person is recorded.
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'emit')
    body, _ = c.build_submit_body(_output(), change=_change(reason='上游分类改过'))
    assert body['override_source'] == 'human_correction'
    assert body['override_actor'] == 'ops' and body['override_reason'] == '上游分类改过'


def test_an_automatic_run_carries_no_override_fields(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'emit')
    for change in (None, _change(trigger_kind='auto_run', actor='system', reason='')):
        body, notes = c.build_submit_body(_output(), change=change)
        assert not any(field in body for field in c.OVERRIDE_FIELDS.values())
        assert not any(n.startswith('override:') for n in notes)


def test_two_records_that_disagree_about_the_writer_fail_closed(monkeypatch):
    # Both are written by the same publish, so a mismatch is our own bug. A dead
    # letter is recoverable; telling the downstream a machine write was a
    # person's is not.
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'emit')
    with pytest.raises(FactoryContractError, match='factory_submit_override_source_conflict'):
        c.build_submit_body(_output(human_override=_human_override()),
                            change=_change(trigger_kind='auto_run'))


def test_a_foreign_override_block_is_not_trusted(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'emit')
    body, _ = c.build_submit_body(_output(human_override=_human_override(schema_version='other-v9')))
    assert 'override_source' not in body


def test_an_over_long_actor_or_reason_is_clipped_and_reported(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(c, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'emit')
    body, notes = c.build_submit_body(
        _output(human_override=_human_override(actor='人' * 200, reason='因' * 400)))
    assert len(body['override_actor']) == 120 and len(body['override_reason']) == 200
    assert any(n.startswith('override_actor:truncated:') for n in notes)
    assert any(n.startswith('override_reason:truncated:') for n in notes)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_submit_delivery.py =====
"""The HTTPS submit adapter behaves like any other downstream adapter.

It slots into resolve_configured_downstream_adapter, so the outbox tick,
delivery loop, publish ledger, pauses and dead letters need no changes. What is
tested here is the adapter's own contract: allowlisted endpoint from the
deployment environment, retryable only for transport failures, read-back from
the server's own acceptance, and the end-to-end delivery loop completing an
outbox event through it without any network.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import json
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_delivery import (
    ConnectorDeliveryError, DeliveryBatch, DeliveryItem, DeliveryKey, deliver_outbox_batch,
    resolve_configured_downstream_adapter,
)
from app.database import Base
from app.factory_submit_delivery import (
    FACTORY_SUBMIT_ADAPTER_KEY, FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT, FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter,
    resolve_factory_submit_adapter, resolve_locator_endpoint,
)
from app.factory_test_claim import PRODUCTION_URL, TEST_URL
from app.models import RuntimeOutboxEvent
from app.runtime_outbox import RuntimeOutboxRequest, enqueue_runtime_outbox
from test_connector_delivery import _mechanism
from app import factory_submit_contract as submit_contract
from test_factory_submit_contract import _output

LOCATOR = 'logical.factory.prod.submit'


@contextlib.contextmanager
def _raises(code):
    # ConnectorDeliveryError's str() is the operator message; the platform keys off .code.
    with pytest.raises(ConnectorDeliveryError) as info:
        yield
    assert info.value.code == code, info.value.code
ENV = 'LABEL_SYSTEM_ENDPOINT_LOGICAL_FACTORY_PROD_SUBMIT'


def _payload(task_id=255, change=None, **overrides):
    out = _output(**overrides)
    out['factory_task']['task_id'] = task_id
    # ``result`` is the whole workflow-node-result-v1, the automatic run and the
    # human correction alike; the adapter reads the projection out of it.
    payload = {'schema_version': 'runtime-outbox-payload-v1',
               'result': {'schema_version': 'workflow-node-result-v1',
                          'node_instance_id': 'downstream', 'status': 'succeeded', 'output': out}}
    if change is not None:
        payload['change'] = change
    return payload


def _key(task_id, payload_hash='1' * 64):
    return DeliveryKey(business_idempotency_key=f'model_3d_su:prod-task:{task_id}:node:downstream',
                       mechanism_version_id=296, payload_hash=payload_hash)


def _ok(task_id, request_id='req-1', accepted=True):
    return json.dumps({'code': 1, 'msg': '请求成功', 'request_id': request_id,
                       'data': {'task_id': task_id, 'status': 2, 'accepted': accepted}}).encode()


class _Send:
    def __init__(self, responder):
        self.calls = []
        self._responder = responder

    def __call__(self, url, fields):
        self.calls.append((url, fields))
        return self._responder(url, fields)


# --- endpoint resolution -----------------------------------------------------

def test_endpoint_comes_from_the_environment_and_must_be_allowlisted(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv(ENV, raising=False)
    with _raises('downstream_adapter_unavailable'):
        resolve_locator_endpoint(LOCATOR)
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENV, 'https://userapi.3d66.com.evil.example/api/factory')
    with _raises('downstream_adapter_unavailable'):
        resolve_locator_endpoint(LOCATOR)
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENV, PRODUCTION_URL)
    assert resolve_locator_endpoint(LOCATOR) == PRODUCTION_URL
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENV, TEST_URL)
    assert resolve_locator_endpoint(LOCATOR) == TEST_URL
    with pytest.raises(ConnectorDeliveryError):
        resolve_locator_endpoint('https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory')  # a DSN-like value is not a locator


def test_the_adapter_itself_refuses_a_non_allowlisted_url():
    with pytest.raises(ConnectorDeliveryError):
        FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter('https://example.invalid/api/factory', send=lambda *a: b'')


# --- adapter behaviour ---------------------------------------------------------

def test_verify_target_reports_the_frozen_fingerprint_and_the_host():
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=lambda *a: b'')
    evidence = adapter.verify_target()
    assert evidence.least_privileged is True
    assert evidence.schema_fingerprint == FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT
    assert PRODUCTION_URL in evidence.verifier


def test_deliver_posts_the_signed_form_and_read_back_matches_the_acceptance():
    send = _Send(lambda url, f: _ok(json.loads(f['body'])['task_id'], request_id='req-9'))
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=send, clock=lambda: 1700000000.9)
    key = _key(255)
    attempt = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('factory.prod.downstream', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, key, _payload(255)),)))
    (result,) = attempt.results
    assert result.succeeded and result.key == key
    assert result.external_record_key == 'factory-submit:task:255:req:req-9:accepted:true'
    url, fields = send.calls[0]
    assert url == PRODUCTION_URL
    assert fields['service'] == 'ResInternalLLM' and fields['action'] == 'submit' and fields['time'] == '1700000000'
    body = json.loads(fields['body'])
    assert body['task_id'] == 255 and body['grade'] == 2 and body['result_status'] == 'success'
    (row,) = adapter.read_back([key])
    assert row.key == key and row.external_record_key == result.external_record_key
    # A different payload hash for the same business key is not the row we wrote.
    assert adapter.read_back([_key(255, payload_hash='2' * 64)]) == ()
    assert adapter.notes[key.business_idempotency_key]  # cons truncation recorded


def test_server_side_dedupe_is_still_delivered():
    send = _Send(lambda url, f: _ok(255, accepted=False))
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=send)
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, _key(255), _payload(255)),))).results
    assert result.succeeded and result.external_record_key.endswith(':accepted:false')


@pytest.mark.parametrize('error', [URLError('timed out'), TimeoutError(), ConnectionResetError()])
def test_transport_failures_are_retryable_and_leave_no_row(error):
    def boom(url, f):
        raise error
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=boom)
    key = _key(255)
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, key, _payload(255)),))).results
    assert not result.succeeded and result.retryable and result.error_code == 'factory_submit_transport_failed'
    assert adapter.read_back([key]) == ()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('status,retryable', [
    # Task 258 on 2026-09-15: a deterministic 404 retried five times in
    # thirty-one seconds, exhausting the budget and dead-lettering the material.
    (404, False), (400, False), (403, False), (422, False),
    (408, True), (429, True), (500, True), (502, True), (503, True),
])
def test_a_status_the_server_chose_is_classified_by_that_status(status, retryable):
    def boom(url, f):
        raise HTTPError(PRODUCTION_URL, status, 'x', {}, None)
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=boom)
    key = _key(255)
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, key, _payload(255)),))).results
    assert not result.succeeded and result.retryable is retryable
    assert result.error_code == 'factory_submit_http_%d' % status
    assert adapter.notes[key.business_idempotency_key] == ['http:%d' % status]
    assert adapter.read_back([key]) == ()


def test_a_dead_letter_records_which_status_caused_it(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "g.db"}'); Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENV, PRODUCTION_URL)

    def not_found(url, f):
        raise HTTPError(PRODUCTION_URL, 404, 'Not Found', {}, None)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism(db)
        target = _submit_contract(db)
        event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, RuntimeOutboxRequest(
            event_key='delivery:prod-task:258', target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
            target_contract_version_id=target.id,
            business_idempotency_key='model_3d_su:prod-task:258:node:downstream',
            mechanism_version_id=version.id, payload=_payload(258), max_attempts=5))
        db.commit()
        result = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id='auxiliary:test',
                                      adapter=resolve_factory_submit_adapter(target, send=not_found))
        db.commit()
        # One attempt, not five: the status is not retried away into a dead letter.
        assert result.blocked_event_ids == (event.id,) and not result.retry_event_ids
        stored = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id)
        assert stored.status == 'dead_lettered' and stored.attempt_count == 1
        assert stored.last_error_code == 'factory_submit_http_404'
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('raw,code', [
    (b'not json', 'factory_submit_response_invalid'),
    # 工厂的业务码要一路带到台账与审计：这是拒绝原因唯一的按条通道
    (json.dumps({'code': 0, 'request_id': 'r', 'data': {}}).encode(), 'factory_submit_not_successful_code_0'),
    (json.dumps({'code': 4003, 'request_id': 'r', 'data': {}}).encode(), 'factory_submit_not_successful_code_4003'),
    (json.dumps({'code': 1, 'request_id': 'r', 'data': {'task_id': 999, 'accepted': True}}).encode(),
     'factory_submit_task_mismatch'),
    (b'x' * (1024 * 1024 + 1), 'factory_submit_response_limit'),
])
def test_a_response_the_server_did_send_but_is_invalid_is_not_retried(raw, code):
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=lambda *a: raw)
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, _key(255), _payload(255)),))).results
    assert not result.succeeded and not result.retryable and result.error_code == code


def test_a_rejection_carries_the_factory_own_reason_for_the_audit_trail():
    """2026-09-20：工厂连拒几十条，台账只有「未通过」，连它写的原因都没留，只能靠猜。"""
    raw = json.dumps({'code': 0, 'msg': '任务已结束，不可提交', 'request_id': 'r', 'data': {}}).encode()
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=lambda *a: raw)
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, _key(255), _payload(255)),))).results
    assert not result.succeeded
    assert result.error_code == 'factory_submit_not_successful_code_0'
    assert result.diagnostic == '任务已结束，不可提交'


def test_a_successful_delivery_carries_no_upstream_text():
    """成功路径不需要诊断文本，别把上游的话平白带进台账。"""
    raw = json.dumps({'code': 1, 'msg': '请求成功', 'request_id': 'r',
                      'data': {'task_id': 255, 'accepted': True, 'status': 2}}).encode()
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=lambda *a: raw)
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, _key(255), _payload(255)),))).results
    assert result.succeeded and result.diagnostic == ''


def test_a_body_that_cannot_be_built_never_reaches_the_network():
    send = _Send(lambda *a: b'')
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=send)
    payload = _payload(255)
    payload['result']['output']['prompt_a_fields']['production_fields']['tags'] = ['one']
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, _key(255), payload),))).results
    assert not result.succeeded and not result.retryable
    assert result.error_code == 'factory_submit_tags_insufficient'
    assert send.calls == []


def test_each_item_in_a_batch_is_judged_on_its_own():
    def responder(url, f):
        task = json.loads(f['body'])['task_id']
        if task == 2:
            raise URLError('flaky')
        return _ok(task)
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=_Send(responder))
    items = tuple(DeliveryItem(i, _key(i), _payload(i)) for i in (1, 2, 3))
    results = {r.key.business_idempotency_key: r for r in adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, items)).results}
    assert results[_key(1).business_idempotency_key].succeeded
    assert not results[_key(2).business_idempotency_key].succeeded and results[_key(2).business_idempotency_key].retryable
    assert results[_key(3).business_idempotency_key].succeeded
    assert {r.key.business_idempotency_key for r in adapter.read_back([_key(1), _key(2), _key(3)])} == {
        _key(1).business_idempotency_key, _key(3).business_idempotency_key}


# --- dispatch and end-to-end ---------------------------------------------------

def _submit_contract(db, *, fingerprint=FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT, adapter_key=FACTORY_SUBMIT_ADAPTER_KEY):
    return create_connector_contract_version(
        db, contract_key='factory.prod.downstream', direction='downstream', category_key='model_3d_su',
        logical_locator=LOCATOR, adapter_key=adapter_key, schema_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        field_mapping={'schema_version': 'factory-submit-body-v1'}, cursor_contract={},
        idempotency_contract={'key_fields': ['content_id', 'mechanism_version'], 'scope': 'contract'},
        batch_size=1, timeout_seconds=30, owner='ops', status='active', created_by='test')


def test_resolve_configured_downstream_adapter_dispatches_to_the_submit_adapter(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "d.db"}'); Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENV, PRODUCTION_URL)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        target = _submit_contract(db); db.commit()
        adapter = resolve_configured_downstream_adapter(target)
        assert isinstance(adapter, FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter) and adapter.url == PRODUCTION_URL
        drifted = _submit_contract(db, fingerprint='e' * 64); db.commit()
        with _raises('downstream_schema_drift'):
            resolve_factory_submit_adapter(drifted)
    monkeypatch.delenv(ENV)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        target = db.scalar(select(type(target)).where(type(target).schema_fingerprint == FACTORY_SUBMIT_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT))
        with _raises('downstream_adapter_unavailable'):
            resolve_configured_downstream_adapter(target)
    engine.dispose()


def test_the_delivery_loop_completes_an_outbox_event_through_the_submit_adapter(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "e.db"}'); Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENV, PRODUCTION_URL)
    send = _Send(lambda url, f: _ok(json.loads(f['body'])['task_id'], request_id='req-e2e'))
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism(db)
        target = _submit_contract(db)
        event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, RuntimeOutboxRequest(
            event_key='delivery:prod-task:255', target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
            target_contract_version_id=target.id,
            business_idempotency_key='model_3d_su:prod-task:255:node:downstream',
            mechanism_version_id=version.id, payload=_payload(255), max_attempts=3))
        db.commit()
        adapter = resolve_factory_submit_adapter(target, send=send)
        result = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id='auxiliary:test', adapter=adapter)
        db.commit()
        assert result.delivered_event_ids == (event.id,) and not result.retry_event_ids and not result.blocked_event_ids
        stored = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id)
        assert stored.status == 'delivered'
        receipt = json.loads(stored.delivery_receipt_json)
        assert receipt['external_record_key'] == 'factory-submit:task:255:req:req-e2e:accepted:true'
        assert len(send.calls) == 1 and send.calls[0][0] == PRODUCTION_URL
    engine.dispose()


def test_a_transport_failure_in_the_loop_requeues_instead_of_dead_lettering(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "f.db"}'); Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    monkeypatch.setenv(ENV, PRODUCTION_URL)

    def flaky(url, f):
        raise URLError('down')
    with Session(engine) as db:
        version = _mechanism(db)
        target = _submit_contract(db)
        event = enqueue_runtime_outbox(db, RuntimeOutboxRequest(
            event_key='delivery:prod-task:256', target_contract_key=target.contract_key,
            target_contract_version_id=target.id,
            business_idempotency_key='model_3d_su:prod-task:256:node:downstream',
            mechanism_version_id=version.id, payload=_payload(256), max_attempts=3))
        db.commit()
        result = deliver_outbox_batch(db, target, worker_id='auxiliary:test',
                                      adapter=resolve_factory_submit_adapter(target, send=flaky))
        db.commit()
        assert result.retry_event_ids == (event.id,)
        stored = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, event.id)
        assert stored.status == 'queued' and stored.last_error_code == 'factory_submit_transport_failed'
    engine.dispose()


# --- who overwrote a task the server already accepted ---------------------------

def test_the_change_record_reaches_the_body_so_a_correction_is_labelled(monkeypatch):
    # The downstream only honours a second write a person made. The evidence for
    # that lives in the event's change record, one level above the projection,
    # so an adapter that reads only ``result`` would silently downgrade every
    # human correction to look like the mechanism's own rewrite.
    monkeypatch.setattr(submit_contract, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'emit')
    send = _Send(lambda url, f: _ok(json.loads(f['body'])['task_id']))
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=send)
    payload = _payload(255, change={'schema_version': 'downstream-change-record-v1',
                                    'trigger_kind': 'human_correction', 'actor': 'ops',
                                    'reason': '这张实际是好图'})
    payload['result']['output']['human_override'] = {
        'schema_version': 'incremental-level-override-v1', 'field': 'final_level',
        'previous_level': 'L3', 'level': 'L4', 'actor': 'ops', 'reason': '这张实际是好图'}
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, _key(255), payload),))).results
    assert result.succeeded
    body = json.loads(send.calls[0][1]['body'])
    assert body['override_source'] == 'human_correction' and body['override_actor'] == 'ops'


def test_an_automatic_publish_is_never_labelled_as_a_person(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(submit_contract, 'OVERRIDE_SOURCE_POLICY', 'emit')
    send = _Send(lambda url, f: _ok(json.loads(f['body'])['task_id']))
    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=send)
    payload = _payload(255, change={'schema_version': 'downstream-change-record-v1',
                                    'trigger_kind': 'auto_run', 'actor': 'system', 'reason': ''})
    (result,) = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch('k', 1, (DeliveryItem(1, _key(255), payload),))).results
    assert result.succeeded
    assert 'override_source' not in json.loads(send.calls[0][1]['body'])


def test_twenty_parallel_submissions_preserve_individual_receipts():
    from threading import Barrier, Lock
    barrier = Barrier(20)
    lock = Lock()
    active = peak = 0

    def send(_url, fields):
        nonlocal active, peak
        task_id = json.loads(fields['body'])['task_id']
        with lock:
            active += 1
            peak = max(peak, active)
        try:
            barrier.wait(timeout=5)
            if task_id == 7:
                raise RuntimeError('one request failed')
            return _ok(task_id, request_id=f'req-{task_id}')
        finally:
            with lock:
                active -= 1

    adapter = FactorySubmitDownstreamAdapter(PRODUCTION_URL, send=send)
    items = tuple(DeliveryItem(event_id=i, key=_key(i), payload=_payload(i)) for i in range(1, 21))
    result = adapter.deliver(DeliveryBatch(target_contract_key='factory.prod.downstream',
                                         contract_version=1, items=items))
    assert peak == 20
    assert sum(r.succeeded for r in result.results) == 19
    assert [r.key for r in result.results] == [i.key for i in items]
    assert not result.results[6].succeeded
    assert len(adapter.read_back([i.key for i in items])) == 19

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_test_claim.py =====
"""A failed/ambiguous POST must never be retried or lose its durable intent."""
import importlib
import importlib.util
import json
import sqlite3
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

import pytest


def client():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec('app.factory_test_claim'), 'missing durable test-only claim client'
    return importlib.import_module('app.factory_test_claim')


def response():
    return json.dumps({'code':1,'request_id':'test-one','data':[
        {'task_id':10,'res_type':1,'ll_id':20,'img_id':30,'class_id_1':4,
         'image_url':'https://example.com/a.jpg'}]}).encode()


def test_disabled_or_unlisted_target_never_sends(tmp_path):
    # Production became an allowlisted target for the 2026-09-15 gray phase, so
    # it is no longer refused here. Being disabled still refuses, and so does
    # any target outside the allowlist; neither may reach the network.
    c=client(); calls=[]
    send=lambda *a: calls.append(a)
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError): c.claim_once(tmp_path/'a.db',enabled=False,send=send)
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError):
        c.claim_once(tmp_path/'a.db',enabled=False,url=c.PRODUCTION_URL,send=send)
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError):
        c.claim_once(tmp_path/'a.db',enabled=True,url='https://example.invalid/api/factory',send=send)
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError):
        c.claim_once(tmp_path/'a.db',enabled=True,url=c.PRODUCTION_URL+'/',send=send)
    assert calls==[]


def test_production_target_is_reachable_but_labels_content_as_production(tmp_path):
    c=client(); path=tmp_path/'p.db'
    def send(url,fields):
        assert url=='https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory'
        return response()
    receipt=c.claim_once(path,enabled=True,url=c.PRODUCTION_URL,send=send)
    assert receipt['state']=='received' and receipt['tasks']==1
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        content_id,=db.execute('select content_id from tasks').fetchone()
    assert content_id=='factory-prod:1:20:30'  


def test_intent_is_committed_before_post_and_identifiers_are_persisted(tmp_path):
    c=client(); path=tmp_path/'a.db'
    def send(url,fields):
        with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
            assert db.execute('select state from attempts').fetchone()==('sending',)
        assert url=='https://userapitest.3d66.com/api/factory'
        assert fields['service']=='ResInternalLLM' and fields['body']=='{"limit":1}'
        return response()
    receipt=c.claim_once(path,enabled=True,send=send)
    assert receipt['state']=='received' and receipt['tasks']==1
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        row=db.execute('select task_id,resource_kind,content_id from tasks').fetchone()
        assert row==(10,'3d','factory-test:1:20:30')
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError): c.claim_once(path,enabled=True,send=send)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('fault',['timeout','bad_json','wrong_code','oversize','crash'])
def test_unknown_outcomes_survive_restart_and_block_another_claim(tmp_path,fault):
    c=client(); path=tmp_path/'a.db'; calls=[]
    def send(*a):
        calls.append(1)
        if fault=='timeout': raise TimeoutError('must not leak')
        if fault=='crash': raise KeyboardInterrupt()
        return {'bad_json':b'not json','wrong_code':b'{"code":200}',
                'oversize':b'x'*(c.MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES+1)}[fault]
    with pytest.raises((c.ClaimGateError,KeyboardInterrupt)):
        c.claim_once(path,enabled=True,send=send)
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError): c.claim_once(path,enabled=True,send=send)
    assert calls==[1]
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        assert db.execute('select count(*) from attempts').fetchone()[0]==1


def test_concurrent_invocations_do_not_double_claim(tmp_path):
    c=client(); path=tmp_path/'a.db';calls=[]
    def run(_):
        try: return c.claim_once(path,enabled=True,send=lambda *a:(calls.append(1) or response()))
        except c.ClaimGateError:return None
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as p: results=list(p.map(run,range(2)))
    assert len(calls)==1 and sum(r is not None for r in results)==1


def test_su_mapping_is_separate_from_3d_and_unknown_mapping_rejected():
    c=client()
    assert c.resource_identity(6,20,30)==('su','factory-test:6:20:30')
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError): c.resource_identity(9,20,30)


def test_unknown_error_records_only_safe_classification(tmp_path):
    c=client();path=tmp_path/'a.db'
    def fail(*a): raise TimeoutError('sensitive-token-do-not-save')
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError):c.claim_once(path,enabled=True,send=fail)
    with sqlite3.connect(path) as db:
        error=db.execute('select error_code from attempts').fetchone()[0]
    assert error=='transport:TimeoutError'
    assert b'sensitive-token-do-not-save' not in path.read_bytes()


def test_tls_context_rejects_untrusted_certificates():
    import ssl
    c=client()
    assert hasattr(c,'verified_tls_context'), 'missing explicit verified trust store'
    context=c.verified_tls_context()
    assert context.verify_mode==ssl.CERT_REQUIRED and context.check_hostname


def test_staging_pause_resume_and_replay_preserve_one_source_event(tmp_path):
    c=client(); journal=tmp_path/'claim.db'; target=tmp_path/'source.db'
    c.claim_once(journal,enabled=True,send=lambda *a:response())
    assert hasattr(c,'stage_received'), 'missing durable received-to-source bridge'
    assert c.stage_received(journal,target,local_asset_id=7,enabled=False)['staged']==0
    assert not target.exists()
    assert c.stage_received(journal,target,local_asset_id=7,enabled=True)['staged']==1
    assert c.stage_received(journal,target,local_asset_id=7,enabled=True)['staged']==0
    with sqlite3.connect(target) as db:
        row=db.execute('select content_id,source_version,category_key,asset_id from changes').fetchone()
        assert row==('factory-test:1:20:30','test-task:10','model_3d_su',7)
        assert db.execute('select count(*) from changes').fetchone()[0]==1
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError):c.stage_received(journal,target,local_asset_id=8,enabled=True)


def test_unknown_claim_cannot_be_dispatched_to_simulation(tmp_path):
    c=client(); journal=tmp_path/'claim.db';target=tmp_path/'source.db'
    def timeout(*a):raise TimeoutError()
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError):c.claim_once(journal,enabled=True,send=timeout)
    assert hasattr(c,'stage_received'), 'missing durable received-to-source bridge'
    with pytest.raises(c.ClaimGateError):c.stage_received(journal,target,local_asset_id=7,enabled=True)
    assert not target.exists()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_factory_upstream_targets.py =====
"""The two upstreams number tasks independently, so their keys must not collide.

Production and test both hand out task ids starting from small integers. If they
shared a content id prefix, a version prefix or a submission id prefix, then a
production task would either be deduped away as an already seen test task, or
would land on top of one downstream. Every prefix is therefore paired with the
target, and an unknown target is refused outright rather than defaulted to test.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.factory_test_claim import (
    PRODUCTION_URL,
    TEST_URL,
    UPSTREAMS,
    ClaimGateError,
    resource_identity,
    upstream_prefixes,
)
from app.incremental_input import IncrementalInputError, validate_factory_task_identity


def test_only_the_two_known_targets_are_allowed():
    assert set(UPSTREAMS) == {TEST_URL, PRODUCTION_URL}
    assert TEST_URL == "https://userapitest.3d66.com/api/factory"
    assert PRODUCTION_URL == "https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("target", [
    "https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory/",          # trailing slash
    "https://userapi.3d66.com/api/Factory",           # case
    "http://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory",            # plaintext
    "https://userapi.3d66.com.evil.example/api/factory",
    "https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory?x=1",
    "",
    None,
    b"https://userapi.3d66.com/api/factory",
])
def test_anything_outside_the_allowlist_is_refused(target):
    with pytest.raises(ClaimGateError, match="factory_upstream_target_not_allowed"):
        upstream_prefixes(target)
    with pytest.raises(ClaimGateError, match="factory_upstream_target_not_allowed"):
        resource_identity(1, 152548309, 46224623, url=target)


def test_every_prefix_differs_between_the_two_targets():
    test, production = UPSTREAMS[TEST_URL], UPSTREAMS[PRODUCTION_URL]
    assert test.identity != production.identity
    assert test.version != production.version
    assert test.submission != production.submission
    # The bare 'task:' namespace is retired: production tasks 1-5 landed in it
    # through a forwarding bug, so a later test task 1-5 would have been deduped
    # away. Neither target may use it again.
    assert test.submission != "task:"
    assert production.submission != "task:"


def test_the_same_task_id_yields_different_identities_per_target():
    _, from_test = resource_identity(1, 152548309, 46224623, url=TEST_URL)
    _, from_production = resource_identity(1, 152548309, 46224623, url=PRODUCTION_URL)
    assert from_test != from_production
    assert from_test.startswith("factory-test:")
    assert from_production.startswith("factory-prod:")
    # The resource kind is a property of the resource, not of the environment.
    assert resource_identity(6, 1, 2, url=TEST_URL)[0] == resource_identity(
        6, 1, 2, url=PRODUCTION_URL)[0] == "su"


def test_default_target_stays_test_so_existing_callers_do_not_move():
    assert resource_identity(1, 3, 4) == resource_identity(1, 3, 4, url=TEST_URL)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("url", [TEST_URL, PRODUCTION_URL])
def test_identity_validator_accepts_a_consistently_labelled_task(url):
    prefixes = UPSTREAMS[url]
    task = {"task_id": 8, "res_type": 1, "ll_id": 152548309,
            "img_id": 46224623, "class_id_1": 4}
    frozen = {
        "source_content_id": f"{prefixes.identity}:1:152548309:46224623:task:8",
        "source_version": prefixes.version + "8",
    }
    assert validate_factory_task_identity(frozen, task) == task


def test_identity_validator_rejects_a_production_id_with_a_test_version():
    task = {"task_id": 8, "res_type": 1, "ll_id": 152548309,
            "img_id": 46224623, "class_id_1": 4}
    frozen = {
        "source_content_id": "factory-prod:1:152548309:46224623:task:8",
        "source_version": "test-task:8",
    }
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError):
        validate_factory_task_identity(frozen, task)


def test_identity_validator_rejects_a_test_id_with_a_production_version():
    task = {"task_id": 8, "res_type": 1, "ll_id": 152548309,
            "img_id": 46224623, "class_id_1": 4}
    frozen = {
        "source_content_id": "factory-test:1:152548309:46224623:task:8",
        "source_version": "prod-task:8",
    }
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError):
        validate_factory_task_identity(frozen, task)


def test_identity_validator_still_rejects_an_unknown_prefix():
    task = {"task_id": 8, "res_type": 1, "ll_id": 152548309,
            "img_id": 46224623, "class_id_1": 4}
    frozen = {
        "source_content_id": "factory-staging:1:152548309:46224623:task:8",
        "source_version": "staging-task:8",
    }
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError):
        validate_factory_task_identity(frozen, task)


def test_identity_validator_still_rejects_a_mismatched_task_body():
    # Same shape as a production task, but the body disagrees with the id.
    task = {"task_id": 8, "res_type": 1, "ll_id": 152548309,
            "img_id": 46224623, "class_id_1": 4}
    frozen = {
        "source_content_id": "factory-prod:1:152548309:46224624:task:8",
        "source_version": "prod-task:8",
    }
    with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError):
        validate_factory_task_identity(frozen, task)


def test_send_refuses_an_unlisted_target_before_opening_a_connection():
    from app.factory_test_claim import send_upstream_request
    with pytest.raises(ClaimGateError, match="factory_upstream_target_not_allowed"):
        send_upstream_request("https://example.invalid/api/factory", {"service": "x"})


def test_the_deprecated_alias_still_points_at_the_same_function():
    from app.factory_test_claim import send_test_request, send_upstream_request
    assert send_test_request is send_upstream_request


def test_every_downstream_call_in_the_stream_carries_the_target():
    # 2026-09-15: the second enqueue call site was missing upstream=, so five
    # real production tasks were submitted under the test prefixes before the
    # colliding task id raised and the recovery path used the right one.
    import ast
    import inspect
    from app import factory_material_stream

    tree = ast.parse(inspect.getsource(factory_material_stream))
    forwarded = {'enqueue_confirmed_materials', 'pump_once', 'resource_identity',
                 '_settle_allowance'}
    missing = []
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if not isinstance(node, ast.Call) or not isinstance(node.func, ast.Name):
            continue
        if node.func.id not in forwarded:
            continue
        keywords = {kw.arg for kw in node.keywords}
        if not keywords & {'upstream', 'url', 'submission_prefix'}:
            missing.append((node.func.id, node.lineno))
    assert not missing, 'call sites default to the test target: ' + repr(missing)


def test_no_target_may_reuse_the_polluted_bare_task_namespace():
    # 2026-09-15: real production tasks 1-5 were written as task:1..task:5 in
    # incremental_material_jobs. Those rows are kept as-is, so any target that
    # went back to the bare prefix would silently dedupe a test task 1-5 away.
    assert all(prefixes.submission != 'task:' for prefixes in UPSTREAMS.values())
    submissions = [prefixes.submission for prefixes in UPSTREAMS.values()]
    assert len(set(submissions)) == len(submissions), 'submission namespaces overlap'


def test_no_two_targets_share_any_prefix():
    for field in ('identity', 'version', 'submission'):
        values = [getattr(prefixes, field) for prefixes in UPSTREAMS.values()]
        assert len(set(values)) == len(values), field + ' prefixes overlap'
        # A prefix that is a prefix of another still lets keys collide.
        for one in values:
            for other in values:
                if one is not other:
                    assert not one.startswith(other), (one, other)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_failure_console.py =====
"""失败处理：跑挂的素材由人给结论，投得出去，且不伪装成模型事实。

老大 2026-09-20 在两个方案里选了「单独的人工结论记录」：节点输出是证据，人填的东西
不写进去。所以这里盯三件事——

1. 人工结论真的能投出去：组出来的 payload 过得了工厂提交体的合同（十三字段、标签下限、
   等级要有 grade 映射）；
2. 它自证是人填的：``prompt_a_status='manual'`` 与 ``human_override`` 都在，DAG 上那条
   run 仍然是失败，没有被改成成功；
3. 不合格的输入当场拒绝，而不是悄悄截断后投一个残缺的东西出去。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import json
import os
import pathlib
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import func, select

import app

from app.failure_console import (
    FailureConsoleError,
    list_failures,
    resolution_form,
    resolve_failure,
    submittable_levels,
)
from app.failure_resolution_models import ManualFailureResolution
from app.factory_submit_contract import build_submit_body
from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeOutboxEvent

from test_incremental_correction_queue import (  # noqa: F401 - 复用同一套夹具
    db,
    _material,
)

GOOD_FIELDS = {
    "title": "组合圆形茶几",
    "seotitle": "现代风大小组合圆形边桌茶几家具3D模型素材",
    "category": "家具模型",
    "style": "现代简约",
    "cons": "模型细节明显简化，材质表现普通",
    "design": "大小圆叠放组合，木纹与深色撞色",
    "tags": ["现代简约", "家具茶几", "木纹台面", "深灰配色"],
}


def _failed_material(db, index: int = 1, *, stage_a_succeeded: bool = False):
    """把一条素材做成「跑挂了」：run 置 failed，并留下失败的步骤证据。"""
    result, run = _material(db, index, published=False)
    run.status = "failed"
    # 任务身份是进料时钉进冻结输入的，跑挂了也还在——人工结论就靠它投递。
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]["factory_task"] = {
        "task_id": 7000 + index, "res_type": 1, "class_id_1": 2124,
        "img_id": 46000000 + index, "ll_id": 34000000 + index,
    }
    run.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
    run.upstream_task_id = 7000 + index
    run.upstream_img_id = 46000000 + index
    run.upstream_ll_id = 34000000 + index
    run.upstream_class_id = 2124
    db.delete(result)
    db.add(ProductionStepAttempt(
        run_id=run.id, step_key="prompt_a", step_type="model_call", sequence=0, attempt_no=1,
        script_version_id=1, idempotency_key=f"fail-a-{index}", input_manifest_json="{}",
        input_hash="a" * 64, status="succeeded" if stage_a_succeeded else "failed",
        last_error_code="" if stage_a_succeeded else "model_transport_attempts_exhausted",
        last_error_message="" if stage_a_succeeded else "批量及在线兜底尝试已耗尽",
        output_manifest_json=json.dumps({"output": {
            "production_fields": dict(GOOD_FIELDS, tags=list(GOOD_FIELDS["tags"])),
            "factory_task": {"task_id": 7000 + index, "res_type": 1, "class_id_1": 2124,
                             "img_id": 46000000 + index, "ll_id": 34000000 + index},
        }}) if stage_a_succeeded else "{}",
    ))
    db.commit()
    return run


def test_a_material_without_a_task_number_is_refused_before_the_operator_types(db):
    """没有工厂任务号就投不出去。当场拒，别让人填完整张表单再被投递器判错。"""
    run = _failed_material(db, 20)
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    del snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]["factory_task"]
    run.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError) as info:
        resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                        reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-20")
    assert info.value.code == "failure_resolution_task_identity_missing"


def test_the_list_shows_one_row_per_failed_material_with_what_it_needs(db):
    run = _failed_material(db, 1)
    page = list_failures(db)
    assert page["total"] == 1
    row = page["items"][0]
    assert row["production_run_id"] == run.id and row["status"] == "failed"
    assert row["failed_step"] == "prompt_a"
    assert row["error_code"] == "model_transport_attempts_exhausted"
    assert row["upstream"]["task_id"] == 7001 and row["upstream"]["img_id"] == 46000001
    # 调用 A 没跑成，人工得自己把字段填全——页面要能据此提示。
    assert row["stage_a_available"] is False
    assert row["resolution"] is None
    # 时间按东八区给，和纠偏页一致。
    assert row["created_at"].endswith("+08:00")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("options,total,offset", [
    ({"resolved": True}, 0, 0),
    ({"search": "no-such-run"}, 0, 0),
    ({"offset": 1}, 1, 1),
])
def test_empty_failure_pages_do_not_scan_frozen_payloads(db, options, total, offset):
    from sqlalchemy import event

    _failed_material(db, 81)
    statements = []
    bind = db.get_bind()
    def record(_conn, _cursor, statement, _parameters, _context, _many):
        statements.append(statement)
    event.listen(bind, "before_cursor_execute", record)
    try:
        page = list_failures(db, **options)
    finally:
        event.remove(bind, "before_cursor_execute", record)
    assert page == {"items": [], "total": total, "limit": 50,
                    "offset": offset, "has_more": False}
    assert len(statements) == 1, "an empty page needs only its count, not a payload scan"


def test_a_manual_conclusion_produces_a_body_the_factory_contract_accepts(db):
    run = _failed_material(db, 2)
    payload = resolve_failure(
        db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
        reason="调用 A 反复超时，人工看图后定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-2")
    db.commit()

    event = db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id == payload["outbox_event_id"]))
    assert event is not None and event.review_state == "reviewed" and event.revision == 1
    from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob

    sent = load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob)
    output = sent["result"]["output"]
    # 关键：这一份真的能被工厂提交体接住，不是一个投出去必被拒的东西。
    body, _notes = build_submit_body(output, change=sent.get("change"))
    assert body["task_id"] == 7002 and body["grade"] == 6 and body["score"] == 68
    assert body["title"] == GOOD_FIELDS["title"] and len(body["tags"]) >= 4
    assert body["override_source"] == "human_correction" and body["override_actor"] == "ops"


def test_3dsu_manual_result_and_wire_bytes_match_521dc9ac(db):
    """Fixture captured from base commit's resolve_failure and compared byte-for-byte."""
    from app.factory_submit_contract import submit_form
    from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob
    expected = json.loads((pathlib.Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" /
                           "failure_console_3dsu_manual.json").read_text())
    run = _failed_material(db, 2)
    result = resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
        reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="byte-fixture")
    sent = load_snapshot_blob(db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, result["outbox_event_id"]).payload_blob)
    body, notes = build_submit_body(sent["result"]["output"], change=sent["change"])
    assert sent["result"] == expected["result"]
    assert submit_form(timestamp=1727000000, body=body)["body"].encode() == expected["body"].encode()
    assert notes == expected["notes"]


def test_a_manual_conclusion_never_pretends_to_be_a_model_fact(db):
    run = _failed_material(db, 3)
    resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L2", score=75, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                    reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-3")
    db.commit()

    refreshed = db.get(ProductionRun, run.id)
    # DAG 上它就是挂了，这是事实：不改 run 状态，也不补节点输出。
    assert refreshed.status == "failed"
    attempts = db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)).all()
    assert [a.step_key for a in attempts] == ["prompt_a"] and attempts[0].status == "failed"

    row = db.scalar(select(ManualFailureResolution).where(ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id == run.id))
    assert row is not None and row.level == "L2" and row.score == 75 and row.actor == "ops"
    sent = json.loads(row.payload_json)["result"]["output"]
    assert sent["prompt_a_status"] == "manual" and sent["prompt_b_status"] == "manual"
    assert sent["human_override"]["actor"] == "ops"
    assert sent["normalized_result"]["source"] == "manual_failure_resolution"


def test_the_same_submission_twice_publishes_once(db):
    run = _failed_material(db, 4)
    first = resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                            reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-4")
    db.commit()
    again = resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                            reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-4")
    db.commit()
    assert again["id"] == first["id"] and again["outbox_event_id"] == first["outbox_event_id"]
    assert db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.revision == 2)) is None


def test_a_later_human_conclusion_overrides_the_earlier_one(db):
    run = _failed_material(db, 5)
    first = resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                            reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-5a")
    db.commit()
    second = resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L2", score=80, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                             reason="我觉得更高", actor="ops2", idempotency_key="k-5b")
    db.commit()
    assert second["id"] == first["id"]
    assert (second["level"], second["score"], second["actor"]) == ("L2", 80, "ops2")
    assert second["outbox_event_id"] != first["outbox_event_id"]
    # A late retry of the superseded key must not publish the old level again.
    replay = resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                             reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-5a")
    db.commit()
    assert (replay["level"], replay["outbox_event_id"]) == ("L2", second["outbox_event_id"])
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ManualFailureResolution)) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("bad,code", [

    ({"title": "   "}, "failure_resolution_field_required"),
    ({"tags": ["只有", "三个", "标签"]}, "failure_resolution_tags_insufficient"),
])
def test_bad_fields_are_refused_instead_of_quietly_truncated(db, bad, code):

                        reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-6")
    assert info.value.code == code and info.value.status_code == 422

def test_the_form_prefills_from_stage_a_when_it_did_run(db):

    form = resolution_form(db, run.id)
