    assert form["prefill"]["tags"] == GOOD_FIELDS["tags"]
    assert form["limits"]["title"] == 10 and form["min_tags"] == 4


def test_a_score_outside_the_scale_is_refused(db):
    run = _failed_material(db, 9)
    for score in (-1, 101, "68", True):
        with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError) as info:
            resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=score, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                            reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key=f"k-9-{score}")
        assert info.value.code == "failure_resolution_score_invalid"


def _additive_model_modules() -> list[str]:
    """``models.py`` 尾部追加导入的那批模型模块。"""
    source = (pathlib.Path(app.__file__).parent / "models.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    tree = ast.parse(source)
    return sorted({
        node.module
        for node in tree.body
        if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom)
        and node.level == 1
        and node.module
        and node.module.endswith("_models")
    })


def test_additive_model_modules_take_base_from_database() -> None:
    """追加模型模块的 ``Base`` 必须来自 ``app.database``，不能来自 ``app.models``。

    ``models.py`` 尾部会反向导入这些模块来登记建表元数据。谁要是从 ``.models`` 取
    ``Base``，就跟那条尾部导入构成环：只要这个模块被**先**导入（迁移容器就是这个顺序），
    ``models`` 还没定义完就被要名字，直接 ImportError。09-20 .137 部署就挂在这。
    """
    for name in _additive_model_modules():
        source = (pathlib.Path(app.__file__).parent / f"{name}.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "from .models import Base" not in source, name
        assert "from .database import Base" in source, name


def test_manual_failure_resolution_module_imports_first() -> None:
    """冷启动单独先导入本模块不能炸——迁移步骤就是这么导的。"""
    backend_root = pathlib.Path(app.__file__).parent.parent
    proc = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-c",
         "import app.failure_resolution_models as m; print(m.ManualFailureResolution.__tablename__)"],
        cwd=str(backend_root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180,
        env={**os.environ, "PYTHONPATH": str(backend_root)},
    )
    assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stderr[-2000:]
    assert "manual_failure_resolutions" in proc.stdout


def test_category_and_resolution_filters_apply_before_pagination(db):
    unresolved = _failed_material(db, 101)
    resolved = _failed_material(db, 102)
    other = _failed_material(db, 103)
    other.category_key = "knowledge_graph"
    resolve_failure(db, run_id=resolved.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                    reason="人工核验", actor="ops", idempotency_key="filtered-102")
    db.flush()
    page = list_failures(db, category_key="model_3d_su", resolved=False, limit=1)
    assert page["total"] == 1
    assert page["has_more"] is False
    assert [r["production_run_id"] for r in page["items"]] == [unresolved.id]
    page = list_failures(db, category_key="model_3d_su", resolved=True, limit=1)
    assert page["total"] == 1
    assert [r["production_run_id"] for r in page["items"]] == [resolved.id]
    assert list_failures(db, category_key="inspiration_image")["total"] == 0
    page = list_failures(db, category_key="knowledge_graph", search=str(other.id))
    assert page["total"] == 1 and page["items"][0]["category_key"] == "knowledge_graph"


def test_rejected_fields_are_candidates_not_successful_output(db):
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    run = _failed_material(db, 912)
    attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id))
    invalid = dict(GOOD_FIELDS, seotitle='长' * 100)
    call = WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run.id,step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
        node_instance_id='prompt_a',provider='test',model_id='test',status='failed',
        error_code='structured_output_schema_mismatch',error_message='seotitle 超过 maxLength',
        evidence_json=json.dumps({'failure':{'failure_evidence':{
            'candidate_production_fields':invalid,'validation_error':'seotitle 超过 maxLength'}}}))
    db.add(call);db.flush()
    form = resolution_form(db, run.id)
    assert form['candidate_invocation_id'] == call.id
    assert not form['stage_a_available']
    assert len(form['prefill']['seotitle']) == 100
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError):
        resolve_failure(db,run_id=run.id,level='L3',score=50,fields=invalid,reason='核验',actor='ops',idempotency_key='bad')
    saved=resolve_failure(db,run_id=run.id,level='L3',score=50,fields=GOOD_FIELDS,reason='修正超长标题',actor='ops',idempotency_key='fixed')
    assert saved['fields']==GOOD_FIELDS
    assert attempt.status=='failed' and run.status=='failed'


def test_a_manual_conclusion_blocks_checkpoint_retry(db):
    # 复审 09-26：已有人工结论的 run 仍可 retry_failure，白花钱且可能用模型结果覆盖人工。
    from app.failure_console import retry_failure

    run = _failed_material(db, 31)
    resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                    reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-31")
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError) as info:
        retry_failure(db, run_id=run.id, actor="ops", reason="再跑一次")
    assert info.value.code == "run_retry_manual_resolution_exists"
    assert db.get(ProductionRun, run.id).status == "failed"


def test_a_manual_conclusion_stops_automatic_retries_of_a_retryable_run(db):
    run = _failed_material(db, 32)
    run.status = "retryable"
    attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id))
    attempt.status = "retryable"
    db.commit()
    resolve_failure(db, run_id=run.id, level="L3", score=68, fields=GOOD_FIELDS,
                    reason="人工定级", actor="ops", idempotency_key="k-32")
    db.commit()
    stored = db.get(ProductionRun, run.id)
    assert stored.status == "failed" and stored.error_code == "manual_failure_resolved"
    assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempt.id).status == "skipped"
    assert db.scalar(select(ManualFailureResolution).where(
        ManualFailureResolution.production_run_id == run.id)).outbox_event_id is not None


def test_a_failure_later_superseded_by_a_successful_run_counts_as_handled(db):
    """09-30：7 条未处理失败里 6 条的素材已被后续运行成功投递（缩图重进、换机制重跑）。
    同一 content_record 之后有成功的增量运行 → 算已处理，并在行上链到那条运行。"""
    superseded = _failed_material(db, 401)
    still_open = _failed_material(db, 402)
    later = ProductionRun(
        run_key="run-401-retake", idempotency_key="idem-401-retake", source_type="content_ingress_event",
        source_id=superseded.source_id, workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=superseded.workflow_version_id,
        snapshot_hash=superseded.snapshot_hash, snapshot_json=superseded.snapshot_json,
        category_key="model_3d_su", queue_class="production_batch", status="succeeded", blockers_json="[]",
        requested_by="t", owner="t", reason="", environment="dry_run", total_steps=3, completed_steps=3, failed_steps=0)
    db.add(later)
    db.commit()
    open_page = list_failures(db, category_key="model_3d_su", resolved=False)
    assert [r["production_run_id"] for r in open_page["items"]] == [still_open.id]
    assert open_page["items"][0]["superseded_by"] is None
    done_page = list_failures(db, category_key="model_3d_su", resolved=True)
    assert [r["production_run_id"] for r in done_page["items"]] == [superseded.id]
    assert done_page["items"][0]["superseded_by"] == {"production_run_id": later.id, "status": "succeeded"}
    # 更早的成功运行不算补上（失败发生在它之后）
    assert list_failures(db, category_key="model_3d_su")["total"] == 2

===== FILE backend/tests/test_failure_console_inspiration.py =====
"""Offline failure-console → outbox → one-send factory integration."""
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.database import get_db
from app.failure_console import FailureConsoleError, resolution_form, resolve_failure, submittable_levels
from app.failure_console_api import build_failure_console_router
from app.models import ConnectorContractVersion, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeOutboxEvent, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob
from app.connector_delivery import DeliveryBatch, DeliveryItem, DeliveryKey
from app.inspiration_factory_delivery import InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter
from test_failure_console import db, _failed_material
from test_inspiration_factory_delivery import CONFIG, FIELDS, IDENTITY, TEST_URL, FakeFactory, private_path


def ledger_evidence(parsed, semantic=None):
    """与生产调用账本同形：模型调用证据在 result 下，与 reservation、pricing_settlement 并列。"""
    result = {"parsed_output": parsed}
    if semantic is not None:
        result["semantic_validation"] = semantic
    return json.dumps({"schema_version": "workflow-model-invocation-v1", "reservation": {},
                       "pricing_settlement": {}, "result": result})


def inspiration_run(db):
    run = _failed_material(db, 901)
    run.category_key = "inspiration_image"
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    manifest = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]
    manifest["category_key"] = run.category_key
    levels = json.loads(manifest["nodes"][1]["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"])
    levels["levels"][-1].update(enabled=True, min_score=0)
    manifest["nodes"][1]["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"] = json.dumps(levels)
    frozen = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
    frozen.pop("factory_task")
    frozen["inspiration_task"] = deepcopy(IDENTITY)
    run.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
    contract = db.get(ConnectorContractVersion, 1)
    contract.category_key = run.category_key
    contract.adapter_key = "inspiration-factory-submit-v1"
    config = deepcopy(CONFIG)
    config["level_mapping"]["L5"] = {"grade": 73, "review_result": "reject"}
    config["review_result_values"]["reject"] = 9
    contract.field_mapping_json = json.dumps(config)
    db.commit()
    return run, config


def resolve(db, run, key="manual-one", **changes):
    return resolve_failure(db, **dict(dict(run_id=run.id, level="L5", score=0,
        fields=deepcopy(FIELDS), reason="人工核验随手拍，不通过", actor="reviewer", idempotency_key=key), **changes))


def delivery(db, result):
    event = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, result["outbox_event_id"])
    payload = load_snapshot_blob(event.payload_blob)
    key = DeliveryKey(event.business_idempotency_key, event.mechanism_version_id,
                      event.payload_hash, event.revision, event.review_state)
    return DeliveryBatch(event.target_contract_key, event.target_contract_version_id,
                         (DeliveryItem(event.id, key, payload),))


def test_inspiration_form_has_own_fields_and_frozen_contract_levels(db):
    run, _ = inspiration_run(db)
    form = resolution_form(db, run.id)
    assert form["field_profile"] == "inspiration_image"
    assert set(form["prefill"]) == set(FIELDS)
    assert "L5" in form["submittable_levels"]
    assert form["limits"] == {} and form["min_tags"] == 0
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    node = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][1]
    content = json.loads(node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"])
    content["levels"][-1]["enabled"] = False
    node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"] = json.dumps(content)
    run.snapshot_json = json.dumps(snapshot)
    assert "L5" not in submittable_levels(run)


def test_latest_rejected_model_draft_uses_normal_a_projection_and_reasons(db):
    run, _ = inspiration_run(db)
    attempt = db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id))
    reasons = ["redline_reason_conflict:casual_photo", "missing_evidence:redline:casual_photo"]
    raw = dict(FIELDS, seotitle="草稿标题", primary_category="室内", secondary_category="客厅",
               reason=["理由一", "理由二"], image_defects=["模糊", "灰暗"])
    raw.pop("seo_title")
    raw["class_text"] = "模型乱填的分类"
    for title in ("旧草稿", "最新草稿"):
        call = WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run.id, step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
            invocation_key=title, node_instance_id="prompt_a", provider="test", model_id="test",
            status="succeeded", evidence_json=ledger_evidence(dict(raw, title=title),
                {"status": "rejected", "reasons": reasons}))
        db.add(call)
        db.flush()
    form = resolution_form(db, run.id)
    assert not form["stage_a_available"]
    assert form["draft_source"] == "rejected_model_draft"
    assert form["candidate_invocation_id"] == call.id
    assert form["validation_reasons"] == reasons
    assert form["prefill"]["title"] == "最新草稿"
    assert form["prefill"]["seo_title"] == "草稿标题"
    assert form["prefill"]["class_text"] == "室内-客厅"
    assert form["prefill"]["reason"] == "理由一；理由二"
    assert form["prefill"]["image_defects"] == "模糊；灰暗"
    attempt.status = "succeeded"
    attempt.output_manifest_json = json.dumps({"output": dict(raw, title="已通过")})
    db.flush()
    form = resolution_form(db, run.id)
    assert form["stage_a_available"] and form["draft_source"] == "validated_stage_a"
    assert form["prefill"]["title"] == "已通过" and form["candidate_invocation_id"] is None


def test_manual_l5_api_outbox_fake_factory_posts_once_and_keeps_human_priority(db, private_path, monkeypatch):
    run, config = inspiration_run(db)
    app = FastAPI()
    user = lambda: SimpleNamespace(username="reviewer")
    app.include_router(build_failure_console_router(user, user))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db
    client = TestClient(app)
    request = dict(level="L5", score=0, fields=FIELDS, reason="人工核验不通过", idempotency_key="api-one")
    response = client.post(f"/api/failures/{run.id}/resolution", json=request)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    result = response.json()
    submitted = delivery(db, result)
    assert submitted.items[0].key.revision == 1
    output = submitted.items[0].payload["result"]["output"]
    assert output["schema_version"] == "inspiration-factory-result-v1"
    assert output["inspiration_task"] == IDENTITY and output["a_fields"] == FIELDS
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_INSPIRATION_FACTORY_TEST_ENABLED", "1")
    fake = FakeFactory()
    bodies = []
    def send(url, envelope):
        response = fake.send(url, envelope)
        if envelope["action"] == "submit":
            bodies.append(deepcopy(envelope["body"]))
            fake.task["review_result"] = config["review_result_values"][envelope["body"]["review_result"]]
        return response
    def worker():
        return InspirationFactoryDownstreamAdapter(TEST_URL, level_mapping=config["level_mapping"],
            review_result_values=config["review_result_values"], journal_path=str(private_path), send=send)
    assert worker().deliver(submitted).results[0].succeeded
    assert worker().deliver(submitted).results[0].succeeded
    assert bodies[0]["review_result"] == "reject" and bodies[0]["grade"] == 73
    # 首次落地按普通提交发：正式工厂对仍是「已领取」的任务拒收人工纠偏标记（10-01 任务 171061）。
    assert "override_source" not in bodies[0]
    assert len(bodies) == 1
    assert client.post(f"/api/failures/{run.id}/resolution", json=request).json()["outbox_event_id"] == result["outbox_event_id"]
    second = resolve(db, run, "manual-two", level="L2", score=80)
    db.commit()
    assert worker().deliver(delivery(db, second)).results[0].succeeded
    assert len(bodies) == 2 and bodies[1]["override_source"] == "human_correction"
    assert not worker().deliver(submitted).results[0].succeeded
    assert len(bodies) == 2
    assert client.post(f"/api/failures/{run.id}/resolution", json=request).json()["outbox_event_id"] == second["outbox_event_id"]
    from app.workflow_runtime import build_incremental_projection_outbox_request, WorkflowRuntimeError
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError, match="人工"):
        build_incremental_projection_outbox_request(db, run, step_key="downstream",
            output_manifest={"output": output})
    assert run.status == "failed"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_failure_recovery.py =====
import json
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.database import Base
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.models import ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, AuditEvent, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.failure_recovery import recovery_preview, recover_failure, recovery_authority
from app.failure_console import FailureConsoleError, resolution_form, resolve_failure
from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation, fail_model_invocation, WorkflowModelBudgetError
from test_native_baseline_regression import _baseline
from test_stock_mechanism_selection import _mechanism
from test_native_workflow_real_regression import _attach_priced_model, _available_test_model_secret

@pytest.fixture
def setup(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    _available_test_model_secret.__wrapped__(monkeypatch)
    engine=create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "recovery.db"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine,expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
        from app.workflow_runtime import terminalize_runtime_run
        from app.workflow_result_contract import hash_manifest
        baseline,workflow=_baseline(db),_mechanism(db)
        _attach_priced_model(db,workflow)
        aggregate=create_native_baseline_run(db,baseline_set=baseline,workflow_version=workflow,
            actor='test',idempotency_key='source',model_execution_mode='real',real_execution_confirmed=True)
        run=db.get(ProductionRun,aggregate.items[0].production_run_id)
        run.source_type='content_ingress_event';run.source_id=123;run.run_mode='incremental';run.queue_class='production_batch'
        snapshot=json.loads(run.snapshot_json);runtime=snapshot['runtime_context']
        runtime.update(run_mode='incremental',content_ingress_event_db_id=123,
            real_execution_authority='frozen_incremental_autorun_policy',incremental_mechanism_snapshot_hash='a'*64,
            content_ingress_payload_hash='b'*64,upstream_intake_payload_hash='c'*64)
        runtime['real_execution_budget']['max_requests']=1
        run.snapshot_json=canonical_json(snapshot);run.snapshot_hash=hash_manifest(snapshot)
        attempt=db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run.id,ProductionStepAttempt.step_key=='prompt_c'))
        node=next(n for n in snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'] if n['node_instance_id']=='prompt_c')
        db.commit()
        call=reserve_model_invocation(db,attempt_id=attempt.id,node=node,run_snapshot=snapshot)
        fail_model_invocation(db,invocation_id=call.id,error_code='model_invocation_outcome_unknown',error_message='timeout',provider_dispatched=True)
        attempt.status='failed';attempt.last_error_code='model_budget_requests_exhausted'
        terminalize_runtime_run(db,run,status='failed',error_code='model_budget_requests_exhausted',error_message='budget')
        db.commit()
        yield db,run,node,snapshot,attempt
    engine.dispose()


def test_recovery_is_bounded_idempotent_and_preserves_facts(setup):
    db,run,node,snapshot,old=setup
    old_snapshot=run.snapshot_json
    quote=recovery_preview(db,run.id,1)
    assert quote['blockers']==[]
    assert quote['model_calls']==1 and quote['budget']['max_requests']==2
    recover_failure(db,run_id=run.id,quote_hash=quote['quote_hash'],requests=1,reason='核验后继续',confirmed=True,actor='ops')
    db.commit()
    assert recover_failure(db,run_id=run.id,quote_hash=quote['quote_hash'],requests=1,reason='核验后继续',confirmed=True,actor='ops')['idempotent_replay']
    assert run.snapshot_json==old_snapshot and old.status=='failed'
    attempt=db.scalar(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run.id,ProductionStepAttempt.step_key=='prompt_c').order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id.desc()))
    new=reserve_model_invocation(db,attempt_id=attempt.id,node=node,run_snapshot=snapshot,invocation_key='recovery-one')
    assert new.id!=1
    fail_model_invocation(db,invocation_id=new.id,error_code='model_invocation_outcome_unknown',error_message='timeout',provider_dispatched=True)
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
        reserve_model_invocation(db,attempt_id=attempt.id,node=node,run_snapshot=snapshot,invocation_key='recovery-two')
    assert exc.value.code=='model_budget_requests_exhausted'
    assert len(db.scalars(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action=='failure_recovery_budget')).all())==1


def test_stale_quote_or_missing_confirmation_cannot_grant(setup):
    db,run,_,_,_=setup
    quote=recovery_preview(db,run.id)
    for confirmed,hash_ in [(False,quote['quote_hash']),(True,'f'*64)]:
        with pytest.raises(FailureConsoleError):
            recover_failure(db,run_id=run.id,quote_hash=hash_,requests=1,reason='核验',confirmed=confirmed,actor='ops')
    assert recovery_authority(db,run) is None


def test_legacy_budget_wait_can_resume_without_resetting_charges(setup):
    db,run,_,_,attempt=setup
    run.status='retryable';attempt.status='retryable';attempt.last_error_code='model_budget_wait'
    for sibling in db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id==run.id)):
        if sibling.sequence < attempt.sequence:
            sibling.status='succeeded';sibling.output_manifest_json='{}'
    db.commit()
    quote=recovery_preview(db,run.id,2)
    assert not quote['blockers']
    recover_failure(db,run_id=run.id,quote_hash=quote['quote_hash'],requests=2,reason='核验并补充',confirmed=True,actor='ops')
    assert run.status=='queued' and attempt.status=='failed'
    assert recovery_authority(db,run)['budget']['max_requests']==3


def test_recovery_endpoint_validates_permissions_and_confirmation(setup):
    from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.failure_console_api import build_failure_console_router
    db,run,_,_,_=setup
    allowed=False
    def reviewer():
        if not allowed: raise HTTPException(403,'review permission required')
        return SimpleNamespace(username='ops')
    app=FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_failure_console_router(lambda:SimpleNamespace(username='reader'),reviewer))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db]=lambda:db
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        quote=client.get(f'/api/failures/{run.id}/recovery-preview').json()
        body={'quote_hash':quote['quote_hash'],'requests':1,'reason':'核验','confirmed':True}
        assert client.post(f'/api/failures/{run.id}/recover',json=body).status_code==403
        allowed=True
        assert client.post(f'/api/failures/{run.id}/recover',json={**body,'requests':3}).status_code==422
        assert client.post(f'/api/failures/{run.id}/recover',json={**body,'confirmed':False}).status_code==422
        # Rollback expires the fixture object; refresh quote before the successful request.
        quote=client.get(f'/api/failures/{run.id}/recovery-preview').json()
        response=client.post(f'/api/failures/{run.id}/recover',json={**body,'quote_hash':quote['quote_hash']})
        assert response.status_code==200, response.text
        assert response.json()['status']=='queued'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_feedback_lock_profile.py =====
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from test_batch_submission_pacing import database, admit, NOW


def test_profile_executes_two_real_feedback_selects_and_explains_without_reserving(database):
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'docs/verification/batch-concurrency-recovery-20260912/feedback-lock-profile.py'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('feedback_profile', path)
    profile = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(profile)
    engine, pool = database
    with Session(engine) as db:
        admit(db, pool, 1, NOW)
        result = profile.profile_feedback(db, pool_id=pool, now=NOW)
        assert len(result['queries']) == 2
        for query in result['queries']:
            assert query['seconds'] >= 0 and query['explain']
            assert query['sql'].lstrip().upper().startswith('SELECT')
        from app.models import ModelQuotaLease, WorkflowModelInvocation
        from sqlalchemy import select, func
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_field_demand_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Iterator

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.field_demand_contracts import (
    FieldDemandContractError,
    create_field_demand_contract,
    record_asset_version,
)
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import Asset, AssetVersion, FieldDemandContract, User


def _fields(*, source_path: str = "semantic.style") -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    return [
        {
            "field_key": "style",
            "source_path": source_path,
            "required": True,
            "data_type": "string",
            "target_roles": ["unified_dimension", "search_labels"],
        }
    ]


def _contract_args(**overrides: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    args: dict[str, object] = {
        "contract_key": "3d-search",
        "category_key": "model_3d_su",
        "consumer_key": "search",
        "owner": "tpeng-search",
        "fields": _fields(),
        "thresholds": {"accuracy": 0.9, "recall": 0.9},
        "status": "draft",
        "created_by": "admin",
    }
    args.update(overrides)
    return args


@pytest.fixture
def sessions() -> Iterator[sessionmaker[Session]]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    factory = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield factory
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.fixture
def db(sessions: sessionmaker[Session]) -> Iterator[Session]:
    with sessions() as session:
        yield session


@pytest.fixture
def asset(db: Session) -> Asset:
    row = Asset(
        original_name="model.png",
        stored_name="model.png",
        mime_type="image/png",
        size_bytes=128,
        width=32,
        height=32,
        sha256="a" * 64,
        category_key="model_3d_su",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(row)
    return row


def test_field_contract_rejects_candidate_path(db: Session) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(FieldDemandContractError, match="Canonical"):
        create_field_demand_contract(
            db,
            **_contract_args(fields=_fields(source_path="candidate.style")),
        )


def test_field_contract_is_idempotent_and_changed_definition_increments_version(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    first = create_field_demand_contract(db, **_contract_args())
    duplicate = create_field_demand_contract(db, **_contract_args())
    changed = create_field_demand_contract(
        db,
        **_contract_args(thresholds={"accuracy": 0.95, "recall": 0.9}),
    )
    db.commit()

    assert duplicate.id == first.id
    assert duplicate.contract_hash == first.contract_hash
    assert first.version == 1
    assert changed.version == 2
    assert changed.contract_hash != first.contract_hash
    assert [item.version for item in db.scalars(
        select(FieldDemandContract).order_by(FieldDemandContract.version.desc())
    ).all()] == [2, 1]


def test_field_contract_rejects_duplicate_keys_and_invalid_thresholds(db: Session) -> None:
    duplicate_fields = _fields() + [
        {
            "field_key": "style",
            "source_path": "quality.style_confidence",
            "required": False,
            "data_type": "number",
        }
    ]
    with pytest.raises(FieldDemandContractError, match="重复"):
        create_field_demand_contract(db, **_contract_args(fields=duplicate_fields))
    with pytest.raises(FieldDemandContractError, match="0 到 1"):
        create_field_demand_contract(
            db,
            **_contract_args(thresholds={"accuracy": 1.1, "recall": 0.9}),
        )


def test_asset_version_reuses_identical_source_version(
    db: Session,
    asset: Asset,
) -> None:
    args = {
        "source_system": "fixture-3d",
        "source_content_id": "1001",
        "source_version": "v7",
        "asset": asset,
        "occurred_at": datetime(2026, 8, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    }
    first, first_duplicate = record_asset_version(db, **args)
    second, second_duplicate = record_asset_version(db, **args)
    db.commit()

    assert first_duplicate is False
    assert second_duplicate is True
    assert second.id == first.id
    assert second.payload_hash == first.payload_hash


def test_asset_version_rejects_conflicting_payload_for_same_source_version(
    db: Session,
    asset: Asset,
) -> None:
    occurred_at = datetime(2026, 8, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    record_asset_version(
        db,
        source_system="fixture-3d",
        source_content_id="1001",
        source_version="v7",
        asset=asset,
        occurred_at=occurred_at,
    )
    with pytest.raises(FieldDemandContractError, match="ASSET_VERSION_CONFLICT"):
        record_asset_version(
            db,
            source_system="fixture-3d",
            source_content_id="1001",
            source_version="v7",
            asset=asset,
            occurred_at=occurred_at + timedelta(seconds=1),
        )


@contextmanager
def _client_context(
    sessions: sessionmaker[Session],
    *,
    role: str,
    is_admin: bool = False,
) -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    user = User(
        username=f"{role}-user",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name=role,
        is_admin=is_admin,
        role=role,
    )

    def override_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        with sessions() as session:
            yield session

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        with TestClient(app) as client:
            yield client
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()


def test_field_contract_api_enforces_admin_write_and_redacted_read(
    sessions: sessionmaker[Session],
) -> None:
    with _client_context(sessions, role="admin", is_admin=True) as client:
        first = client.post("/api/field-demand-contracts", json=_contract_args())
        second_payload = _contract_args(
            thresholds={"accuracy": 0.95, "recall": 0.9}
        )
        second = client.post("/api/field-demand-contracts", json=second_payload)
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        assert [first.json()["version"], second.json()["version"]] == [1, 2]

    with _client_context(sessions, role="viewer") as client:
        denied = client.post("/api/field-demand-contracts", json=_contract_args())
        listed = client.get("/api/field-demand-contracts")
        assert denied.status_code == 403
        assert listed.status_code == 200, listed.text
        body = listed.json()
        assert [item["version"] for item in body["items"]] == [2, 1]
        assert body["items"][0]["fields"] == _fields()
        serialized = listed.text.lower()
        assert "password" not in serialized
        assert "secret" not in serialized
        assert "token" not in serialized


def test_asset_version_api_lists_newest_first(
    sessions: sessionmaker[Session],
) -> None:
    with sessions() as db:
        asset = Asset(
            original_name="api-model.png",
            stored_name="api-model.png",
            mime_type="image/png",
            size_bytes=256,
            sha256="b" * 64,
            category_key="model_3d_su",
        )
        db.add(asset)
        db.flush()
        for source_version, offset in (("v1", 0), ("v2", 1)):
            record_asset_version(
                db,
                source_system="fixture-3d",
                source_content_id="1002",
                source_version=source_version,
                asset=asset,
                occurred_at=datetime(2026, 8, 14, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                + timedelta(minutes=offset),
            )
        db.commit()
        asset_id = asset.id

    with _client_context(sessions, role="viewer") as client:
        response = client.get(f"/api/assets/{asset_id}/versions")
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert [item["source_version"] for item in response.json()["items"]] == [
            "v2",
            "v1",
        ]
        assert set(response.json()["items"][0]) == {
            "id",
            "source_system",
            "source_content_id",
            "source_version",
            "asset_id",
            "sha256",
            "mime_type",
            "size_bytes",
            "occurred_at",
            "payload_hash",
            "created_at",
        }

===== FILE backend/tests/test_field_review_policy.py =====
"""字段校验强度：运营 2026-09-03 的分层口径必须可执行、可复现、不漏字段。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import collections

from app.field_review_policy import (
    CRITICAL,
    EXEMPT,
    REQUIRED,
    SAMPLED,
    policy_for_category,
    resolve_field_review,
    review_summary,
)


def _resolve(field: str, item_key: str, salt: str = "run:215"):
    return resolve_field_review(
        category_key="knowledge_graph", field=field, salt=salt, item_key=item_key
    )


def test_operator_tiers_are_encoded_exactly() -> None:
    rules = policy_for_category("knowledge_graph")
    full = {"ll_id", "res_id", "is_single", "title"}
    every = {"space", "object", "style", "form", "structure"}
    half = {"material", "structural_features", "architectural_element", "color"}
    light = {"soft_decoration", "hard_decoration"}
    assert full | every | half | light == set(rules)
    for f in full:
        assert rules[f].rate == 1.0 and rules[f].emphasis == CRITICAL
    for f in every:
        assert rules[f].rate == 1.0 and rules[f].emphasis != CRITICAL
    for f in half:
        assert rules[f].rate == 0.5
    for f in light:
        assert rules[f].rate == 0.15
    # 3D&SU 走同一套合同，策略共用
    assert policy_for_category("model_3d_su") == rules


def test_required_fields_are_never_exempt() -> None:
    for field in ("res_id", "is_single", "title", "space", "object", "style", "form", "structure"):
        for item in range(50):
            assert _resolve(field, str(item))["state"] == REQUIRED


def test_sampling_is_deterministic_and_near_target_rate() -> None:
    # 同一轮同一条目稳定
    a = _resolve("material", "5872")
    b = _resolve("material", "5872")
    assert a == b and a["state"] in {SAMPLED, EXEMPT}
    # 换一轮换一批样本
    other = [_resolve("material", str(i), salt="run:216")["state"] for i in range(400)]
    same = [_resolve("material", str(i), salt="run:215")["state"] for i in range(400)]
    assert other != same
    for field, target in (("material", 0.5), ("color", 0.5), ("soft_decoration", 0.15)):
        hit = sum(1 for i in range(2000) if _resolve(field, str(i))["state"] == SAMPLED)
        rate = hit / 2000
        assert abs(rate - target) < 0.05, (field, rate)


def test_unknown_field_defaults_to_required_not_silently_exempt() -> None:
    """新增字段没写进策略时必须按必看处理，不能被静默免检。"""

    got = _resolve("brand_new_field", "1")
    assert got["state"] == REQUIRED and got["sample_rate"] == 1.0


def test_categories_without_policy_keep_full_manual_review() -> None:
    assert resolve_field_review(
        category_key="inspiration_image", field="color", salt="run:1", item_key="1"
    ) is None
    assert review_summary("inspiration_image")["fields"] == {}


def test_summary_exposes_rates_for_operators() -> None:
    s = review_summary("knowledge_graph")
    assert s["fields"]["res_id"] == {"sample_rate": 1.0, "emphasis": CRITICAL}
    assert s["fields"]["hard_decoration"]["sample_rate"] == 0.15


def test_每条素材的待看字段数显著下降() -> None:
    """13 个字段全看 → 分层后平均每条约 10 个，其中 8 个必看。"""

    per_item = []
    for item in range(200):
        need = sum(
            1
            for f in policy_for_category("knowledge_graph")
            if f not in {"ll_id"}  # 海外链路用 res_id
            and _resolve(f, str(item))["state"] in {REQUIRED, SAMPLED}
        )
        per_item.append(need)
    avg = sum(per_item) / len(per_item)
    assert 9.0 <= avg <= 11.5, avg
    assert min(per_item) >= 8

===== FILE backend/tests/test_first_module_cutover.py =====
"""Offline control-plane tests: no Docker, network, credentials or database."""
import copy
import importlib.util
import json
import socket
from types import SimpleNamespace
from pathlib import Path
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def load(path, name):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, ROOT / path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


cutover = load('deploy/server/first_module_cutover.py', 'cutover')
gate = load('deploy/server/release-ops/gated-acquisition.py', 'gate')
health = load('deploy/server/first_module_health.py', 'first_health')


def plan():
    return {'contract': 'first-module-cutover-v1', 'host': 'yukina',
        'old_schema': 126, 'new_schema': 127, 'old_owners': 7,
        'old_containers': sorted(cutover.OLD_NAMES),
        'units': {key: 'module-' + key for key in cutover.UNITS},
        'capacities': {'incremental': 16, 'stock': 32, 'compatibility': 1},
        'image': 'sha256:' + 'a' * 64, 'old_image': 'sha256:' + 'b' * 64,
        'source_sha': 'c' * 40, 'schema_name': 'labelsystem_history_20260901_5b275e7',
        'release_id': 'c' * 40 + '-20260924T030000Z',
        'backup_root': '/backup/production',
        'backup_dir': '/backup/production/pre-module-cccccccc-20260924T030000Z',
        'backup_min_free_bytes': 128 * 1024**3}


class Scene:
    def __init__(self, root, failure=None):
        self.root, self.failure = root, failure
        self.plan = plan()
        self.log = []
        self.rows = [{'Id': str(i) * 64, 'Name': '/' + name, 'Image': self.plan['old_image'],
                      'State': {'Running': True, 'Paused': False}}
                     for i, name in enumerate(sorted(cutover.OLD_NAMES), 1)]
        self.locked = {'state': 'locked_idle', 'schema': 126, 'expected_owners': 7,
            'baseline_image': self.plan['old_image'], 'old_ids': [r['Id'] for r in self.rows],
            'first_cutover_sha256': 'd' * 64}
        (root / 'admission-locked.json').write_text(json.dumps(self.locked))
        (root / 'first-cutover.json').write_text(json.dumps(self.plan))
        self.state = {'schema': 126, 'business': {'frozen_items': [['completed', 17, 98]]}}

    def step(self, name):
        self.log.append(name)
        if self.failure == name:
            raise RuntimeError('injected failure: ' + name)

    def inspect(self, ids):
        self.step('inspect')
        return copy.deepcopy(self.rows)

    def admission(self, locked, sha):
        self.step('admission')
        assert locked == self.locked and sha == 'd' * 64

    def preflight(self):
        self.step('preflight')

    def stop(self, ids):
        self.step('stop')
        assert ids == self.locked['old_ids']
        assert json.loads((self.root / 'first-cutover-receipt.json').read_text())['status'] == 'running'
        for row in self.rows:
            row['State']['Running'] = False

    def backup(self):
        self.step('backup')
        assert all(not r['State']['Running'] for r in self.rows)
        (self.root / 'admission-gate-released.json').write_text(json.dumps(
            {'status': 'released', 'stopped_ids': self.locked['old_ids']}))

    def inventory(self):
        self.step('inventory')
        return copy.deepcopy(self.state)

    def migrate(self):
        self.step('migrate')
        self.state.update(schema=127, pools=[['stock', 17]], unclassified=0, wrong_known_pool=0, configuration=[])

    def initialize(self):
        self.step('initialize')
        self.state['configuration'] = [[p, n, 49, 1, ''] for p, n in sorted(self.plan['capacities'].items())]

    def start(self):
        self.step('start')

    def health(self):
        self.step('health')
        return {'status': 'verified', 'source_sha': self.plan['source_sha'], 'schema': 127}

    def run(self):
        return cutover.execute(self.root, self.plan, self, 'd' * 64)


def test_only_exact_legacy_ids_stop_after_live_check_then_backup_before_migration(tmp_path):
    scene = Scene(tmp_path)
    result = scene.run()
    assert scene.log == ['inspect', 'preflight', 'admission', 'stop', 'inspect', 'backup', 'inventory',
                         'migrate', 'inventory', 'initialize', 'inventory', 'start', 'health']
    assert result['status'] == 'topology_ready' and result['production_accepted'] is False
    assert result['before']['business'] == result['after_migration']['business']
    before = scene.log.copy()
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='automatic retry forbidden'):
        scene.run()
    assert scene.log == before


@pytest.mark.parametrize('failure', ['preflight', 'admission', 'stop', 'backup', 'migrate', 'initialize', 'start', 'health'])
def test_failed_stage_never_advances_or_rolls_back(tmp_path, failure):
    scene = Scene(tmp_path, failure)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='injected failure'):
        scene.run()
    assert scene.log[-1] == failure
    path = tmp_path / 'first-cutover-receipt.json'
    if failure in ('preflight', 'admission'):
        assert not path.exists() and not (tmp_path / 'deployment-started').exists()
    else:
        record = json.loads(path.read_text())
        assert record['status'] == 'needs_forward_recovery'
        assert record['production_accepted'] is False


def test_stale_container_identity_or_paused_old_container_never_stops(tmp_path):
    scene = Scene(tmp_path)
    scene.rows[0]['Id'] = 'e' * 64
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='identities changed'):
        scene.run()
    scene.rows[0]['Id'] = scene.locked['old_ids'][0]
    scene.rows[0]['State']['Paused'] = True
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='identities changed'):
        scene.run()
    assert set(scene.log) == {'inspect'}


@pytest.mark.parametrize('bad', ['schema', 'business', 'unclassified', 'wrong_known_pool', 'configuration'])
def test_invalid_migration_never_initializes_or_starts(tmp_path, bad):
    scene = Scene(tmp_path)
    migrate = scene.migrate
    def corrupted():
        migrate()
        scene.state[bad] = {'schema': 126, 'business': {}, 'unclassified': 1,
                            'wrong_known_pool': 1, 'configuration': [['stock', 99]]}[bad]
    scene.migrate = corrupted
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='migration inventory mismatch'):
        scene.run()
    assert 'initialize' not in scene.log and 'start' not in scene.log


def test_live_admission_requires_private_channel_and_matching_current_proof(tmp_path):
    scene = Scene(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='live admission'):
        cutover.live_admission(None, scene.locked, 'd' * 64)
    parent, child = socket.socketpair(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as executor:
            task = executor.submit(cutover.live_admission, child.fileno(), scene.locked, 'd' * 64)
            import select
            assert select.select([parent], [], [], 2)[0]
            proof = {k: scene.locked[k] for k in ('old_ids', 'schema', 'baseline_image', 'first_cutover_sha256')}
            gate.answer_cutover_check(parent, dict(proof, state='held'))
            task.result(timeout=2)
        # A responder with old identities is not sufficient even if it knows the nonce.
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as executor:
            task = executor.submit(cutover.live_admission, child.fileno(), scene.locked, 'd' * 64)
            assert select.select([parent], [], [], 2)[0]
            gate.answer_cutover_check(parent, dict(proof, state='held', schema=125))
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='identity mismatch'):
                task.result(timeout=2)
    finally:
        parent.close(); child.close()


def test_control_plane_stop_is_unbounded_and_uses_ids(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    p = dict(plan(), staged_env='/test/staged.env', compose_files=['/test/compose.yml'])
    host = cutover.Host(p, tmp_path)
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(host, 'command', lambda *args: calls.append(args))
    host.stop(['1' * 64, '2' * 64])
    assert calls == [('docker', 'stop', '--time', '-1', '1' * 64, '2' * 64)]


@pytest.mark.parametrize('fault', ['none', 'unmounted', 'same_disk', 'space', 'busy', 'busy_timer', 'symlink', 'existing'])
def test_backup_storage_guard_refuses_unsafe_scene_before_any_stop(tmp_path, monkeypatch, fault):
    root = tmp_path / 'hdd'; root.mkdir()
    data = tmp_path / 'data'; data.mkdir()
    target = root / 'fresh'
    p = dict(plan(), backup_root=str(root), backup_dir=str(target), data_dir=str(data),
             staged_env='env', compose_files=[])
    host = cutover.Host(p, tmp_path)
    real_stat = Path.stat
    dev = root.stat().st_dev
    def disk_stat(path, *args, **kwargs):
        if path == Path('/backup'):
            return SimpleNamespace(st_dev=dev)
        if path == data:
            return SimpleNamespace(st_dev=dev if fault == 'same_disk' else dev + 1)
        return real_stat(path, *args, **kwargs)
    monkeypatch.setattr(Path, 'stat', disk_stat)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cutover.os.path, 'ismount', lambda p: fault != 'unmounted')
    monkeypatch.setattr(cutover.shutil, 'disk_usage', lambda p: SimpleNamespace(
        free=(127 if fault == 'space' else 129) * 1024**3))
    calls = []
    def command(*args):
        calls.append(args)
        assert args[:2] == ('systemctl', 'show')
        return 'activating\n' if fault == 'busy' or (fault == 'busy_timer' and args[2].endswith('.timer')) else 'inactive\n'
    monkeypatch.setattr(host, 'command', command)
    if fault == 'symlink':
        target.symlink_to(data, target_is_directory=True)
    elif fault == 'existing':
        target.mkdir()
    if fault == 'none':
        host.backup_preflight()
        assert len(calls) == 6
    else:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
            host.backup_preflight()
        # Recheck at the backup boundary too, before invoking its barrier.
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
            host.backup()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('failed', [False, True])
def test_compose_oneoff_compatibility_is_exercised_before_legacy_stop(tmp_path, monkeypatch, failed):
    p = dict(plan(), staged_env='env', compose_files=[])
    host = cutover.Host(p, tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(host, 'backup_preflight', lambda: None)
    services = {'migration': {'image': p['image']}}
    for key in cutover.UNITS:
        env = {'FACTORY_DELIVERY_WORKERS': '20', 'WORKER_CLUSTER_EXPECTED_COUNT': '3'}
        if key in ('executor-incremental', 'executor-stock'):
            env['WORKER_RUNTIME_ASYNC_SLOTS'] = str(p['capacities'][key.removeprefix('executor-')])
        services[key] = {'image': p['image'], 'container_name': p['units'][key],
                         'restart': 'unless-stopped', 'stop_grace_period': '24h0m0s', 'environment': env}
    calls = []
    def command(*args):
        calls.append(args)
        if args[:2] == ('docker', 'ps'):
            return ''
        if args[:3] == ('docker', 'image', 'inspect'):
            return json.dumps([{'Config': {'Env': ['LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA=' + p['source_sha']], 'Entrypoint': None}}])
        if 'config' in args:
            return json.dumps({'services': services})
        assert args[-7:] == ('run', '--rm', '--no-deps', 'auxiliary', 'python', '-c', 'pass')
        if failed:
            raise RuntimeError('installed compose cannot run this one-off')
        return ''
    monkeypatch.setattr(host, 'command', command)
    if failed:
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='one-off'):
            host.preflight()
    else:
        host.preflight()
    assert not any('stop' in cmd for cmd in calls)

                    {'backup_min_free_bytes': 1}, {'release_id': p['source_sha']}):

            cutover.validate(dict(p, **changes))


def test_reviewed_plan_file_is_bound_not_just_source_sha(tmp_path):
    path = tmp_path / 'first-cutover.json'
    path.write_text(json.dumps(plan()))

    assert cutover.checked_json(path, sha) == plan()
    changed = plan(); changed['capacities']['stock'] = 33
    path.write_text(json.dumps(changed))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='changed'):
        cutover.checked_json(path, sha)


def test_topology_health_requires_real_routes_and_fresh_workers(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    p = plan()
    units = {}
    for key, container in p['units'].items():
        if key.startswith('api-'):
            units[key] = {'kind': 'api', 'module': key.split('-')[1], 'address': key + ':8080', 'container': container}
        elif key.startswith('executor-'):
            units[key] = {'kind': 'executor', 'module': key.removeprefix('executor-'), 'container': container}
        else:
            units[key] = {'kind': 'singleton', 'module': 'common', 'role': key, 'container': container}
    template = dict(p, units=units)
    runtime = {'configured': True, 'unsupported': [],
        'pools': [{'pool': k, 'capacity': n, 'global_limit': 49, 'accepting': True, 'release_id': ''} for k, n in p['capacities'].items()],
        'workers': [{'pool': k, 'readiness': 'ready'} for k in p['capacities']]}
    (tmp_path / 'module_singleton_probe.py').write_text('# deterministic substitute\n')
    release = SimpleNamespace(__file__=str(tmp_path / 'module_release.py'),
        inspect=lambda _: {'running': True, 'image': p['image'], 'started_at': 'now'},
        runtime=lambda _: runtime, fresh_worker=lambda *args: True,
        singleton_health=lambda *args: True,
        command=lambda *args: json.dumps({'healthy': True, 'state': 'policy_disabled_dry_run'}))
    def response(url):
        if url.endswith('/module'):
            return {'module': url.split('-')[1], 'build_sha': p['source_sha'], 'draining': False}
        return {'status': 'serving', 'build': {'sha': p['source_sha']}}
    def http_status(url):
        owner = 'incremental' if 'automation/cards' in url else 'stock'
        return 401 if ('api-' + owner + '-') in url else 404
    monkeypatch.setattr(health, 'response', response)
    monkeypatch.setattr(health, 'http_status', http_status)
    report = health.verify(p, template, release)
    assert report['status'] == 'verified' and len(report['proofs']) == 12
    assert report['proofs']['optimization']['state'] == 'policy_disabled_dry_run'
    monkeypatch.setattr(health, 'http_status', lambda _: 404)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='routing/auth'):
        health.verify(p, template, release)
    monkeypatch.setattr(health, 'http_status', http_status)
    release.fresh_worker = lambda *args: False
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='stale or duplicated'):
        health.verify(p, template, release)


def test_inventory_probe_queries_real_table_and_column_names(monkeypatch, capsys):
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    import app.database
    from app.models import ProductionRun, BaselineRegressionItem, WorkflowModelInvocation, RuntimeOutboxEvent
    engine = create_engine('sqlite://')
    tables = [ProductionRun, BaselineRegressionItem, WorkflowModelInvocation, RuntimeOutboxEvent]
    with engine.begin() as con:
        con.execute(text('CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (version INTEGER)'))
        con.execute(text('INSERT INTO schema_migrations VALUES (126)'))
        for model in tables:
            cols = ['id', 'status'] + (['source_type', 'run_mode'] if model is ProductionRun else [])
            assert all(c in model.__table__.c for c in cols)
            con.execute(text('CREATE TABLE ' + model.__tablename__ + ' (' + ','.join(c+' TEXT' for c in cols) + ')'))
            con.execute(text('INSERT INTO ' + model.__tablename__ + ' (id,status) VALUES (1,\'succeeded\')'))
    class ReadSession(Session):
        def execute(self, statement, *args, **kwargs):
            if str(statement) == 'SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY':
                return None  # SQLite has no such command; all other SQL executes.
            assert str(statement).startswith('SELECT ')
            return super().execute(statement, *args, **kwargs)
    monkeypatch.setattr(app.database, 'SessionLocal', lambda: ReadSession(engine))
    exec(cutover.STATE_PROBE, {})
    facts = json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
    assert facts['schema'] == 126
    assert set(facts['business']) == {m.__tablename__ for m in tables}


window = load('deploy/server/first_module_window.py', 'window')
activation = load('deploy/server/first_module_activate.py', 'activation')


class WindowScene:
    def __init__(self, root, failure=None):
        self.root, self.failure, self.log = root, failure, []
    def step(self, name):
        self.log.append(name)
        if name == self.failure:
            raise RuntimeError(name)
        return True
    def operations(self): return self.step('operations')
    def pause(self): return self.step('pause')
    def intake_idle(self): return self.step('intake_idle')
    def hold(self): return self.step('hold')
    def idle_proof_ready(self): return self.step('idle_proof_ready')
    def release(self):
        (self.root / 'deployment-started').write_text('started')
        return self.step('release')
    def before_stop(self): return not (self.root / 'deployment-started').exists()
    def resume(self, legacy=False):
        self.step('resume_legacy' if legacy else 'resume')
        return {'resumed_count': 7, 'failed_count': 0}
    def start_intake(self): return self.step('start_intake')
    def intake_seen(self): return self.step('intake_seen')


def test_window_resumes_and_requires_new_intake_before_completion(tmp_path):
    scene = WindowScene(tmp_path)
    receipt = window.execute(tmp_path, scene)
    assert receipt['status'] == 'completed_pending_browser_acceptance'
    assert scene.log == ['operations', 'pause', 'intake_idle', 'hold', 'idle_proof_ready', 'release', 'resume',
                         'start_intake', 'intake_seen', 'operations']
    before = scene.log.copy()
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='already attempted'):
        window.execute(tmp_path, scene)
    assert before == scene.log


@pytest.mark.parametrize('phase', ['pause', 'intake_idle', 'hold', 'idle_proof_ready', 'release', 'resume', 'intake_seen'])
def test_window_failures_restore_only_before_first_stop(tmp_path, phase):
    scene = WindowScene(tmp_path, phase)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match=phase):
        window.execute(tmp_path, scene)
    receipt = json.loads((tmp_path / 'first-window-receipt.json').read_text())
    if phase in ('pause', 'intake_idle', 'hold', 'idle_proof_ready'):
        assert scene.log[-2:] == ['resume_legacy', 'start_intake']
        assert receipt['status'] == 'failed_before_stop_resumed_intake_unverified'
    else:
        assert 'resume_legacy' not in scene.log
        assert receipt['status'] == 'needs_forward_recovery'


def test_window_does_not_enter_gate_until_post_settlement_idle_proof(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    scene = WindowScene(tmp_path)
    samples = iter([False, False, True])
    def idle():
        scene.log.append('idle_proof_ready')
        return next(samples)
    scene.idle_proof_ready = idle
    monkeypatch.setattr(window.time, 'sleep', lambda _: None)
    window.execute(tmp_path, scene)
    assert scene.log.index('release') == scene.log.index('hold') + 4
    assert scene.log.count('idle_proof_ready') == 3


def test_idle_probe_preserves_the_reviewed_gate_and_propagates_technical_errors(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    p = plan()
    host = window.WindowHost(tmp_path, p, 'hash')
    source = '# exact reviewed gate source'
    (tmp_path / 'stop-admission-base.py').write_text(source)
    codes = iter([75, 0, 1]); calls = []
    def invoke(args, **kwargs):
        calls.append((args, kwargs))
        return SimpleNamespace(returncode=next(codes))
    monkeypatch.setattr(window.subprocess, 'run', invoke)
    assert host.idle_proof_ready() is False
    assert host.idle_proof_ready() is True
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='probe failed'):
        host.idle_proof_ready()
    assert all(c[1]['input'] == source for c in calls)
    assert all(c[0][-2:] == ['7', '126'] for c in calls)
    assert 'SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY' in window.IDLE_PROBE
    assert "namespace['assert_idle']" in window.IDLE_PROBE
    assert 'acquire(' not in window.IDLE_PROBE


@pytest.mark.parametrize('outcome', ['ready', 'stale', 'database_error'])
def test_idle_probe_runs_read_only_and_always_releases_its_connection(monkeypatch, outcome):
    import io
    import sys
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    import app.database
    events = []
    class Raw:
        def cursor(self): return self
        def execute(self, statement):
            events.append(statement)
            assert statement == 'SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY'
        def rollback(self): events.append('rollback')
        def close(self): events.append('close')
    raw = Raw()
    monkeypatch.setattr(app.database, 'engine', SimpleNamespace(raw_connection=lambda: raw))
    source = "def assert_idle(raw, owners, schema):\n assert (owners,schema)==(7,126)\n"
    if outcome == 'stale':
        source += " raise AssertionError('idle proof predates runtime settlement')\n"
    elif outcome == 'database_error':
        source += " raise RuntimeError('database unavailable')\n"
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, 'stdin', io.StringIO(source))
    monkeypatch.setattr(sys, 'argv', ['probe', '7', '126'])
    if outcome == 'stale':
        with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as caught:
            exec(window.IDLE_PROBE, {})
        assert caught.value.code == 75
    elif outcome == 'database_error':
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='database unavailable'):
            exec(window.IDLE_PROBE, {})
    else:
        exec(window.IDLE_PROBE, {})
    assert events == ['SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY', 'rollback', 'close']

        window.execute(tmp_path, scene)
    assert 'start_intake' not in scene.log
    receipt = json.loads((tmp_path / 'first-window-receipt.json').read_text())
    assert receipt['resume']['failed_count'] == 1


def test_window_uses_unique_hold_marker_for_both_hold_and_resume(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    p = plan(); host = window.WindowHost(tmp_path, p, 'hash')
    commands = []
    def run(args, **kwargs):
        commands.append(args)
        return SimpleNamespace(returncode=0)
    monkeypatch.setattr(window.subprocess, 'run', run)
    def command(*args):
        commands.append(args)
        if args[:2] == ('docker', 'inspect'):
            return json.dumps([{'Image':p['old_image'], 'State':{'Running':True}}])
        return '{"failed_count":0}'
    monkeypatch.setattr(host, 'command', command)
    assert host.hold()
    host.resume(legacy=True)
    hold_resume = [c for c in commands if '--release' in c]
    assert len(hold_resume) == 2
    assert all(c[c.index('--release') + 1] == p['release_id'] for c in hold_resume)
    assert all(p['source_sha'] not in c for c in hold_resume)

        calls.append(args)
        return outputs.pop(0)

    assert all('--after-cursor' in c and 'current-cursor' in c for c in calls)


def test_atomic_install_never_follows_symlink_or_overwrites_unexplained_staging(tmp_path):
    source = tmp_path / 'source'; source.write_text('new')
    target = tmp_path / 'target'; target.write_text('old')
    link = tmp_path / 'link'; link.symlink_to(target)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='symbolic'):
        activation.install(source, link, 0o600)
    assert target.read_text() == 'old'
    pending = tmp_path / 'target.first-module-pending'; pending.write_text('unexplained')
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        activation.install(source, target, 0o600)
    assert target.read_text() == 'old'
    pending.unlink()
    activation.install(source, target, 0o600)
    assert target.read_text() == 'new' and target.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o600


def activation_scene(tmp_path, monkeypatch, failure=None):
    import hashlib
    p = plan()
    for key in ('installed_env', 'nginx_site', 'intake_guard', 'intake_unit', 'staged_env'):
        file = tmp_path / key
        file.write_text("'3d66-label-system-server'" if key == 'intake_guard' else key)
        p[key] = str(file)
    p.update(frontend_source=str(tmp_path / 'static'), frontend_revision='frontend-sha',
             previous_frontend_source=str(tmp_path / 'old-static'), previous_frontend_revision='old-frontend')
    raw = json.dumps(p); (tmp_path / 'first-cutover.json').write_text(raw)
    (tmp_path / 'first-cutover-receipt.json').write_text(json.dumps({
        'status': 'topology_ready', 'source_sha': p['source_sha'],
        'plan_sha256': hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()}))
    for name in ('nginx-upstreams.conf', 'nginx-proxy.conf', 'nginx.site'):
        (tmp_path / name).write_text(name)
    calls = []
    def run(*args):
        calls.append(args)
        if failure and failure(args):
            raise RuntimeError('injected activation failure')
        if args[:2] == ('systemctl', 'show'):
            return 'inactive'
        if any('publish_frontend.py' in arg for arg in args):
            return '{"status":"published"}'
        return ''
    original_install = activation.install
    def install(source, target, mode):
        calls.append(('install', str(target)))
        if str(target).startswith('/etc/nginx/snippets/'):
            return  # only real fixed host paths are replaced by deterministic doubles
        return original_install(source, target, mode)
    import io
    monkeypatch.setattr(activation, 'run', run)
    monkeypatch.setattr(activation, 'install', install)
    monkeypatch.setattr(activation.urllib.request, 'urlopen', lambda *a, **k:
        io.BytesIO(json.dumps({'status':'serving','build':{'sha':p['source_sha']}}).encode()))
    return p, calls


def test_activation_checks_installed_nginx_before_reload_and_repins_without_resuming(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    p, calls = activation_scene(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    report = activation.activate(tmp_path, p)
    assert report['status'] == 'activated_pending_resume' and not report['production_accepted']
    assert calls.index(('nginx','-t')) < calls.index(('systemctl','reload','nginx'))
    assert Path(p['installed_env']).read_text() == 'staged_env'
    assert Path(p['intake_guard']).read_text() == "'3d66-label-system-server-auxiliary'"
    assert (tmp_path / 'before-installed_env').read_text() == 'installed_env'
    assert any('--image' in call and p['image'] in call and '--container' in call for call in calls)
    assert not any(call[:2] in [('docker','stop'),('systemctl','start')] for call in calls)
    count = len(calls)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='already attempted'):
        activation.activate(tmp_path, p)
    assert len(calls) == count


def test_activation_invalid_nginx_preserves_scene_and_does_not_reload_or_repin(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    p, calls = activation_scene(tmp_path, monkeypatch, lambda args: args==('nginx','-t'))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match='injected activation'):
        activation.activate(tmp_path, p)
    report = json.loads((tmp_path / 'first-activation-receipt.json').read_text())
    assert report['status'] == 'needs_forward_recovery'
    assert ('systemctl','reload','nginx') not in calls
    assert not any('--image' in call for call in calls)
    assert Path(p['installed_env']).read_text() == 'installed_env'
    assert (tmp_path / 'before-nginx_site').read_text() == 'nginx_site'


builder = load('deploy/server/build_first_module_entry.py', 'entry_builder')


def test_entry_builder_binds_all_inputs_and_pending_acceptance_never_reaches_gate(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    import subprocess, os, hashlib
    p = plan();root = tmp_path / p['source_sha'];root.mkdir()
    installed = {}
    for name in builder.INSTALLED:
        path = tmp_path / name;path.write_text(name);installed[name]=str(path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(builder, 'INSTALLED', installed)
    prepared = {'status':'prepared_only','source_sha':p['source_sha'],'image':p['image'],
        'old_image':p['old_image'],'capacities_proposed_not_yet_validated':p['capacities'],
        'preparation_spec':{'archive_sha256':'a'*64},'previous_frontend_revision':'b'*40}
    (root/'prepared.json').write_text(json.dumps(prepared))
    compose = root/'compose.yml';compose.write_text('services: {}')
    env=root/'runtime.env.staging';env.write_text('REFERENCES_ONLY=1\n')
    template={'source_sha':p['source_sha'],'image':p['image'],'units':{k:{'container':v} for k,v in p['units'].items()},
        'compose_files':[str(compose)],'env_file':str(env)}
    (root/'manifest-template.json').write_text(json.dumps(template))
    for name in ('nginx.site','nginx-upstreams.conf','nginx-proxy.conf'):
        (root/name).write_text('nginx')
    for name in ('scripts/module_release.py','scripts/module_singleton_probe.py',
                 'scripts/publish_frontend.py','deploy/release/repin-intake.py','deploy/server/backup.sh',
                 'backend/app/server_backup.py','backend/app/server_runtime.py'):
        path=root/'source'/name;path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);path.write_text('# immutable test input')
    frontend=tmp_path/'frontend';(frontend/'assets').mkdir(parents=True)
    (frontend/'index.html').write_text('<div id="root"></div>');(frontend/'assets/app.js').write_text('console.log(1)')
    old=root/'previous-frontend';(old/'assets').mkdir(parents=True)
    (old/'index.html').write_text('old');(old/'assets/old.js').write_text('old')
    acceptance=tmp_path/'acceptance.json';acceptance.write_text('{"status":"pending"}')
    receipt=builder.build(root,acceptance,frontend,'e'*40,ROOT/'deploy/server')
    assert receipt['status']=='bound_not_authorized' and receipt['acceptance_status']=='pending'
    bound=json.loads((root/'first-cutover.json').read_text())
    assert bound['backup_dir'].startswith('/backup/production/pre-module-')
    assert bound['release_id'] != bound['source_sha']
    cutover.validate(bound)
    assert bound['frontend_manifest']==builder.regular_tree(frontend)
    assert bound['artifacts'][str(compose)]==builder.sha(compose)
    assert bound['artifacts'][str(root/'gated-acquisition.py')]==builder.sha(ROOT/'deploy/server/release-ops/gated-acquisition.py')
    subprocess.run(['bash','-n',str(root/'deploy-reviewed.sh')],check=True)
    result=subprocess.run(['bash',str(root/'deploy-reviewed.sh')],env={**os.environ,'RELEASE_DIR':str(root)},capture_output=True,text=True)
    assert result.returncode!=0 and 'AssertionError' in result.stderr
    assert not (root/'admission-locked.json').exists() and not (root/'deployment-started').exists()
    # Even changing an otherwise valid compose before a pending receipt can be
    # inspected fails at the file gate. No Docker command is reached.
    compose.write_text('changed')
    changed=subprocess.run(['bash',str(root/'deploy-reviewed.sh')],env={**os.environ,'RELEASE_DIR':str(root)},capture_output=True,text=True)
    assert changed.returncode!=0 and 'FAILED' in changed.stdout
    with pytest.raises(ValueError,match='overwrite'):
        builder.build(root,acceptance,frontend,'e'*40,ROOT/'deploy/server')

===== FILE backend/tests/test_formal_switch_rename.py =====
"""灰度转正式改名：只改名字，不改身份（评审文档 2026-09-17 第 7 节、用例 5）。

改名那轮最大的风险不是漏改一处文案，而是顺手把身份键也"正式化"了——
``factory-test-material`` 里的 test 是历史遗留，却是素材身份与冻结输入的判定键；
上游 ``prod-task:`` 前缀是结算找 job 的依据。动了它们，台账与下游幂等键会断，
同一素材会被当成新素材再跑一遍、再投一次。这里把两边都钉死。
"""
import inspect
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
# 镜像内只装了后端与 scripts/deploy，前端源码不在里面；那部分由
# `npm run contract:incremental-routing` 守。
FRONTEND = REPO / "frontend/src"
needs_frontend = pytest.mark.skipif(not FRONTEND.is_dir(), reason="镜像内没有前端源码")


def test_identity_keys_survive_the_rename():
    """不可改的身份：素材来源、上游标识与 job 前缀、绑定的定义与合同。"""
    from app import factory_material_intake, factory_test_claim, incremental_input

    assert "factory-test-material" in inspect.getsource(factory_material_intake), "素材身份来源被改名，台账与冻结输入判定会断"
    assert "factory-test-material" in inspect.getsource(incremental_input), "冻结输入的素材身份前缀被改名"
    upstream = inspect.getsource(factory_test_claim)
    assert "'factory-prod', 'prod-task:', 'prod-task:'" in upstream, "上游标识或 job 前缀被改名，_settle_allowance 找不到 job"


def test_intake_error_codes_are_renamed_off_gray():
    """错误码 factory_gray_* -> factory_intake_*，旧码不得残留。"""
    from app import factory_material_intake

    source = inspect.getsource(factory_material_intake)
    assert "factory_gray_" not in source
    for code in ("factory_intake_limit_invalid",
                 "factory_intake_existing_tasks_exceed_limit",
                 "factory_intake_placeholder_journal_refused"):
        assert code in source, code


def test_automation_card_reports_routing_status_not_gray_status():
    """API 字段 gray_status -> routing_status，值域不变；前端类型同步。"""
    from app import incremental_automation

    source = inspect.getsource(incremental_automation)
    assert '"routing_status"' in source and '"gray_status"' not in source


@needs_frontend
def test_frontend_type_follows_the_renamed_field():
    types = (FRONTEND / "lib/types.ts").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert 'routing_status: "shadow" | "split" | "not_configured"' in types
    assert "gray_status" not in types


def test_intake_pin_points_at_the_formal_directory():
    """进料钉子跟着宿主目录改名走；单元名与挂载父目录不变。"""
    repin = (REPO / "deploy/release/repin-intake.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "/production-data/factory-prod-material/guard.py" in repin
    assert "factory-prod-gray-20260915" not in repin
    assert "/etc/systemd/system/factory-prod-material.service" in repin


def test_first_gray_command_is_retired():
    """首次灰度专用命令退役；生产进料走 factory-material-stream.py。"""
    assert not (REPO / "scripts/factory-material-gray.py").exists()
    assert (REPO / "scripts/factory-material-stream.py").exists()


@needs_frontend
def test_no_gray_wording_left_in_incremental_console():
    """增量控制台不再出现"灰度"字样（影子/分流/路由是正式术语）。"""
    panels = FRONTEND / "features/incremental-automation"
    for path in sorted(panels.glob("*.tsx")):
        assert "灰度" not in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), path.name
    assert not (panels / "gray-control-panel.tsx").exists()
    assert (panels / "routing-control-panel.tsx").exists()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_golden_regression.py =====
import json

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    HumanReview,
    PromptRegressionRun,
    PromptVersion,
    User,
)


DIMENSIONS = {
    "composition_viewpoint": {"grade": 3},
    "lighting_atmosphere": {"grade": 4},
    "color_material": {"grade": 3},
    "spatial_design_furnishing": {"grade": 4},
    "visual_hierarchy": {"grade": 3},
    "detail_completion": {"grade": 4},
    "inspiration_reference": {"grade": 3},
    "presentation_integrity": {"grade": 4},
}


def test_golden_set_locks_runs_and_preserves_history() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(
        username="tester",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="测试员",
        is_admin=True,
        role="admin",
    )
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="golden.jpg",
        stored_name="golden.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        width=1200,
        height=800,
        sha256="g" * 64,
        status="evaluated",
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A", name="预检", version="A1", system_prompt="s", user_prompt="u", status="published"
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B", name="美感", version="B1", system_prompt="s", user_prompt="u", status="published"
    )
    db.add_all([user, asset, prompt_a, prompt_b])
    db.flush()
    job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset.id, prompt_a_id=prompt_a.id, prompt_b_id=prompt_b.id, status="completed")
    db.add(job)
    db.flush()
    result = EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        job_id=job.id,
        precheck_json=json.dumps(
            {
                "classification": {"primary_category": "住宅设计"},
                "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "moderate"},
                "media_form": {
                    "real_photo": {"status": "yes"},
                    "professional_photography": {"status": "no"},
                },
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        ),
        aesthetic_json=json.dumps({"dimensions": DIMENSIONS}, ensure_ascii=False),
        scoring_json="{}",
        raw_response_a="{}",
        raw_response_b="{}",
        score=72,
        level="L3",
        confidence=0.9,
        needs_review=False,
        model_id="doubao-2.0",
        prompt_a_version="A1",
        prompt_b_version="B1",
        rubric_version="R1",
        engine_version="E1",
    )
    db.add(result)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        HumanReview(
            evaluation_id=result.id,
            reviewer_name="审核员",
            decision="corrected",
            corrected_level="L2",
            note="画质问题导致等级偏高",
            corrections_json=json.dumps(
                [
                    {
                        "target_type": "dimension",
                        "field_key": "lighting_atmosphere",
                        "model_value": 4,
                        "human_value": 2,
                        "reason_codes": ["quality_issue"],
                        "note": "过曝影响氛围",
                    }
                ],
                ensure_ascii=False,
            ),
        )
    )
    db.commit()

    def test_db():
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = test_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        created = client.post(
            "/api/sample-sets",
            json={"name": "黄金回归集", "description": "发布门槛", "kind": "golden"},
        )
        assert created.status_code == 200
        sample_set_id = created.json()["id"]
        added = client.post(
            f"/api/sample-sets/{sample_set_id}/items", json={"asset_ids": [asset.id]}
        )
        assert added.status_code == 200

        detail = client.get(f"/api/sample-sets/{sample_set_id}").json()
        item = detail["items"][0]
        assert detail["summary"]["kind"] == "golden"
        assert item["truth"]["level"] == "L2"
        assert item["truth"]["quality_severity"] == "moderate"
        assert item["truth"]["dimensions"]["lighting_atmosphere"] == 2
        assert len(item["truth"]["dimensions"]) == 8

        locked = client.patch(
            f"/api/sample-sets/{sample_set_id}/status", json={"status": "locked"}
        )
        assert locked.status_code == 200
        run = client.post(
            "/api/prompt-regressions", json={"sample_set_id": sample_set_id}
        )
        assert run.status_code == 200
        run_id = run.json()["ids"][0]
        regression = db.get(PromptRegressionRun, run_id)
        assert regression is not None
        assert regression.total == 1
        assert regression.items[0].job_id is not None

        history = client.get(
            f"/api/sample-sets/{sample_set_id}/items/{item['id']}/history"
        ).json()
        assert len(history["evaluations"]) == 1
        assert history["evaluations"][0]["reviews"][0]["note"] == "画质问题导致等级偏高"
        assert history["truth_revisions"][0]["revision"] == 1
        assert history["regressions"][0]["run_id"] == run_id

        prompt_b2 = PromptVersion(
            stage="B", name="美感", version="B2", system_prompt="s2", user_prompt="u2", status="draft"
        )
        db.add(prompt_b2)
        db.commit()
        published = client.post(f"/api/prompts/{prompt_b2.id}/publish")
        assert published.status_code == 200
        listed_prompt = next(
            item
            for item in client.get("/api/prompts").json()["items"]
            if item["id"] == prompt_b2.id
        )
        assert listed_prompt["updated_at"]
        assert published.json()["regression_run_ids"] == []
        assert db.scalar(
            select(PromptRegressionRun).where(
                PromptRegressionRun.trigger_prompt_id == prompt_b2.id
            )
        ) is None
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_legacy_prompt_publish_requires_admin_and_isolated_from_global_regression() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    admin = User(username="admin", password_hash="unused", display_name="管理员", is_admin=True, role="admin")
    reviewer = User(username="reviewer", password_hash="unused", display_name="审核员", is_admin=False, role="reviewer")
    bound_prompt = PromptVersion(
        stage="A", name="类目基线", version="bound-a", system_prompt="绑定类目 system prompt",
        user_prompt="绑定类目 user prompt", rubric_version="R1", status="published"
    )
    prompt = PromptVersion(
        stage="A", name="手工", version="legacy-a", system_prompt="完整手工 system prompt 文本",
        user_prompt="请返回结构化结果", rubric_version="R1", status="draft"
    )
    bound_draft = PromptVersion(
        stage="B", name="绑定草稿", version="bound-draft-b", system_prompt="绑定草稿 system prompt",
        user_prompt="绑定草稿 user prompt", rubric_version="R1", status="draft"
    )
    rollback_current = PromptVersion(
        stage="A", name="待回滚版本", version="rollback-current-a", system_prompt="待回滚 system prompt",
        user_prompt="待回滚 user prompt", rubric_version="R1", status="published"
    )
    db.add_all([admin, reviewer, bound_prompt, prompt, bound_draft, rollback_current])
    db.flush()
    rollback_current.rollback_prompt_id = bound_prompt.id
    db.add_all(
        [
            EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                category_key="space_image",
                display_name="空间图",
                status="active",
                prompt_a_id=bound_prompt.id,
            ),
            EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                category_key="material_image",
                display_name="材质图",
                status="active",
                prompt_b_id=bound_draft.id,
            ),
        ]
    )
    db.commit()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db
    try:
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: reviewer
        denied = TestClient(app).post(f"/api/prompts/{prompt.id}/publish")
