        assert denied.status_code == 403
        denied_rollback = TestClient(app).post(
            f"/api/prompts/{rollback_current.id}/rollback"
        )
        assert denied_rollback.status_code == 403
        app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: admin
        published = TestClient(app).post(f"/api/prompts/{prompt.id}/publish")
        assert published.status_code == 200
        assert published.json()["regression_run_ids"] == []
        assert db.scalar(
            select(PromptRegressionRun).where(
                PromptRegressionRun.trigger_prompt_id == prompt.id
            )
        ) is None
        assert bound_prompt.status == "published"

        bound_publish = TestClient(app).post(
            f"/api/prompts/{bound_draft.id}/publish"
        )
        assert bound_publish.status_code == 409
        assert "活动类目" in bound_publish.text
        assert bound_draft.status == "draft"

        bound_rollback = TestClient(app).post(
            f"/api/prompts/{rollback_current.id}/rollback"
        )
        assert bound_rollback.status_code == 409
        assert "活动类目" in bound_rollback.text
        assert rollback_current.status == "published"
        assert bound_prompt.status == "published"
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_health_liveness.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient


def test_liveness_endpoint_does_not_build_database_or_worker_snapshot(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app import main as main_module

    def fail_if_called(*_args, **_kwargs):
        raise AssertionError("liveness must not query database or worker state")

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        main_module,
        "_runtime_health_snapshot",
        fail_if_called,
    )

    response = TestClient(main_module.app).get("/api/health/live")

    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json() == {
        "status": "ok",
        "service": "3d66-label-system",
        "build": {
            "sha": main_module.os.getenv("LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA", "dev").strip()
            or "dev"
        },
    }

===== FILE backend/tests/test_historical_corrections.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import zipfile

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.historical_corrections import preview_historical_workbooks
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import User


def _xlsx(rows: list[list[object]]) -> bytes:
    def cell(ref: str, value: object) -> str:
        text = (
            str(value).replace("&", "&amp;").replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;")
        )
        return f'<c r="{ref}" t="inlineStr"><is><t>{text}</t></is></c>'

    sheet_rows = []
    for row_number, values in enumerate(rows, start=1):
        cells = "".join(
            cell(f"{chr(64 + column)}{row_number}", value)
            for column, value in enumerate(values, start=1)
        )
        sheet_rows.append(f'<row r="{row_number}">{cells}</row>')
    entries = {
        "[Content_Types].xml": (
            '<?xml version="1.0"?><Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/'
            'package/2006/content-types"><Override PartName="/xl/workbook.xml" '
            'ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.'
            'spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml"/><Override '
            'PartName="/xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.'
            'openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml"/></Types>'
        ),
        "xl/workbook.xml": (
            '<?xml version="1.0"?><workbook xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/'
            'spreadsheetml/2006/main" xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/'
            'officeDocument/2006/relationships"><sheets><sheet name="reviewed" '
            'sheetId="1" r:id="rId1"/></sheets></workbook>'
        ),
        "xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels": (
            '<?xml version="1.0"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.'
            'openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" '
            'Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/'
            'relationships/worksheet" Target="worksheets/sheet1.xml"/></Relationships>'
        ),
        "xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml": (
            '<?xml version="1.0"?><worksheet xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/'
            'spreadsheetml/2006/main"><sheetData>'
            + "".join(sheet_rows)
            + "</sheetData></worksheet>"
        ),
    }
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buffer, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
        for name, value in entries.items():
            archive.writestr(name, value.encode())
    return buffer.getvalue()


def test_preview_maps_owner_confirmed_corrections_without_forming_gold() -> None:
    payload = _xlsx(
        [
            ["llid", "评测等级", "豆包等级", "hwreason"],
            ["a-1", "L2", "L4", "模型高估材质"],
            ["a-2", "L3", "L3", ""],
        ]
    )
    preview = preview_historical_workbooks(
        [("3D1.xlsx", payload)], blind_holdout_ratio=0
    )
    assert preview["writes_business_database"] is False
    assert preview["downloads_performed"] is False
    assert preview["model_runs_performed"] is False
    assert preview["forms_gold"] is False
    assert preview["summary"]["unique_item_count"] == 2
    by_key = {item["dedupe_key"]: item for item in preview["items"]}
    corrected = by_key["business:3d:a-1"]
    assert corrected["sample_role"] == "target_error"
    assert corrected["correction_candidate"]["human_level"] == "L2"
    assert corrected["correction_candidate"]["reason"] == "模型高估材质"
    assert corrected["provenance"]["owner_confirmed"] is True
    assert len(corrected["provenance"]["source_row_sha256"]) == 64
    assert by_key["business:3d:a-2"]["sample_role"] == "stable_control"


def test_3dreason_is_reason_only_and_never_invents_final_level() -> None:
    preview = preview_historical_workbooks(
        [
            (
                "3Dreason样本.xlsx",
                _xlsx(
                    [
                        ["llid", "豆包等级", "reason"],
                        ["reason-1", "L5", "光感判断错误"],
                    ]
                ),
            )
        ],
        blind_holdout_ratio=0,
    )
    item = preview["items"][0]
    assert item["sample_role"] == "reason_only"
    assert item["correction_candidate"]["human_level"] is None
    assert item["correction_candidate"]["model_level"] == "L5"
    assert item["forms_gold"] is False


def test_preview_deduplicates_stably_and_api_requires_authentication() -> None:
    payload = _xlsx(
        [["img_id", "评测等级"], ["same", "L3"], ["same", "L3"]]
    )
    first = preview_historical_workbooks(
        [("SU测评1.xlsx", payload)], blind_holdout_ratio=0.2
    )
    second = preview_historical_workbooks(
        [("SU测评1.xlsx", payload)], blind_holdout_ratio=0.2
    )
    assert first == second
    assert first["summary"]["unique_item_count"] == 1
    assert first["summary"]["duplicate_count"] == 1

    client = TestClient(app)
    unauthenticated = client.post(
        "/api/historical-corrections/preview",
        files={"files": ("SU测评1.xlsx", payload)},
    )
    assert unauthenticated.status_code == 401
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: User(
        id=1,
        username="owner",
        password_hash="unused",
        display_name="Owner",
    )
    try:
        authenticated = client.post(
            "/api/historical-corrections/preview",
            files={"files": ("SU测评1.xlsx", payload)},
        )
        assert authenticated.status_code == 200, authenticated.text
        assert authenticated.json()["summary"]["unique_item_count"] == 1
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_historical_source_correction.py =====
"""Historical source correction remains durable without a new publication."""
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.models import ContentRecord, EvaluationResult, ProductionRun, RuntimeOutboxEvent, WorkflowVersion
from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission
from app.workflow_correction import append_node_correction_and_replay
from app.workflow_result_contract import hash_manifest
from app.workflow_runtime import WorkflowRuntimeError
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close, _headers, _payload
from test_workflow_correction import _create_level_native_run


@pytest.fixture(params=['unmanaged', 'formal'])
def completed_source_run(monkeypatch, request):
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        if request.param == 'formal':
            scope = dict(category_key='space_image',
                         workflow_definition_id=db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id)
            policy = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode='split', ratio_percent=100,
                ownership_contract='historical-correction-test', reason='test', actor='operator')
            set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=policy['revision'], paused=False, reason='test', actor='operator')
            db.commit()
        response = client.post('/api/content-ingress/events', headers=_headers(monkeypatch),
                               json=_payload(event_id='historical-correction', asset_id=asset.id))
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        ingress_run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(
            ProductionRun.run_key == response.json()['production_run_key']))
        source_snapshot = json.loads(ingress_run.snapshot_json)
        # Run the native correction fixture deterministically before attaching the
        # real admitted source's frozen identity and connector to its result.
        ingress_run.status = 'blocked'
        from app.models import ProductionStepAttempt
        for step in db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == ingress_run.id)):
            step.status = 'blocked'
        _, run_id, _ = _create_level_native_run(db)
        db.commit()
        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, 'SessionLocal', sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False))
        while executor.process_runtime_step_once('historical-correction-fixture'):
            pass
        db.expire_all()
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
        assert run.status == 'succeeded'
        evaluation = db.scalar(select(EvaluationResult).where(EvaluationResult.production_run_id == run.id))
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        snapshot['runtime_context'] = source_snapshot['runtime_context']
        if request.param == 'formal':
            assert snapshot['runtime_context']['incremental_route']['destination'] == 'formal'
            run.workflow_definition_id = ingress_run.workflow_definition_id
            snapshot['workflow']['workflow_definition_id'] = run.workflow_definition_id
        source_node = next(n for n in source_snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'] if n['kind'] == 'downstream_projection')
        target_node = next(n for n in snapshot['workflow']['manifest']['nodes'] if n['kind'] == 'downstream_projection')
        target_node['config'] = source_node['config']
        run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
        run.snapshot_hash = hash_manifest(snapshot)
        run.source_type, run.source_id = ingress_run.source_type, ingress_run.source_id
        evaluation.workflow_snapshot_hash = run.snapshot_hash
        db.commit()
        yield db, run, evaluation
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def correct(db, run):
    return append_node_correction_and_replay(db, run_id=run.id,
        node_instance_id='prompt_c', field_path='level', value='L1',
        reason='人工确认源版本等级', actor='operator')


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change', ['new_version', 'deleted'])
def test_historical_source_correction_is_saved_without_publication(completed_source_run, change):
    db, run, evaluation = completed_source_run
    record = db.scalar(select(ContentRecord))
    if change == 'deleted':
        record.status = 'deleted'
    else:
        record.source_version = 'v2'
    db.commit()
    before = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent))
    replay = correct(db, run)
    assert replay.downstream_publish_status == 'superseded_source_not_published'
    assert replay.source_version == 'v1'
    db.commit()
    db.expire_all()
    history = json.loads(db.get(EvaluationResult, evaluation.id).correction_history_json)
    assert history[-1]['correction_key'] == replay.event_id
    assert history[-1]['new_value'] == 'L1'
    assert history[-1]['downstream_publish_status'] == 'superseded_source_not_published'
    assert not history[-1].get('downstream_republish_event_ids')
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)) == before


def test_current_source_correction_still_publishes_reviewed(completed_source_run):
    db, run, evaluation = completed_source_run
    replay = correct(db, run)
    assert replay.downstream_publish_status == 'queued'
    db.commit()
    history = json.loads(evaluation.correction_history_json)
    ids = history[-1]['downstream_republish_event_ids']
    assert len(ids) == 1
    assert db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, ids[0]).review_state == 'reviewed'


def test_correction_publication_view_tracks_actual_delivery(completed_source_run):
    from app.correction_publication import correction_publication_state
    db, run, evaluation = completed_source_run
    correct(db, run)
    db.commit()
    publication = correction_publication_state(db, evaluation)
    assert publication['source_version'] == 'v1'
    assert publication['status'] == 'queued'
    event = db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, publication['event_ids'][0])
    event.status = 'delivered'
    db.flush()
    assert correction_publication_state(db, evaluation)['status'] == 'delivered'
    event.status = 'dead_lettered'
    db.flush()
    assert correction_publication_state(db, evaluation)['status'] == 'failed'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('historical', [False, True])
def test_malformed_source_evidence_is_not_treated_as_historical(completed_source_run, historical):
    db, run, evaluation = completed_source_run
    if historical:
        db.scalar(select(ContentRecord)).source_version = 'v2'
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    snapshot['runtime_context']['input_manifest']['source_version'] = 'forged'
    run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
    run.snapshot_hash = hash_manifest(snapshot)
    evaluation.workflow_snapshot_hash = run.snapshot_hash
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as raised:
        correct(db, run)
    assert raised.value.code == 'incremental_source_mismatch'
    db.rollback()
    assert json.loads(evaluation.correction_history_json) == []


def test_historical_source_does_not_hide_invalid_route(completed_source_run):
    db, run, evaluation = completed_source_run
    db.scalar(select(ContentRecord)).source_version = 'v2'
    snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
    snapshot['runtime_context']['incremental_route'] = {'assignment_id': 999}
    run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
    run.snapshot_hash = hash_manifest(snapshot)
    evaluation.workflow_snapshot_hash = run.snapshot_hash
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as raised:
        correct(db, run)
    assert raised.value.code == 'incremental_route_mismatch'
    db.rollback()
    assert json.loads(evaluation.correction_history_json) == []

===== FILE backend/tests/test_host_observer.py =====
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

path = Path(__file__).parents[2] / 'deploy/server/host_observer.py'
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('host_observer', path)
observer = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(observer)


def test_missing_heartbeat_and_low_memory_are_not_reported_healthy():
    metric = {'memory': {'MemAvailable': 4 * 1024**3}}
    assert observer.health(10, metric, now=30)['status'] == 'observed'
    assert observer.health(10, metric, now=101)['alerts'] == ['host_heartbeat_missing']
    assert observer.health(None, None, now=30)['status'] == 'critical'
    metric['memory']['MemAvailable'] = 1024**3
    assert observer.health(10, metric, now=30)['alerts'] == ['host_memory_below_2GiB']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('raw', [b'{}', b'[]', b'not-json', b' ' * 8193,
    b'{"format":"host-observer-v1","memory":{"MemAvailable":true}}'])
def test_malformed_telemetry_cannot_refresh_heartbeat(raw):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        observer.metric_packet(raw)


def test_valid_metric_packet_and_bounded_rotation(tmp_path):
    metric = {'format': 'host-observer-v1', 'memory': {'MemAvailable': 123}}
    assert observer.metric_packet(json.dumps(metric).encode()) == metric
    log = observer.logger(tmp_path, 'fixture')
    handler = log.handlers[0]
    assert handler.maxBytes * (handler.backupCount + 1) == 64 * 1024**2
    handler.close(); log.removeHandler(handler)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_image_defects_toggle.py =====
"""image_defects 前检按合同开关可配置。

默认启用(合同无 image_defects_enabled 字段)时 schema/适配器/正文与现役完全一致;
合同显式 image_defects_enabled=false(灵感图新机制把水印并入红线)时,调用A的
schema 不含 image_defects、适配器不再把它当决定性字段(不误报 missing/invalid)、
合同正文不再要求模型输出它。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from app.schema_adapter import (
    adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck,
    inspiration_authoritative_precheck_prompt_contract,
    inspiration_image_defects_enabled,
)
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import strict_output_contract
from app.inspiration_category_seed import build_inspiration_v3_contract


def _call_a_precheck() -> dict:
    # 模型侧调用A原始形状(扁平、未经适配器归一),故意不含 image_defects 字段。
    return {
        "redline_triggered": {},
        "reason": [],
        "hard_defects": [],
        "decisive_evidence": {"redline_triggered": {}, "hard_defects": []},
        "decision_status": "complete",
        "uncertain_fields": [],
        "track_classification": "class_one",
        "primary_category": "建筑设计",
        "classification_confidence": 0.9,
        "trait": "实景照片",
    }


def test_helper_defaults_enabled_and_reads_flag() -> None:
    assert inspiration_image_defects_enabled(None) is True
    assert inspiration_image_defects_enabled({}) is True
    assert inspiration_image_defects_enabled({"image_defects_enabled": False}) is False
    assert inspiration_image_defects_enabled({"image_defects_enabled": True}) is True
    # 非布尔值按启用兜底(fail-safe,不静默关闭)
    assert inspiration_image_defects_enabled({"image_defects_enabled": "no"}) is True


def test_schema_includes_image_defects_by_default() -> None:
    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    blob = json.dumps(strict_output_contract("inspiration_image", "A", evaluation_contract=contract))
    assert "image_defects" in blob


def test_schema_drops_image_defects_when_disabled() -> None:
    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    contract["image_defects_enabled"] = False
    blob = json.dumps(strict_output_contract("inspiration_image", "A", evaluation_contract=contract))
    assert "image_defects" not in blob
    # 其余决定性字段仍在
    assert "hard_defects" in blob
    assert "redline_triggered" in blob


def test_adapter_flags_missing_image_defects_when_enabled() -> None:
    normalized = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(_call_a_precheck())
    reasons = normalized["review_reasons"]
    assert any("image_defects" in r for r in reasons)


def test_adapter_ignores_image_defects_when_disabled() -> None:
    normalized = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(
        _call_a_precheck(), image_defects_enabled=False
    )
    reasons = normalized["review_reasons"]
    assert not any("image_defects" in r for r in reasons)
    # production 也不再映射 image_defects
    assert "image_defects" not in normalized.get("production_fields", {})


def test_prompt_contract_toggles_image_defects_line() -> None:
    assert "image_defects" in inspiration_authoritative_precheck_prompt_contract(None)
    assert "image_defects" not in inspiration_authoritative_precheck_prompt_contract(
        None, image_defects_enabled=False
    )


def test_image_defects_flag_survives_workflow_projection() -> None:
    # 开关是工作流级合同字段：必须能通过 WORKFLOW_LEVEL_FIELDS 白名单进入
    # legacy_projection_snapshot，否则 executor/finalizer 读不到 false。
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import _ownership_audit, _workflow_fields

    bundle = {
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "contract": {"image_defects_enabled": False},
    }
    projected = _workflow_fields(bundle)
    assert projected["contract"]["image_defects_enabled"] is False
    unclassified, _multiply = _ownership_audit(bundle)
    assert "contract.image_defects_enabled" not in unclassified


def test_latest_call_a_schema_carries_title_and_seotitle() -> None:
    from app.strict_prompt_contracts import LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE

    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    doc = strict_output_contract(
        "inspiration_image", "A", evaluation_contract=contract,
        contract_profile=LATEST_INSPIRATION_CONTRACT_PROFILE,
    )
    props = doc["schema"]["properties"]
    assert "title" in props and "seotitle" in props
    assert "title" in doc["schema"]["required"]


def test_adapter_passes_title_and_seotitle_to_production_fields() -> None:
    precheck = _call_a_precheck()
    precheck.update({"title": "现代商业空间", "seotitle": "现代简约商业空间设计参考"})
    normalized = adapt_inspiration_call_a_precheck(precheck, image_defects_enabled=False)
    assert normalized["production_fields"]["title"] == "现代商业空间"
    assert normalized["production_fields"]["seotitle"] == "现代简约商业空间设计参考"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_image_first_seen_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql, postgresql
from sqlalchemy.schema import CreateTable

from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import (
    create_mysql_baseline,
    mysql_metadata,
    upgrade_mysql_schema,
)
from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import postgres_metadata
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
    "id",
    "provider_key",
    "provider_version",
    "image_digest",
    "status",
    "earliest_discovered_at",
    "evidence_count",
    "response_hash",
    "created_at",
    "updated_at",
}


def test_migration_90_creates_idempotent_first_seen_cache(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'first-seen-v90.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 90)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)
        inspector = inspect(engine)
        assert EXPECTED_COLUMNS == {
            item["name"]
            for item in inspector.get_columns("image_first_seen_cache")
        }
        unique_shapes = {
            tuple(item["column_names"])
            for item in inspector.get_unique_constraints("image_first_seen_cache")
        }
        assert ("provider_key", "provider_version", "image_digest") in unique_shapes
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_and_postgres_baselines_compile_equivalent_cache_contract() -> None:
    for metadata, dialect in (
        (mysql_metadata(), mysql.dialect()),
        (postgres_metadata(), postgresql.dialect()),
    ):
        table = metadata.tables["image_first_seen_cache"]
        assert EXPECTED_COLUMNS == set(table.c.keys())
        ddl = str(CreateTable(table).compile(dialect=dialect)).lower()
        assert "unique" in ddl
        assert "provider_key" in ddl
        assert "image_digest" in ddl


def test_mysql_v89_upgrades_additively_to_v90(mysql_engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE workflow_model_daily_budgets")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE image_first_seen_cache")
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= 90"
        )
        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=90)

        assert "image_first_seen_cache" in inspect(connection).get_table_names()
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 90

===== FILE backend/tests/test_image_first_seen_node.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

import app.workflow_fixture_executor as fixture_executor
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
from app.connector_intake import IntakeRequest, ingest_upstream_event
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowExternalWait,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.workflow_composer_runtime import ensure_composer_fixture_version
from app.workflow_external_wait import (
    ExternalResumeRequest,
    resume_external_wait,
)
from app.workflow_node_executors import (
    NodeExecutionContext,
    NodeExecutorError,
    execute_native_node,
)
from app.workflow_result_contract import hash_manifest
from app.image_first_seen_provider import FirstSeenResult, first_seen_callback_payload
from app.workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    create_production_run,
)


FIRST_SEEN_SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object",
    "properties": {
        "status": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["found", "not_found", "unavailable", "failed"],
        },
        "web_first_seen_at": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
        "source_url": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
        "provider": {"type": "string"},
        "match_kind": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["exact", "partial", "similar", "unknown"],
        },
        "confidence": {"type": "number"},
        "checked_at": {"type": "string"},
        "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "provider_result_id": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
                "matched_urls_count": {"type": "integer"},
                "result_hash": {"type": "string"},
            },
            "required": [
                "provider_result_id",
                "matched_urls_count",
                "result_hash",
            ],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    },
    "required": [
        "status",
        "web_first_seen_at",
        "source_url",
        "provider",
        "match_kind",
        "confidence",
        "checked_at",
        "evidence",
    ],
    "additionalProperties": False,
}


def _node() -> dict:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": "first-seen",
        "kind": "image_first_seen_lookup",
        "display_name": "查询图片最早出现时间",
        "config": {
            "provider_connector_key": "logical-image-search",
            "request_contract_version_id": 21,
            "response_contract_version_id": 22,
            "wait_timeout_seconds": 3600,
            "timeout_policy": "manual_review",
        },
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": deepcopy(FIRST_SEEN_SCHEMA),
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 2,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": [],
    }


def _result_hash(payload: dict) -> str:
    basis = deepcopy(payload)
    basis["evidence"].pop("result_hash", None)
    return hash_manifest(basis)


def _context(
    *,
    frozen_input: dict,
    input_manifest: dict | None = None,
    run_mode: str = "stock_regression",
    attempt_no: int = 1,
) -> NodeExecutionContext:
    node = _node()
    return NodeExecutionContext(
        run_snapshot={
            "workflow": {
                "manifest": {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "category_key": "space_image",
                    "nodes": [node],
                    "edges": [],
                }
            },
            "runtime_context": {
                "run_mode": run_mode,
                "input_manifest": frozen_input,
            },
        },
        node=node,
        input_manifest=input_manifest or {"asset_id": 42},
        raw_fixture_output={},
        attempt_no=attempt_no,
        started_at=datetime(2026, 8, 29, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )


def test_stock_first_seen_without_imported_evidence_is_offline_and_explicit() -> None:
    result = execute_native_node(
        _context(frozen_input={"asset_id": 42})
    )

    output = result["output"]
    assert output == {
        "status": "unavailable",
        "web_first_seen_at": None,
        "source_url": None,
        "provider": "unconfigured",
        "match_kind": "unknown",
        "confidence": 0.0,
        "checked_at": "2026-08-29T12:00:00Z",
        "evidence": {
            "provider_result_id": None,
            "matched_urls_count": 0,
            "result_hash": output["evidence"]["result_hash"],
        },
    }
    assert output["evidence"]["result_hash"] == _result_hash(output)
    assert result["external_io_performed"] is False
    assert result["io_mode"] == "unavailable"


def test_provider_timeout_maps_to_explicit_unknown_callback_without_a_url() -> None:
    payload = first_seen_callback_payload(
        FirstSeenResult(
            status="timeout",
            earliest_discovered_at=None,
            provider="tineye",
            evidence_count=0,
            cache_key="a" * 64,
        ),
        checked_at=datetime(2026, 8, 31, 9, 30, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    assert payload["status"] == "unavailable"
    assert payload["source_url"] is None
    assert payload["provider"] == "tineye"
    assert payload["evidence"]["provider_result_id"] == "a" * 64
    assert payload["evidence"]["result_hash"] == _result_hash(payload)


def test_stock_first_seen_replays_node_level_imported_evidence() -> None:
    imported = {
        "status": "found",
        "web_first_seen_at": "2023-02-01T00:00:00Z",
        "source_url": "https://example.invalid/redacted",
        "provider": "fixture-provider",
        "match_kind": "exact",
        "confidence": 0.98,
        "checked_at": "2026-08-20T00:00:00Z",
        "evidence": {
            "provider_result_id": "fixture-17",
            "matched_urls_count": 3,
            "result_hash": "",
        },
    }
    imported["evidence"]["result_hash"] = _result_hash(imported)
    result = execute_native_node(
        _context(
            frozen_input={
                "asset_id": 42,
                "workflow_node_evidence": {"first-seen": imported},
            }
        )
    )

    assert result["output"] == imported
    assert result["external_io_performed"] is False
    assert result["io_mode"] == "imported_evidence"


def test_first_seen_rejects_tampered_imported_or_callback_result() -> None:
    tampered = {
        "status": "not_found",
        "web_first_seen_at": None,
        "source_url": None,
        "provider": "fixture-provider",
        "match_kind": "unknown",
        "confidence": 0.0,
        "checked_at": "2026-08-20T00:00:00Z",
        "evidence": {
            "provider_result_id": None,
            "matched_urls_count": 0,
            "result_hash": "f" * 64,
        },
    }
    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as imported_error:
        execute_native_node(
            _context(
                frozen_input={
                    "workflow_node_evidence": {"first-seen": tampered}
                }
            )
        )
    assert imported_error.value.code == "first_seen_result_hash_mismatch"

    with pytest.raises(NodeExecutorError) as callback_error:
        execute_native_node(
            _context(
                frozen_input={"asset_id": 42},
                input_manifest=tampered,
                run_mode="incremental",
                attempt_no=2,
            )
        )
    assert callback_error.value.code == "first_seen_result_hash_mismatch"


def _incremental_first_seen_world(db):
    request_contract = create_connector_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key="image-first-seen-request",
        direction="downstream",
        category_key="space_image",
        logical_locator="logical.image.first_seen.request",
        adapter_key="api-json-v1",
        schema_fingerprint="a" * 64,
        field_mapping={"asset_id": "asset_id"},
        cursor_contract={},
        idempotency_contract={"field": "correlation_id"},
        batch_size=100,
        timeout_seconds=30,
        owner="test",
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    response_contract = create_connector_contract_version(
        db,
        contract_key="image-first-seen-response",
        direction="upstream",
        category_key="space_image",
        logical_locator="logical.image.first_seen.response",
        adapter_key="api-json-v1",
        schema_fingerprint="b" * 64,
        field_mapping={"status": "status"},
        cursor_contract={
            "kind": "text",
            "ordering": "ascending",
            "out_of_order": "accept",
        },
        idempotency_contract={"field": "source_event_id"},
        batch_size=100,
        timeout_seconds=30,
        owner="test",
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    node = _node()
    node["config"]["request_contract_version_id"] = request_contract.id
    node["config"]["response_contract_version_id"] = response_contract.id
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key="runtime.image-first-seen.incremental",
        name="Image first seen incremental",
        description="",
        owner="test",
        allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]',
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "space_image",
        "lane_key": "incremental",
        "run_modes": ["incremental"],
        "nodes": [node],
        "edges": [],
        "queue_class": "production_batch",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {},
        "layout": {},
    }
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json([node]),
        edge_manifest_json="[]",
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": "space_image",
                "lane_key": "incremental",
                "run_modes": ["incremental"],
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "layout": {},
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=hash_manifest(manifest),
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(version)
    ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, "image_first_seen_lookup")
    db.flush()
    run = create_production_run(
        db,
        ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=version.id,
            idempotency_key="image-first-seen-asset-42",
            category_key="space_image",
            queue_class="production_batch",
            runtime_context={"run_mode": "incremental"},
            input_manifest={"asset_id": 42, "content_key": "asset-42"},
            owner="test",
            source_type="incremental_material",
            source_id=42,
        ),
        actor="test",
    )
    return run, request_contract, response_contract


def test_incremental_first_seen_uses_outbox_wait_and_callback_without_io(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'first-seen-wait.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, request_contract, response_contract = (
                _incremental_first_seen_world(db)
            )
            db.commit()
            sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
            monkeypatch.setattr(
                fixture_executor,
                "SessionLocal",
                sessions,
            )
            assert fixture_executor.process_runtime_step_once(
                "first-seen-worker"
            ) is True
            db.expire_all()
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no == 1,
                )
            )
            wait = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowExternalWait).where(
                    WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id == run.id
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None
            assert wait is not None
            assert run.status == "waiting_external"
            assert attempt.status == "retryable"
            assert wait.request_node_instance_id == "first-seen"
            assert wait.resume_node_instance_id == "first-seen"
            assert wait.expected_contract_version_id == response_contract.id
            assert wait.outbox_event.target_contract_version_id == (
                request_contract.id
            )
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 1

            callback_payload = {
                "status": "found",
                "web_first_seen_at": "2023-02-01T00:00:00Z",
                "source_url": "https://example.invalid/redacted",
                "provider": "fixture-provider",
                "match_kind": "exact",
                "confidence": 0.98,
                "checked_at": "2026-08-29T12:01:00Z",
                "evidence": {
                    "provider_result_id": "fixture-42",
                    "matched_urls_count": 1,
                    "result_hash": "",
                },
            }
            callback_payload["evidence"]["result_hash"] = _result_hash(
                callback_payload
            )
            callback = ingest_upstream_event(
                db,
                response_contract,
                IntakeRequest(
                    source_event_id="first-seen-result-42",
                    source_cursor="",
                    source_occurred_at=None,
                    category_key="space_image",
                    mechanism_key="runtime.image-first-seen.incremental",
                    payload=callback_payload,
                    actor="fixture-provider",
                ),
            ).event
            resume_external_wait(
                db,
                ExternalResumeRequest(
                    correlation_id=wait.correlation_id,
                    callback_intake_event_id=callback.id,
                    callback_payload_hash=callback.payload_hash,
                    actor="callback-api",
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            assert [row.attempt_no for row in attempts] == [1, 2]
            assert attempts[1].status == "pending"
            assert json.loads(attempts[1].input_manifest_json) == callback_payload
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(RuntimeDispatchItem)
                .where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempts[1].id)
            ) == 1

            assert fixture_executor.process_runtime_step_once(
                "first-seen-resume"
            ) is True
            db.expire_all()
            assert run.status == "succeeded"
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowExternalWait)
            ) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_first_seen_request_is_sent_when_first_attempt_expired_before_wait(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """第一次尝试在登记首见等待前租约过期：第二次尝试必须发请求，不能当回调消费。"""

    from datetime import timedelta

    from app.runtime_queue import ClaimRequest, claim_runtime_item
    from app.workflow_runtime import recover_expired_runtime_steps

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'first-seen-expired.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, _request_contract, _response_contract = (
                _incremental_first_seen_world(db)
            )
            db.commit()
            claimed = claim_runtime_item(
                db, ClaimRequest(worker_id="dead-worker", global_limit=10,
                                 lease_seconds=30),
            )
            assert claimed is not None and claimed.attempt_id is not None
            # 工作进程在登记首见等待之前就卡死：租约过期。
            expired_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1)
            db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, claimed.attempt_id).lease_expires_at = expired_at
            db.get(RuntimeDispatchItem, claimed.item_id).lease_expires_at = expired_at
            db.commit()
            recover_expired_runtime_steps(db)
            db.commit()
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            assert [a.attempt_no for a in attempts] == [1, 2]
            assert attempts[0].status == "failed"
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowExternalWait)) == 0

            sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
            monkeypatch.setattr(fixture_executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
            assert fixture_executor.process_runtime_step_once(
                "first-seen-worker"
            ) is True
            db.expire_all()
            second = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, attempts[1].id)
            assert second.last_error_code == "external_wait_requested"
            assert second.status == "retryable"
            wait = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowExternalWait).where(
                    WorkflowExternalWait.production_run_id == run.id
                )
            )
            assert wait is not None
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run.id).status == "waiting_external"
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)
            ) == 1
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_image_first_seen_provider.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.image_first_seen_provider import (
    FirstSeenProviderError,
    ProviderHttpResponse,
    TinEyeImageFirstSeenProvider,
    UnconfiguredImageFirstSeenProvider,
    first_seen_callback_payload,
)
from app.models import ImageFirstSeenCache, ModelQuotaLease, ModelQuotaPool


NOW = datetime(2026, 8, 31, 9, 30, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
IMAGE_BYTES = b"deterministic-image-fixture"
IMAGE_DIGEST = hashlib.sha256(IMAGE_BYTES).hexdigest()


def _pool(db: Session) -> ModelQuotaPool:
    row = ModelQuotaPool(
        pool_key="tineye-test",
        name="TinEye test quota",
        provider="tineye",
        scope_kind="api_key",
        scope_identifier="secret-ref:test",
        max_concurrency=2,
        requests_per_minute=10,
        tokens_per_minute=None,
        images_per_minute=10,
        lease_seconds=30,
        enabled=True,
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def _provider(
    db: Session,
    pool: ModelQuotaPool,
    *,
    transport,
    secret: str = "secret-value-that-must-not-leak",
) -> TinEyeImageFirstSeenProvider:
    def resolve(reference: str) -> str:
        assert reference == "file-aead:v1:image-first-seen-provider:opaque"
        return secret

    return TinEyeImageFirstSeenProvider(
        db,
        provider_key="tineye",
        provider_version="api-v2",
        secret_reference="file-aead:v1:image-first-seen-provider:opaque",
        quota_pool_id=pool.id,
        secret_resolver=resolve,
        transport=transport,
        timeout_seconds=12,
        now=lambda: NOW,
    )


def test_unconfigured_provider_returns_unknown_without_failing_workflow() -> None:
    result = UnconfiguredImageFirstSeenProvider().lookup(
        IMAGE_DIGEST,
        IMAGE_BYTES,
    )

    assert result.status == "unknown"
    assert result.earliest_discovered_at is None
    assert result.provider == "unconfigured"
    assert result.evidence_count == 0
    assert len(result.cache_key) == 64

    callback = first_seen_callback_payload(result, checked_at=NOW)
    assert callback["status"] == "unavailable"
    assert callback["web_first_seen_at"] is None
    assert callback["source_url"] is None
    assert callback["provider"] == "unconfigured"


def test_tineye_shape_extracts_earliest_discovery_and_reuses_digest_cache(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'first-seen.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    secret = "secret-value-that-must-not-leak"

    def transport(**kwargs):
        calls.append(kwargs)
        assert kwargs["api_key"] == secret
        assert kwargs["image_bytes"] == IMAGE_BYTES
        assert kwargs["timeout_seconds"] == 12
        # The quota lease must be committed before the external side effect.
        with Session(engine) as observer:
            assert observer.scalar(
                select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.status == "active"
                )
            ) == 1
        return ProviderHttpResponse(
            status_code=200,
            payload={
                "results": {
                    "matches": [
                        {
                            "backlinks": [
                                {
                                    "crawl_date": "2022-07-03T10:00:00Z",
                                    "url": "https://must-not-enter-cache.invalid/one",
                                },
                                {
                                    "crawl_date": "2020-01-02T03:04:05Z",
                                    "url": "https://must-not-enter-api.invalid/two",
                                },
                            ]
                        }
                    ]
                }
            },
        )

    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db)
            db.commit()
            provider = _provider(db, pool, transport=transport, secret=secret)
            first = provider.lookup(IMAGE_DIGEST, IMAGE_BYTES)
            second = provider.lookup(IMAGE_DIGEST, IMAGE_BYTES)

            assert first == second
            assert first.status == "found"
            assert first.earliest_discovered_at == datetime(
                2020, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, tzinfo=timezone.utc
            )
            assert first.evidence_count == 2
            assert len(calls) == 1
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ImageFirstSeenCache.id))) == 1
            row = db.scalar(select(ImageFirstSeenCache))
            assert row is not None
            serialized = json.dumps(
                {
                    "result": first.as_public_dict(),
                    "cache": {
                        key: value
                        for key, value in vars(row).items()
                        if not key.startswith("_")
                    },
                },
                default=str,
            )
            assert secret not in serialized
            assert "must-not-enter" not in serialized
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(ModelQuotaLease.id)).where(
                    ModelQuotaLease.status == "active"
                )
            ) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("transport", "expected_status"),
    (
        (
            lambda **_kwargs: ProviderHttpResponse(
                status_code=200,
                payload={"results": {"matches": []}},
            ),
            "not_found",
        ),
        (
            lambda **_kwargs: (_ for _ in ()).throw(TimeoutError("private URL")),
            "timeout",
        ),
        (
            lambda **_kwargs: ProviderHttpResponse(
                status_code=429,
                payload={"private": "must-not-persist"},
                retry_after_seconds=45,
            ),
            "rate_limited",
        ),
    ),
)
def test_no_match_timeout_and_429_are_explicit_redacted_states(
    tmp_path,
    transport,
    expected_status: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / (expected_status + '.db')}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db)
            db.commit()
            result = _provider(db, pool, transport=transport).lookup(
                IMAGE_DIGEST,
                IMAGE_BYTES,
            )
            assert result.status == expected_status
            assert result.earliest_discovered_at is None
            assert result.evidence_count == 0
            assert "private" not in json.dumps(result.as_public_dict())
            if expected_status == "rate_limited":
                db.refresh(pool)
                assert pool.cooldown_reason == "provider_rate_limited"
                assert pool.cooldown_until is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_digest_must_match_bytes_before_secret_or_provider_access(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'digest.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            pool = _pool(db)
            db.commit()
            provider = _provider(
                db,
                pool,
                transport=lambda **_kwargs: pytest.fail("provider was called"),
            )
            with pytest.raises(FirstSeenProviderError) as exc_info:
                provider.lookup("f" * 64, IMAGE_BYTES)
            assert exc_info.value.code == "image_digest_mismatch"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incident_queue_restore.py =====
"""One-time bulk rerun lane restoration preserves the normal recovery throttle."""
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select, func

from app.models import AuditEvent, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, RuntimeDispatchItem, WorkflowModelInvocation
from app.native_baseline_incident import quiesce_baseline_incident, resume_baseline_incident
from app.queue_scheduler import QueuePolicy, queue_capacities
from app.runtime_queue import _queue_counts
from app.workflow_runtime import claim_next_runtime_step
from test_native_baseline_incident import shared_case, fallback_case, _incident_fixture

restore = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] /
    'docs/verification/batch-breaker-recovery-20260911/restore-recovery-lane.py'))['restore_pending_incident_queue']


@pytest.fixture
def restored_incident(shared_case):
    sessions, cases = shared_case
    contract = _incident_fixture(sessions, cases)
    with sessions() as db:
        # Native baseline creation always uses validation, unlike the generic
        # real-model fixture's production_batch lane.
        for run in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)):
            run.queue_class = 'validation'
        quiesce_baseline_incident(db, **contract)
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert resume_baseline_incident(db, **contract)['resumed'] == 4
        db.commit()
    return sessions, contract


def facts(db):
    return {
        'snapshots': [(r.id, r.snapshot_json, r.snapshot_hash) for r in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).order_by(ProductionRun.id))],
        'attempts': [(a.id, a.attempt_no, a.status, a.input_hash, a.output_manifest_json) for a in db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.id))],
        'fees': [(r.id, r.status, r.estimated_cost_micros, r.actual_cost_micros, r.evidence_json) for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))],
        'available_at': [(d.id, d.available_at) for d in db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).order_by(RuntimeDispatchItem.id))],
    }


def test_bulk_rerun_rejoins_validation_without_replaying_or_raising_recovery_cap(restored_incident):
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    sessions, contract = restored_incident
    with sessions() as db:
        before = facts(db)
        pending, running = _queue_counts(db, now=datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        policy = QueuePolicy(global_limit=48)
        assert queue_capacities(policy, pending=pending, running=running)['recovery'].effective_limit > 4  # borrows idle fresh-lane capacity
        assert restore(db, **contract)['moved'] == 4
        assert facts(db) == before
        pending, running = _queue_counts(db, now=datetime.now(timezone.utc))
        assert pending['validation'] == 4 and pending['recovery'] == 0
        assert queue_capacities(policy, pending=pending, running=running)['validation'].effective_limit >= 24
        assert queue_capacities(policy, pending={'recovery':1000})['recovery'].effective_limit == 48
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert restore(db, **contract)['moved'] == 0
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(AuditEvent.action == 'operator_incident_queue_restore')) == 4


def test_lane_restore_leaves_claimed_work_untouched(restored_incident):
    sessions, contract = restored_incident
    with sessions() as db:
        claimed = claim_next_runtime_step(db, 'existing-worker', global_limit=48)
        db.commit()
    with sessions() as db:
        dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == claimed))
        before = (dispatch.queue_class, dispatch.status, dispatch.lease_token, dispatch.attempt_count)
        frozen = facts(db)
        assert restore(db, **contract)['moved'] == 3
        assert facts(db) == frozen
        assert (dispatch.queue_class, dispatch.status, dispatch.lease_token, dispatch.attempt_count) == before
        db.commit()


def test_lane_restore_preview_rolls_back_and_rejects_wrong_origin(restored_incident):
    sessions, contract = restored_incident
    with sessions() as db:
        assert restore(db, **contract)['moved'] == 4
        db.rollback()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id)).where(
            RuntimeDispatchItem.status == 'queued', RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class == 'recovery')) == 4
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.status == 'queued').order_by(ProductionRun.id))
        run.queue_class = 'interactive'
        db.flush()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='identity changed'):
            restore(db, **contract)
        db.rollback()


def test_lane_restore_refuses_previously_attempted_dispatch(restored_incident):
    sessions, contract = restored_incident
    with sessions() as db:
        first = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.status == 'queued').order_by(ProductionRun.id))
        dispatch = db.scalar(select(RuntimeDispatchItem).join(ProductionStepAttempt,
            ProductionStepAttempt.id == RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id).where(
                ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == first.id, RuntimeDispatchItem.status == 'queued'))
        # An attempted dispatch must never be moved even if its status was reset.
        dispatch.attempt_count = 1
        db.flush()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='already been attempted'):
            restore(db, **contract)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_automation.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app.database import get_db
from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_due_incremental_batches
from app.incremental_mechanisms_api import build_incremental_mechanisms_router
from app.models import AuditEvent, ProductionRun, User, WorkflowVersion
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _close, _fixture, _headers, _payload


def _operator(db, role="admin"):
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_incremental_mechanisms_router(lambda: User(
        username=role, role=role, is_admin=role == "admin", password_hash="x", display_name=role,
    )))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    return TestClient(app)


def test_cards_and_switch_revision_and_permission():
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        operator = _operator(db)
        response = operator.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards")
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        card = next(c for c in response.json()["items"] if c["bound"])
        assert card["category_key"] == "space_image"
        assert card["metrics"]["runs"] == 0
        path = f'/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{card["workflow_definition_id"]}'
        payload = {"enabled": False, "expected_revision": 0, "reason": "暂停联调"}
        assert _operator(db, "manager").put(path, json=payload).status_code == 403
        paused = operator.put(path, json=payload)
        assert paused.status_code == 200, paused.text
        assert paused.json()["enabled"] is False
        assert paused.json()["revision"] == 1
        assert operator.put(path, json=payload).status_code == 409
        assert operator.put(path, json={**payload, "expected_revision": 1, "reason": "  "}).status_code == 422
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(AuditEvent.category == "incremental_automation")) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_pause_blocks_immediate_and_worker_dispatch_then_resume_preserves_event(monkeypatch):
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        operator = _operator(db)
        path = f"/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{version.workflow_definition_id}"
        paused = operator.put(path, json={"enabled": False, "expected_revision": 0, "reason": "暂停"})
        assert paused.status_code == 200, paused.text
        response = ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="paused-event", asset_id=asset.id))
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["routing_status"] == "automation_paused"
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0
        assert dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor="test", now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=1)) == ()
        db.commit()
        resumed = operator.put(path, json={"enabled": True, "expected_revision": 1, "reason": "继续"})
        assert resumed.status_code == 200, resumed.text
        runs = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor="test", now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=1))
        assert len(runs) == 1
        db.commit()
        card = next(c for c in operator.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards").json()["items"] if c["bound"])
        assert card["metrics"]["received"] == 1
        assert card["recent_runs"][0]["run_key"] == runs[0]
        assert card["recent_runs"][0]["workflow_version_id"] == version.id
        # Closing automation never cancels a run already handed to the runtime.
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        status = run.status
        assert operator.put(path, json={"enabled": False, "expected_revision": 2, "reason": "再次暂停"}).status_code == 200
        db.refresh(run)
        assert run.status == status
        replay = ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="paused-event", asset_id=asset.id))
        assert replay.status_code == 200
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_paused_workflow_does_not_starve_another_lane(monkeypatch):
    from test_incremental_autorun import _two_waiting_incremental_lanes
    from app.models import MaterialPackage, IncrementalAutomationControl
    import app.incremental_autorun as autorun

    engine, db, packages, due_at = _two_waiting_incremental_lanes(monkeypatch, separate_workflows=True)
    try:
        # Verify fairness under the smallest scan budget: a paused oldest lane
        # must not consume the only slot while another workflow is eligible.
        package = db.scalar(select(MaterialPackage).order_by(MaterialPackage.id))
        version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, package.workflow_version_id)
        db.add(IncrementalAutomationControl(category_key=package.category_key,
            workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id, enabled=False,
            revision=1, updated_by="test", reason="暂停最早链路"))
        db.commit()
        cards = _operator(db).get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards").json()["items"]
        assert len(cards) == 2
        assert next(c for c in cards if c["workflow_definition_id"] == version.workflow_definition_id)["enabled"] is False
        assert next(c for c in cards if c["workflow_definition_id"] != version.workflow_definition_id)["enabled"] is True
        monkeypatch.setattr(autorun, "MAX_INCREMENTAL_LANES_PER_TICK", 1)
        dispatched = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor="test", now=due_at)
        assert dispatched
        assert all(run.workflow_definition_id != version.workflow_definition_id for run in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all())
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_sqlite_v101_additive_upgrade_preserves_existing_binding():
    from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
    from app.migrations import run_migrations
    from app.models import CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, IncrementalAutomationControl
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        original = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)).workflow_version_id
        db.close()
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            IncrementalAutomationControl.__table__.drop(connection)
            connection.execute(text("DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 102"))
            run_migrations(connection)
            run_migrations(connection)
            assert "incremental_automation_controls" in inspect(connection).get_table_names()
            assert connection.execute(text("SELECT workflow_version_id FROM category_incremental_mechanism_bindings")).scalar_one() == original
            assert connection.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM incremental_automation_controls")).scalar_one() == 0
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

def test_sqlite_switch_and_dispatch_share_a_transaction_lock(tmp_path):
    import pytest
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine
    from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    from app.database import Base
    from app.incremental_automation import automation_paused, set_automation_control
    from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
    from incremental_mechanism_fixtures import seed_incremental_versions

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'controls.sqlite'}", connect_args={"timeout": 0.01})
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as seed:
        version = seed_incremental_versions(seed)[0]
        set_incremental_mechanism(seed, category_key="space_image", workflow_version_id=version.id,
            expected_lock_version=None, reason="fixture", actor="test")
        version_id, definition_id = version.id, version.workflow_definition_id
        seed.commit()
    try:
        with Session(engine) as dispatch, Session(engine) as switch:
            assert automation_paused(dispatch, category_key="space_image", workflow_version_id=version_id) is False
            args = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition_id,
                        enabled=False, expected_revision=0, reason="暂停", actor="test")
            with pytest.raises(OperationalError, match="locked"):
                set_automation_control(switch, **args)
            switch.rollback()
            dispatch.commit()
            set_automation_control(switch, **args)
            switch.commit()
            assert automation_paused(dispatch, category_key="space_image", workflow_version_id=version_id) is True
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def test_old_bound_workflow_remains_switchable_while_frozen_backlog_drains(monkeypatch):
    from test_incremental_autorun import _two_waiting_incremental_lanes

        cards = operator.get("/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/cards").json()["items"]
        old = next(c for c in cards if not c["bound"])
        assert old["enabled"] is True  # Its frozen backlog is still dispatchable.
        path = f'/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{old["workflow_definition_id"]}'
        assert operator.put(path, json={"enabled": False, "expected_revision": 0, "reason": "停止旧工作流积压"}).status_code == 200
        assert operator.put(path, json={"enabled": True, "expected_revision": 1, "reason": "不能绕过当前绑定"}).status_code == 409
        runs = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor="test", now=due_at)
        assert len(runs) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(ProductionRun)).workflow_definition_id != old["workflow_definition_id"]
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

def test_archive_is_reversible_incremental_only_and_cannot_hide_current_binding():
    from app.models import CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding, WorkflowDefinition, IncrementalAutomationControl
    from app.incremental_automation import archive_automation_control, set_automation_operation, automation_cards
    from app.incremental_routing import current_policy
    from app.incremental_mechanisms import IncrementalMechanismError
    import pytest
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id)
        policy = current_policy(db, **scope)
        set_automation_operation(db, **scope, enabled=False, expected_revision=0,
            expected_routing_revision=policy.revision if policy else 0, reason="归档旧增量入口", actor="test")
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalMechanismError, match="正式增量"):
            archive_automation_control(db, **scope, archived=True, expected_revision=1, reason="归档", actor="test")
        binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding))
        db.delete(binding); db.flush()
        result = archive_automation_control(db, **scope, archived=True, expected_revision=1, reason="归档", actor="test")
        assert result == {"archived": True, "enabled": False, "revision": 2}
        assert not automation_cards(db)["items"]
        assert db.get(WorkflowDefinition, version.workflow_definition_id).status == "active"
        assert db.get(WorkflowVersion, version.id) is not None
        restored = archive_automation_control(db, **scope, archived=False, expected_revision=2, reason="恢复入口", actor="test")
        assert restored["revision"] == 3 and not restored["enabled"]
        assert len(automation_cards(db)["items"]) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import io
import json

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse

    Asset,
    AuditEvent,
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    ContentIngressEvent,
    ContentRecord,
    MaterialPackage,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    WorkflowModelInvocation,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_due_incremental_batches
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.strategy_bundle import build_model_config_snapshot
from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation
