def _enable_real_incremental_policy(
    db,
    monkeypatch,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1,
) -> tuple[WorkflowVersion, ModelConfig]:
    """Turn the fixture mechanism into a server-priced real autorun policy."""

    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
    model = db.scalar(select(ModelConfig))
    registry = db.scalar(select(ModelRegistryEntry))
    assert version is not None and model is not None and registry is not None
    model.encrypted_api_key = "opaque-test-secret-reference"
    model.benchmark_enabled = True
    model.max_input_tokens = 12_000
    model.input_micros_per_million_tokens = 1_000_000
    model.output_micros_per_million_tokens = 2_000_000
    nodes = json.loads(version.step_manifest_json)
    for node in nodes:
        if node.get("kind") != "prompt":
            continue
        model_snapshot = {
            "model_registry_id": registry.id,
            "name": registry.name,
            "provider": registry.provider,
            "protocol": registry.protocol,
            "model_id": registry.model_id,
            "source_model_config_id": model.id,
            "structured_output": registry.structured_output,
            "runtime_config": build_model_config_snapshot(model),
        }
        model_snapshot["snapshot_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(model_snapshot).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        node["config"]["model_snapshot"] = model_snapshot
    version.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
    policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
    policy["incremental_autorun"] = {
        "enabled": True,
        "batch_size": batch_size,
        "max_wait_seconds": 3_600 if batch_size > 1 else 0,
        "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
        "model_execution_mode": "real",
    }
    version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
    version.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(version)).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow_model_budget,
        "secret_reference_available",
        lambda reference: bool(reference),
        raising=False,
    )
    db.commit()
    return version, model


def _two_waiting_incremental_lanes(monkeypatch, *, separate_workflows=False):
    """Create one due pending package in each of two frozen mechanism lanes."""

    engine, db, client, first_asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    versions = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowVersion).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id.asc())
    ).all()
    assert len(versions) == 2
    if separate_workflows:
        from app.models import WorkflowDefinition
        definition = WorkflowDefinition(workflow_key="second.incremental", name="第二条增量工作流",
            owner="test", created_by="test", allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]')
        db.add(definition)
        db.flush()
        versions[1].workflow_definition_id = definition.id
    for version in versions:
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy["incremental_autorun"] = {
            "enabled": True,
            "batch_size": 2,
            "max_wait_seconds": 60,
            "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
        }
        version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        version.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(version)).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
    second_asset = Asset(
        original_name="asset-2.jpg",
        stored_name="asset-2.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=11,
        sha256="b" * 64,
        category_key="space_image",
    )
    db.add(second_asset)
    db.commit()

    first = client.post(
        "/api/content-ingress/events",
        headers=headers,
        json=_payload(event_id="evt-two-lanes-1", asset_id=first_asset.id),
    )
    assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
    assert first.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"

    binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding))
    assert binding is not None
    set_incremental_mechanism(
        db,
        category_key="space_image",
        workflow_version_id=versions[1].id,
        expected_lock_version=binding.lock_version,
        reason="测试第二条冻结车道",
        actor="test",
    )
    db.commit()
    second_payload = _payload(event_id="evt-two-lanes-2", asset_id=second_asset.id)
    second_payload["occurred_at"] = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        13,
        0,
        0,
        1,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    ).isoformat()
    second_payload["payload"]["content_id"] = "asset-2"
    second = client.post(
        "/api/content-ingress/events",
        headers=headers,
        json=second_payload,
    )
    assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
    assert second.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"

    packages = db.scalars(
        select(MaterialPackage).order_by(MaterialPackage.id.asc())
    ).all()
    assert len(packages) == 2
    assert packages[0].incremental_lane_key != packages[1].incremental_lane_key
    due_at = max(package.incremental_due_at for package in packages)
    assert due_at is not None
    if due_at.tzinfo is None:
        due_at = due_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return engine, db, packages, due_at + timedelta(seconds=1)


def _backlogged_incremental_lanes(
    monkeypatch,
    *,
    lane_sizes: tuple[int, ...],
):
    """Queue batch-size-one lanes without letting request ingress flush them."""

    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    versions = db.scalars(
        select(WorkflowVersion).order_by(WorkflowVersion.id.asc())
    ).all()
    assert len(versions) >= len(lane_sizes)
    for version in versions[: len(lane_sizes)]:
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy["incremental_autorun"] = {
            "enabled": True,
            "batch_size": 1,
            "max_wait_seconds": 0,
            "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
        }
        version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        version.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(version)).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
    db.commit()

    original_backoff = incremental_autorun._active_lane_backoff
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        incremental_autorun,
        "_active_lane_backoff",
        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: (
            datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=5),
            1,
        ),
    )
    sequence = 0
    try:
        for lane_index, lane_size in enumerate(lane_sizes):
            if lane_index:
                binding = db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding))
                assert binding is not None
                set_incremental_mechanism(
                    db,
                    category_key="space_image",
                    workflow_version_id=versions[lane_index].id,
                    expected_lock_version=binding.lock_version,
                    reason=f"测试积压车道 {lane_index + 1}",
                    actor="test",
                )
                db.commit()
            for _ in range(lane_size):
                sequence += 1
                payload = _payload(
                    event_id=f"evt-backlog-{lane_index + 1}-{sequence}",
                    asset_id=asset.id,
                )
                payload["payload"]["content_id"] = (
                    f"backlog-{lane_index + 1}-{sequence}"
                )
                payload["occurred_at"] = (
                    datetime(2026, 8, 13, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                    + timedelta(seconds=sequence)
                ).isoformat()
                response = client.post(
                    "/api/content-ingress/events",
                    headers=headers,
                    json=payload,
                )
                assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
                assert response.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
    finally:
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            incremental_autorun,
            "_active_lane_backoff",
            original_backoff,
        )

    packages = db.scalars(
        select(MaterialPackage).order_by(MaterialPackage.id.asc())
    ).all()
    assert len(packages) == sum(lane_sizes)
    lane_keys = list(dict.fromkeys(str(item.incremental_lane_key) for item in packages))
    assert len(lane_keys) == len(lane_sizes)
    due_at = max(item.incremental_due_at for item in packages)
    assert due_at is not None
    if due_at.tzinfo is None:
        due_at = due_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return engine, db, lane_keys, due_at + timedelta(seconds=1)


def test_worker_tick_drains_batch_size_one_backlog_to_global_cap(monkeypatch) -> None:
    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, lane_keys, now = _backlogged_incremental_lanes(
        monkeypatch,
        lane_sizes=(7,),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        incremental_autorun,
        "MAX_INCREMENTAL_RUNS_PER_TICK",
        5,
        raising=False,
    )
    try:
        dispatched = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=now,
        )
        db.commit()

        assert len(dispatched) == 5
        statuses = db.scalars(
            select(MaterialPackage.status).where(
                MaterialPackage.incremental_lane_key == lane_keys[0]
            )
        ).all()
        assert statuses.count("incremental_sent") == 5
        assert statuses.count("incremental_pending") == 2
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_worker_tick_round_robins_backlogged_lanes_under_global_cap(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, lane_keys, now = _backlogged_incremental_lanes(
        monkeypatch,
        lane_sizes=(3, 3),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        incremental_autorun,
        "MAX_INCREMENTAL_RUNS_PER_TICK",
        4,
        raising=False,
    )
    try:
        dispatched = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=now,
        )
        db.commit()

        assert len(dispatched) == 4
        sent_by_lane = {
            lane_key: db.scalar(
                select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id)).where(
                    MaterialPackage.incremental_lane_key == lane_key,
                    MaterialPackage.status == "incremental_sent",
                )
            )
            for lane_key in lane_keys
        }
        assert sent_by_lane == {lane_keys[0]: 2, lane_keys[1]: 2}
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_worker_tick_quarantines_poison_lane_and_dispatches_healthy_lane(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, packages, now = _two_waiting_incremental_lanes(monkeypatch)
    first_package_id = packages[0].id
    original_snapshot = incremental_autorun._mechanism_snapshot

    def poison_first_lane(package):
        if package.id == first_package_id:
            raise incremental_autorun.IncrementalAutorunError(
                "incremental_snapshot_invalid",
                "测试毒化车道",
            )
        return original_snapshot(package)

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        incremental_autorun,
        "_mechanism_snapshot",
        poison_first_lane,
    )
    try:
        dispatched = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=now,
        )
        db.commit()

        db.expire_all()
        refreshed = [db.get(MaterialPackage, package.id) for package in packages]
        assert len(dispatched) == 1
        assert refreshed[0] is not None and refreshed[0].status == "incremental_blocked"
        assert refreshed[1] is not None and refreshed[1].status == "incremental_sent"
        audit = db.scalar(
            select(AuditEvent).where(
                AuditEvent.action == "incremental_lane_blocked"
            )
        )
        assert audit is not None
        payload = json.loads(audit.payload_json)
        assert payload["lane_key"] == packages[0].incremental_lane_key
        assert payload["error_code"] == "incremental_snapshot_invalid"
        assert "测试毒化车道" not in audit.payload_json
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_worker_tick_retries_transient_lane_after_configuration_recovers(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, packages, now = _two_waiting_incremental_lanes(monkeypatch)
    first_package_id = packages[0].id
    original_snapshot = incremental_autorun._mechanism_snapshot

    def unavailable_secret(package):
        if package.id == first_package_id:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "model_secret_unavailable",
                "测试临时密钥不可用",
                status_code=409,
            )
        return original_snapshot(package)

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        incremental_autorun,
        "_mechanism_snapshot",
        unavailable_secret,
    )
    try:
        first_tick = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=now,
        )
        db.commit()

        db.expire_all()
        first_package = db.get(MaterialPackage, packages[0].id)
        second_package = db.get(MaterialPackage, packages[1].id)
        assert len(first_tick) == 1
        assert first_package is not None and first_package.status == (
            "incremental_pending"
        )
        assert second_package is not None and second_package.status == (
            "incremental_sent"
        )
        deferred = db.scalar(
            select(AuditEvent).where(
                AuditEvent.action == "incremental_lane_deferred"
            )
        )
        assert deferred is not None
        deferred_payload = json.loads(deferred.payload_json)
        assert deferred_payload["error_code"] == "model_secret_unavailable"
        assert "测试临时密钥不可用" not in deferred.payload_json

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            incremental_autorun,
            "_mechanism_snapshot",
            original_snapshot,
        )
        second_tick = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=now + timedelta(seconds=5),
        )
        db.commit()

        db.expire_all()
        recovered_package = db.get(MaterialPackage, packages[0].id)
        assert len(second_tick) == 1
        assert recovered_package is not None
        assert recovered_package.status == "incremental_sent"
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 2
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_transient_lane_backoff_does_not_starve_next_eligible_lane(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, packages, now = _two_waiting_incremental_lanes(monkeypatch)
    first_package_id = packages[0].id
    original_snapshot = incremental_autorun._mechanism_snapshot

    def unavailable_secret(package):
        if package.id == first_package_id:
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "model_secret_unavailable",
                "test transient provider secret outage",
                status_code=409,
            )
        return original_snapshot(package)

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        incremental_autorun,
        "MAX_INCREMENTAL_LANES_PER_TICK",
        1,
        raising=False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        incremental_autorun,
        "_mechanism_snapshot",
        unavailable_secret,
    )
    try:
        first_tick = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=now,
        )
        db.commit()

        db.expire_all()
        deferred = db.get(MaterialPackage, packages[0].id)
        healthy = db.get(MaterialPackage, packages[1].id)
        assert first_tick == ()
        assert deferred is not None
        assert deferred.status == "incremental_pending"
        assert deferred.incremental_failure_count == 1
        assert deferred.incremental_retry_at is not None
        assert deferred.incremental_retry_at > now.replace(tzinfo=None)
        assert healthy is not None
        assert healthy.status == "incremental_pending"
        assert healthy.incremental_retry_at is None

        second_tick = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=now + timedelta(seconds=1),
        )
        db.commit()

        db.expire_all()
        deferred = db.get(MaterialPackage, packages[0].id)
        healthy = db.get(MaterialPackage, packages[1].id)
        assert len(second_tick) == 1
        assert deferred is not None
        assert deferred.status == "incremental_pending"
        assert healthy is not None
        assert healthy.status == "incremental_sent"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_new_ingress_inherits_active_lane_backoff_without_hot_retry(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    original_snapshot = incremental_autorun._mechanism_snapshot
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        assert version is not None
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy["incremental_autorun"] = {
            "enabled": True,
            "batch_size": 2,
            "max_wait_seconds": 3_600,
            "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
        }
        version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        db.commit()

        first = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=_payload(event_id="evt-backoff-1", asset_id=asset.id),
        )
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert first.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        first_package = db.get(
            MaterialPackage,
            first.json()["material_package_id"],
        )
        assert first_package is not None
        due_at = first_package.incremental_due_at
        assert due_at is not None
        if due_at.tzinfo is None:
            due_at = due_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        defer_now = due_at + timedelta(seconds=1)

        def unavailable_secret(_package):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "model_secret_unavailable",
                "test temporary secret outage",
                status_code=409,
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            incremental_autorun,
            "_mechanism_snapshot",
            unavailable_secret,
        )
        assert dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=defer_now,
        ) == ()
        db.commit()
        db.refresh(first_package)
        retry_at = first_package.incremental_retry_at
        assert retry_at is not None

        second_payload = _payload(
            event_id="evt-backoff-2",
            asset_id=asset.id,
        )
        second_payload["payload"]["content_id"] = "asset-backoff-2"
        second_payload["occurred_at"] = datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            13,
            0,
            0,
            2,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ).isoformat()
        second = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=second_payload,
        )

        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        assert second.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        assert second.json()["writes_evaluation_job"] is False
        packages = db.scalars(
            select(MaterialPackage).order_by(MaterialPackage.id)
        ).all()
        assert len(packages) == 2
        assert {item.status for item in packages} == {"incremental_pending"}
        assert {item.incremental_failure_count for item in packages} == {1}
        assert {item.incremental_retry_at for item in packages} == {retry_at}
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            incremental_autorun,
            "_mechanism_snapshot",
            original_snapshot,
        )
        retry_at_utc = (
            retry_at
            if retry_at.tzinfo is not None
            else retry_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        )
        recovered = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=retry_at_utc + timedelta(seconds=1),
        )
        db.commit()

        assert len(recovered) == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 2
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_immediate_transient_failure_preserves_ingress_for_periodic_recovery(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    original_plan = incremental_autorun._real_execution_plan
    try:
        _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch, batch_size=1)

        def unavailable_plan(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "model_secret_unavailable",
                "test temporary secret outage",
                status_code=409,
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            incremental_autorun,
            "_real_execution_plan",
            unavailable_plan,
        )
        response = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=_payload(event_id="evt-immediate-defer", asset_id=asset.id),
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        package = db.get(MaterialPackage, response.json()["material_package_id"])
        assert package is not None
        assert package.status == "incremental_pending"
        assert package.incremental_failure_count == 1
        assert package.incremental_retry_at is not None
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            incremental_autorun,
            "_real_execution_plan",
            original_plan,
        )
        retry_at = package.incremental_retry_at
        retry_at_utc = (
            retry_at
            if retry_at.tzinfo is not None
            else retry_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        )
        recovered = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=retry_at_utc + timedelta(seconds=1),
        )
        db.commit()

        assert len(recovered) == 1
        db.refresh(package)
        assert package.status == "incremental_sent"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_expired_lane_backoff_is_extended_when_new_ingress_still_fails(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch, batch_size=1)

        def unavailable_plan(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise WorkflowRuntimeError(
                "model_secret_unavailable",
                "test continuing secret outage",
                status_code=409,
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            incremental_autorun,
            "_real_execution_plan",
            unavailable_plan,
        )
        first = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=_payload(event_id="evt-expired-defer-1", asset_id=asset.id),
        )
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        first_package = db.get(
            MaterialPackage,
            first.json()["material_package_id"],
        )
        assert first_package is not None
        first_package.incremental_retry_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(
            seconds=1
        )
        db.commit()

        second_payload = _payload(
            event_id="evt-expired-defer-2",
            asset_id=asset.id,
        )
        second_payload["payload"]["content_id"] = "expired-defer-2"
        second_payload["occurred_at"] = datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            13,
            0,
            0,
            2,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ).isoformat()
        second = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=second_payload,
        )

        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        assert second.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        packages = db.scalars(
            select(MaterialPackage).order_by(MaterialPackage.id)
        ).all()
        assert len(packages) == 2
        assert {item.status for item in packages} == {"incremental_pending"}
        assert {item.incremental_failure_count for item in packages} == {2}
        assert all(
            item.incremental_retry_at is not None
            and (
                item.incremental_retry_at
                if item.incremental_retry_at.tzinfo is not None
                else item.incremental_retry_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            )
            > datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            for item in packages
        )
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_pending_replay_uses_frozen_category_after_projection_category_changes(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        assert version is not None
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy["incremental_autorun"] = {
            "enabled": True,
            "batch_size": 2,
            "max_wait_seconds": 3_600,
            "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
        }
        version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        db.commit()

        payload = _payload(event_id="evt-frozen-category", asset_id=asset.id)
        first = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=payload,
        )
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert first.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        package = db.get(MaterialPackage, first.json()["material_package_id"])
        event = db.scalar(
            select(ContentIngressEvent).where(
                ContentIngressEvent.event_id == "evt-frozen-category"
            )
        )
        assert package is not None and event is not None
        record = db.get(ContentRecord, event.content_record_id)
        assert record is not None
        record.category_key = "model_3d"
        package.incremental_failure_count = 1
        package.incremental_retry_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(
            minutes=5
        )
        db.commit()

        replay = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=payload,
        )

        assert replay.status_code == 200, replay.text
        assert replay.json()["duplicate"] is True
        assert replay.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        db.refresh(package)
        assert package.category_key == "space_image"
        assert package.incremental_failure_count == 1
        assert package.incremental_retry_at is not None
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

            db,

        )

def test_content_ingress_dispatches_one_idempotent_runtime_run(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    payload = _payload(event_id="evt-autorun", asset_id=asset.id)
    try:
        first = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=payload,
        )
        second = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=payload,
        )

        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        first_body = first.json()
        second_body = second.json()
        assert first_body["routing_status"] == "dispatched"
        assert first_body["writes_evaluation_job"] is True
        assert first_body["production_run_key"].startswith("run-")
        assert first_body["batch_key"].startswith("content-ingress-batch:")
        assert second_body["duplicate"] is True
        assert second_body["production_run_key"] == first_body["production_run_key"]
        assert second_body["batch_key"] == first_body["batch_key"]

        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(MaterialPackage.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeDispatchItem.id))) == 1
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run is not None
        assert run.source_type == "content_ingress_event"
        frozen = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        context = frozen["runtime_context"]
        assert context["run_mode"] == "incremental"
        assert context["content_ingress_event_id"] == "evt-autorun"
        assert context["batch_trigger_reason"] == "batch_size"
        assert context["input_manifest"]["asset_id"] == asset.id
        assert context["input_manifest"]["asset_sha256"] == asset.sha256
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_content_ingress_payload_drift_remains_a_conflict(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        first_payload = _payload(event_id="evt-autorun-drift", asset_id=asset.id)
        changed_payload = _payload(event_id="evt-autorun-drift", asset_id=asset.id)
        changed_payload["payload"]["content_version"] = "v2"

        first = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=first_payload,
        )
        second = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=changed_payload,
        )

        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert second.status_code == 409, second.text
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_v91_legacy_package_replay_routes_to_review_without_dispatch(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    payload = _payload(event_id="evt-v91-legacy-replay", asset_id=asset.id)
    try:
        first = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=payload,
        )
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        package = db.get(MaterialPackage, first.json()["material_package_id"])
        assert package is not None
        legacy_snapshot = json.loads(package.mechanism_snapshot_json)
        legacy_snapshot.pop("input_manifest", None)
        legacy_snapshot.pop("input_manifest_hash", None)
        db.execute(text("DROP TRIGGER trg_material_packages_no_metadata_update"))
        db.execute(
            text(
                "UPDATE material_packages "
                "SET mechanism_snapshot_json=:snapshot, "
                "status='incremental_legacy', incremental_event_id=NULL, "
                "incremental_snapshot_hash=NULL, incremental_lane_key=NULL, "
                "incremental_batch_size=NULL, incremental_due_at=NULL "
                "WHERE id=:package_id"
            ),
            {
                "snapshot": json.dumps(legacy_snapshot, ensure_ascii=False),
                "package_id": package.id,
            },
        )
        db.commit()
        db.expire_all()

        replay = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=payload,
        )

        assert replay.status_code == 200, replay.text
        assert replay.json()["routing_status"] == "legacy_snapshot_review"
        assert replay.json()["writes_evaluation_job"] is False
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_lane_batch_threshold_dispatches_all_waiting_events(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        assert version is not None
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy["incremental_autorun"] = {
            "enabled": True,
            "batch_size": 2,
            "max_wait_seconds": 3600,
            "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
        }
        version.resource_policy_json = json.dumps(policy, ensure_ascii=False)
        db.commit()

        first_payload = _payload(event_id="evt-batch-1", asset_id=asset.id)
        second_payload = _payload(event_id="evt-batch-2", asset_id=asset.id)
        second_payload["payload"]["content_id"] = "asset-2"
        second_payload["occurred_at"] = (
            datetime(2026, 8, 13, tzinfo=timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=1)
        ).isoformat()

        first = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=first_payload,
        )
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert first.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        assert first.json()["writes_evaluation_job"] is False
        first_package = db.get(
            MaterialPackage,
            first.json()["material_package_id"],
        )
        assert first_package is not None
        assert first_package.status == "incremental_pending"
        second = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=second_payload,
        )
        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        assert second.json()["routing_status"] == "dispatched"
        assert second.json()["writes_evaluation_job"] is True
        runs = db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).order_by(ProductionRun.id)).all()
        assert len(runs) == 2
        contexts = [json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"] for run in runs]
        assert {item["batch_key"] for item in contexts} == {
            second.json()["batch_key"]
        }
        assert {item["batch_trigger_reason"] for item in contexts} == {
            "batch_size"
        }
        packages = db.scalars(
            select(MaterialPackage).order_by(MaterialPackage.id)
        ).all()
        assert {item.status for item in packages} == {
            "incremental_sent"
        }
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_waiting_event_keeps_its_frozen_asset_when_same_content_advances(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """A v2 projection must never rewrite the queued v1 event input."""

    engine, db, client, asset_v1 = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        assert version is not None
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy["incremental_autorun"] = {
            "enabled": True,
            "batch_size": 2,
            "max_wait_seconds": 3600,
            "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
        }
        version.resource_policy_json = canonical_json(policy)
        asset_v2 = Asset(
            original_name="asset-1-v2.jpg",
            stored_name="asset-1-v2.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=20,
            sha256="b" * 64,
            category_key="space_image",
        )
        db.add(asset_v2)
        db.commit()

        v1 = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=_payload(event_id="evt-version-v1", asset_id=asset_v1.id),
        )
        assert v1.status_code == 200, v1.text
        assert v1.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        package_v1 = db.get(MaterialPackage, v1.json()["material_package_id"])
        assert package_v1 is not None
        frozen_v1 = json.loads(package_v1.mechanism_snapshot_json)
        assert frozen_v1["input_manifest"]["asset_id"] == asset_v1.id
        assert frozen_v1["input_manifest"]["source_version"] == "v1"

        v2_payload = _payload(event_id="evt-version-v2", asset_id=asset_v2.id)
        v2_payload["event_type"] = "content.updated"
        v2_payload["occurred_at"] = datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            13,
            0,
            0,
            1,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ).isoformat()
        v2_payload["payload"]["content_version"] = "v2"
        v2 = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=v2_payload,
        )
        assert v2.status_code == 200, v2.text
        assert v2.json()["routing_status"] == "dispatched"

        manifests_by_event: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
        for run in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all():
            runtime = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]
            manifests_by_event[runtime["content_ingress_event_id"]] = runtime[
                "input_manifest"
            ]
        assert manifests_by_event["evt-version-v1"]["asset_id"] == asset_v1.id
        assert manifests_by_event["evt-version-v1"]["asset_sha256"] == asset_v1.sha256
        assert manifests_by_event["evt-version-v1"]["source_version"] == "v1"
        assert manifests_by_event["evt-version-v2"]["asset_id"] == asset_v2.id
        assert manifests_by_event["evt-version-v2"]["asset_sha256"] == asset_v2.sha256
        assert manifests_by_event["evt-version-v2"]["source_version"] == "v2"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_worker_tick_flushes_lane_at_max_wait(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
        assert version is not None
        policy = json.loads(version.resource_policy_json)
        policy["incremental_autorun"] = {
            "enabled": True,
            "batch_size": 10,
            "max_wait_seconds": 60,
            "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
        }
        version.resource_policy_json = json.dumps(policy, ensure_ascii=False)
        db.commit()
        response = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=_payload(event_id="evt-max-wait", asset_id=asset.id),
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"

        package = db.scalar(select(MaterialPackage))
        assert package is not None
        created_at = package.created_at
        if created_at.tzinfo is None:
            created_at = created_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        now = created_at + timedelta(seconds=61)

        dispatched = dispatch_due_incremental_batches(
            db,
            actor="worker-test",
            now=now,
        )
        db.commit()

        assert len(dispatched) == 1
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run is not None
        context = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]
        assert context["batch_trigger_reason"] == "max_wait"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("shadow,revoked", [(False, False), (True, False), (True, True)])
def test_real_incremental_autorun_freezes_budget_and_can_reserve_a_call(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    shadow,
    revoked,
) -> None:
    """A real incremental run reaches the worker/provider path and completes."""

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        _version, _model = _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch)
        if shadow:
            from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission
            scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=_version.workflow_definition_id)
            policy = configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="本地真实执行分支验收", actor="test")
            set_admission(db, **scope, expected_revision=policy["revision"], paused=False, reason="本地验收", actor="test")
            db.commit()

        image = io.BytesIO()
        Image.new("RGB", (640, 480), (12, 34, 56)).save(image, format="JPEG")
        image_bytes = image.getvalue()
        image_path = tmp_path / "asset-1.jpg"
        image_path.write_bytes(image_bytes)
        asset.stored_name = image_path.name
        asset.size_bytes = len(image_bytes)
        asset.width = 640
        asset.height = 480
        asset.sha256 = hashlib.sha256(image_bytes).hexdigest()
        db.commit()

        response = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=_payload(event_id="evt-real-autorun", asset_id=asset.id),
        )

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        assert run is not None
        assert run.source_type == "content_ingress_event"
        assert run.model_execution_mode == "real"
        snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
        context = snapshot["runtime_context"]
        assert context["run_mode"] == "incremental"
        assert context["real_execution_confirmed"] is True
        assert context["real_execution_authority"] == (
            "frozen_incremental_autorun_policy"
        )
        assert context["real_execution_budget"]["expected_requests"] == 2
        assert set(context["real_execution_node_limits"]) == {
            "prompt_a",
            "prompt_b",
        }

        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 0

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as runtime_worker
        import app.workflow_model_executor as model_executor

        calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []

        class FakeProvider:
            def __init__(self, config):
                self.config = config

            async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                calls.append(
                    {
                        "model_id": self.config.model_id,
                        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                        "options": options,
                    }
                )
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"label": "fixture", "score": 88},
                    raw_text='{"label":"fixture","score":88}',
                    raw_payload={"id": f"req-{len(calls)}"},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id=f"req-{len(calls)}",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=2_048,
                        reasoning_effort=None,
                        thinking_mode="disabled",
                    input_tokens=120,
                    output_tokens=20,
                    total_tokens=140,
                )

        monkeypatch.setattr(runtime_worker, "SessionLocal", lambda: type(db)(bind=engine))
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            model_executor,
            "get_settings",
            lambda: SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path, nas_maps_root=None),
        )
        processed = 0
        if revoked:
            from app.models import IncrementalEntityAssignment
            assignment = db.scalar(select(IncrementalEntityAssignment))
            assignment.ownership_epoch += 1
            db.commit()
        while runtime_worker.process_runtime_step_once(
            "real-incremental-worker",
            model_client_type=FakeProvider,
        ):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 20

        db.expire_all()
        completed = db.get(ProductionRun, run.id)
        assert completed is not None
        if revoked:
            assert completed.status in {"blocked", "failed"}
            assert calls == []
            return
        assert completed.status == "succeeded", (
            completed.error_code,
            completed.error_message,
            calls,
        )
        assert len(calls) == 2
        settled = db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(
                WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run.id
            )
        ).all()
        assert len(settled) == 2
        assert {item.status for item in settled} == {"succeeded"}
        if shadow:
            from app.models import IncrementalShadowResult, RuntimeOutboxEvent
            result = db.scalar(select(IncrementalShadowResult))
            assert result is not None
            assert result.run_key == run.run_key
            assert result.result_hash
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id))) == 0
            from app.incremental_observability import cost_summary
            costs = cost_summary(db, category_key=run.category_key, workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id)
            assert costs["by_destination"]["shadow"]["calls"] == 2
            assert costs["by_destination"]["formal"]["calls"] == 0
            from app.incremental_routing import drain_state
            settled[0].status = "failed"
            settled[0].error_code = "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            db.commit()
            assert drain_state(db, category_key=run.category_key, workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id)["provider_unresolved"] == 1
            unresolved = cost_summary(db, category_key=run.category_key, workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id)["by_destination"]["shadow"]
            assert unresolved["unresolved_calls"] == 1
            assert unresolved["reserved_estimate_micros"] >= settled[0].estimated_cost_micros
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

def test_fully_populated_incremental_request_is_repriced_and_source_unique(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Copied ingress facts cannot inflate paid limits or create a second run."""

    import app.incremental_autorun as incremental_autorun
    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        _enable_real_incremental_policy(
            db,
            monkeypatch,
            batch_size=2,
        )
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            workflow_model_budget,
            "secret_reference_available",
            lambda reference: bool(reference),
            raising=False,
        )
        response = client.post(
            "/api/content-ingress/events",
            headers=headers,
            json=_payload(event_id="evt-forged-full", asset_id=asset.id),
        )
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert response.json()["routing_status"] == "awaiting_batch"
        package = db.get(MaterialPackage, response.json()["material_package_id"])
        event = db.scalar(
            select(ContentIngressEvent).where(
                ContentIngressEvent.event_id == "evt-forged-full"
            )
        )
        assert package is not None and event is not None
        record = db.get(ContentRecord, event.content_record_id)
        assert record is not None
        snapshot = json.loads(package.mechanism_snapshot_json)
        input_manifest = snapshot["input_manifest"]
        input_hash = snapshot["input_manifest_hash"]
        contract_id, contract_key = incremental_autorun._frozen_upstream_contract(
            snapshot
        )
        expected_budget, expected_limits = incremental_autorun._real_execution_plan(
            db,
            snapshot=snapshot,
        )
        forged_budget = {**expected_budget, "max_requests": 999_999}
        forged_limits = json.loads(canonical_json(expected_limits))
        forged_limits["prompt_a"]["max_input_tokens"] = 999_999_999
        runtime_context = {
            "run_mode": "incremental",
            "content_ingress_event_db_id": event.id,
            "content_ingress_event_id": event.event_id,
            "content_ingress_payload_hash": event.payload_hash,
            "content_record_id": record.id,
            "material_package_id": package.id,
            "incremental_mechanism_snapshot_hash": snapshot["snapshot_hash"],
            "upstream_intake_contract_version_id": contract_id,
            "upstream_intake_contract_key": contract_key,
            "upstream_intake_payload_hash": input_hash,
            "batch_key": "content-ingress-batch:manual-security-test",
            "batch_trigger_reason": "batch_size",
            "batch_size": 2,
            "max_wait_seconds": 3_600,
            "real_execution_confirmed": True,
            "real_execution_authority": "frozen_incremental_autorun_policy",
            "real_execution_budget": forged_budget,
            "real_execution_node_limits": forged_limits,
        }
        request = ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=int(snapshot["workflow_version_id"]),
            idempotency_key=incremental_autorun._bounded_idempotency_key(
                event.event_id,
                snapshot["snapshot_hash"],
            ),
            category_key=record.category_key,
            queue_class="production_batch",
            runtime_context=runtime_context,
            input_manifest=input_manifest,
            owner="forged-internal-caller",
            source_type="content_ingress_event",
            source_id=event.id,
            model_execution_mode="real",
        )

        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as forged_input_error:
            create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    **{
                        **request.__dict__,
                        "input_manifest": {
                            **input_manifest,
                            "source_uri": "file:///forged-source.jpg",
                        },
                    }
                ),
                actor="security-test",
            )
        assert forged_input_error.value.code == "content_ingress_binding_mismatch"
        db.rollback()

        run = create_production_run(db, request, actor="security-test")
        frozen_runtime = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]
        assert frozen_runtime["real_execution_budget"] == expected_budget
        assert frozen_runtime["real_execution_node_limits"] == expected_limits

        with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as exc_info:
            create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    **{
                        **request.__dict__,
                        "idempotency_key": "forged-second-run-for-one-event",
                    }
                ),
                actor="security-test",
            )
        assert exc_info.value.code == "content_ingress_run_conflict"
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_incremental_real_reservations_share_a_daily_spend_boundary(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    headers = _headers(monkeypatch)
    try:
        _enable_real_incremental_policy(db, monkeypatch)
        # 2026-09-16 起这道门槛默认关闭；这里显式打开，因为本测试要证的就是
        # 「账本跨 run 共享，新 run 不会把额度重置」——关着时它由下面的累计断言证。
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            workflow_model_budget,
            "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED",
            True,
            raising=False,
        )
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            workflow_model_budget,
            "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS",
            1,
            raising=False,
        )
        run_ids: list[int] = []
        for index in (1, 2):
            payload = _payload(
                event_id=f"evt-daily-cap-{index}",
                asset_id=asset.id,
            )
            payload["payload"]["content_id"] = f"daily-cap-{index}"
            payload["occurred_at"] = (
                datetime(2026, 8, 13, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                + timedelta(seconds=index)
            ).isoformat()
            response = client.post(
                "/api/content-ingress/events",
                headers=headers,
                json=payload,
            )
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
            run = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionRun).where(
                    ProductionRun.run_key == response.json()["production_run_key"]
                )
            )
            assert run is not None
            run_ids.append(run.id)

        def reservation(run_id: int):
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert run is not None
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "prompt_a",
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None
            node = next(
                item
                for item in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
                if item["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a"
            )
            return run, snapshot, attempt, node

        first_run, first_snapshot, first_attempt, first_node = reservation(run_ids[0])
        reserve_model_invocation(
            db,
            attempt_id=first_attempt.id,
            node=first_node,
            run_snapshot=first_snapshot,
        )
        second_run, second_snapshot, second_attempt, second_node = reservation(
            run_ids[1]
        )
        with pytest.raises(workflow_model_budget.WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc_info:
            reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=second_attempt.id,
                node=second_node,
                run_snapshot=second_snapshot,
            )
        assert exc_info.value.code == "incremental_daily_requests_exhausted"
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 1
        assert first_run.id != second_run.id

        # 门槛关掉后，跨 run 共享这件事本身不变：第二个 run 照样记进同一天的账本，
        # 只是不再被拒。这条是取消门槛之后仍然成立的那一半。
        from app.models import WorkflowModelDailyBudget

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            workflow_model_budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", False)
        reserve_model_invocation(
            db,
            attempt_id=second_attempt.id,
            node=second_node,
            run_snapshot=second_snapshot,
        )
        ledgers = db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget)).all()
        assert len(ledgers) == 1, "两个 run 必须共用同一天的一本账"
        assert ledgers[0].request_count == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowModelInvocation.id))) == 2
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_campaign_20260909.py =====
"""Campaign selection must preserve frozen truth and short-circuit coverage."""
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/accept-incremental-campaign-20260909.py'

def module():
    assert PATH.exists(), 'incremental campaign harness is missing'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('incremental_campaign', PATH)
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod

def rows():
    return [dict(item_id=i, asset_id=i, truth_hash=str(i)*64,
                 grade='A' if i % 2 else 'B', branch='skipped' if i == 29 else 'succeeded')
            for i in range(1, 31)]

def test_selection_is_order_independent_unique_and_covers_short_circuit():
    mod = module()
    picked = mod.select_samples(rows(), count=20, require_short_circuit=True)
    assert picked == mod.select_samples(list(reversed(rows())), count=20, require_short_circuit=True)
    assert len(picked) == len({r['asset_id'] for r in picked}) == 20
    assert any(r['branch'] == 'skipped' for r in picked)
    assert {r['grade'] for r in picked} == {'A', 'B'}
    assert all(r in rows() for r in picked)

def test_selection_rejects_missing_short_circuit_and_insufficient_unique_assets():
    mod = module()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='short.circuit'):
        mod.select_samples(rows()[:20], count=20, require_short_circuit=True)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='unique'):
        mod.select_samples([dict(r, asset_id=1) for r in rows()], count=20)

def test_frozen_selection_rejects_changed_truth_or_workflow():
    mod = module()
    frozen = {'workflow_hash': 'a'*64, 'samples': rows()[:2]}
    mod.validate_frozen(frozen, workflow_hash='a'*64, candidates=rows())
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='workflow'):
        mod.validate_frozen(frozen, workflow_hash='b'*64, candidates=rows())
    changed = rows()
    changed[0]['truth_hash'] = 'c'*64
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='truth'):
        mod.validate_frozen(frozen, workflow_hash='a'*64, candidates=changed)

def test_fixture_result_cannot_claim_quality_or_formal_version_acceptance():
    mod = module()
    result = mod.fixture_summary(0)
    assert result['passed'] is True
    assert result['formal_versions_validated'] is False
    assert result['quality_accuracy'] is None
    assert mod.fixture_summary(1)['passed'] is False

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_capacity.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path
from threading import Thread

import pytest

from app.incremental_capacity import (
    ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION,
    IngressBudget,
    build_capacity_report,
    execute_actual_ingress,
)


def _record(second: int, kind: str, **values: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "observed_at": datetime(2026, 9, 6, 8, 0, second, tzinfo=timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "evidence_kind": "actual_e2e",
        "category_key": "inspiration_image",
        "workflow_key": "legacy_review",
        "event_kind": kind,
        **values,
    }


def test_report_calculates_latency_rates_backlog_and_evidence_denominators() -> None:
    records = [
        _record(0, "arrival", event_id="e1", backlog=1, oldest_backlog_age_seconds=1),
        _record(1, "arrival", event_id="e2", backlog=2, oldest_backlog_age_seconds=2),
        _record(2, "completion", event_id="e1", latency_ms=100, backlog=1, fanout=2, cost_micros=30, destination="shadow"),
        _record(3, "completion", event_id="e2", latency_ms=300, backlog=0, fanout=4, cost_micros=50, destination="formal"),
        _record(3, "invocation", event_id="e1"),
        _record(3, "invocation", event_id="e2"),
        _record(3, "invocation", event_id="e1-retry"),
        _record(3, "invocation", event_id="e2-retry"),
        _record(4, "error", error_kind="429"),
        _record(5, "error", error_kind="timeout"),
        _record(6, "retry"),
        _record(7, "readback", success=False),
        _record(8, "readback", success=True),
        _record(9, "drained", drain_seconds=7),
        _record(9, "outbox", success=False),
        _record(9, "outbox", success=True),
    ]

    report = build_capacity_report(records, workload={"peak_arrivals_per_second": 1}, sla={"p95_ms": 500})
    lane = report["lanes"][0]

    assert lane["arrivals"] == 2
    assert lane["completions"] == 2
    assert lane["arrival_rate_per_second"] == pytest.approx(2 / 9)
    assert lane["completion_rate_per_second"] == pytest.approx(2 / 9)
    assert lane["latency_ms"] == {"sample_count": 2, "p95": 290.0, "p99": 298.0}
    assert lane["backlog"]["slope_per_second"] == pytest.approx(-1 / 3)
    assert lane["backlog"]["oldest_age_seconds_max"] == 2
    assert lane["errors"]["rate_limited_429"] == {"count": 1, "denominator": 4, "rate": 0.25}
    assert lane["errors"]["timeouts"] == {"count": 1, "denominator": 4, "rate": 0.25}
    assert lane["retries"] == {"count": 1, "completion_denominator": 2, "amplification": 0.5}
    assert lane["fanout"] == {"sample_count": 2, "total": 6, "mean": 3.0, "p50": 3.0, "p95": 3.9, "max": 4}
    assert lane["readback"] == {"attempts": 2, "failures": 1, "failure_rate": 0.5}
    assert lane["outbox"] == {"attempts": 2, "failures": 1, "failure_rate": 0.5}
    assert lane["drain_seconds"] == {"sample_count": 1, "max": 7.0}
    assert lane["cost_micros"] == {"verified": 80, "estimated": 0, "unknown_records": 0, "by_destination": {"formal": {"verified": 50, "estimated": 0}, "shadow": {"verified": 30, "estimated": 0}}}
    assert report["capacity_accepted"] is False
    assert "sla_contract_incomplete" in report["acceptance_blockers"]


def test_report_keeps_missing_evidence_unknown_and_separates_synthetic() -> None:
    synthetic = {
        **_record(0, "arrival", event_id="s1"),
        "evidence_kind": "synthetic",
    }
    report = build_capacity_report([synthetic])
    lane = report["lanes"][0]

    assert lane["latency_ms"] is None
    assert lane["errors"]["rate_limited_429"] is None
    assert lane["readback"] is None
    assert lane["cost_micros"] is None
    assert report["evidence_summary"] == {"synthetic": 1, "actual_e2e": 0}
    assert report["capacity_accepted"] is False
    assert "actual_e2e_evidence_missing" in report["acceptance_blockers"]
    assert "workload_contract_missing" in report["acceptance_blockers"]
    assert "sla_contract_missing" in report["acceptance_blockers"]


def test_report_never_accepts_truncated_observer_evidence() -> None:
    report = build_capacity_report([{**_record(0, "arrival", event_id="e1"), "observer_truncated": True}])
    assert report["capacity_accepted"] is False
    assert "observer_evidence_truncated" in report["acceptance_blockers"]


def test_actual_ingress_requires_exact_confirmation_and_respects_request_budget(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    received: list[dict[str, object]] = []

    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
            length = int(self.headers["content-length"])
            received.append(json.loads(self.rfile.read(length)))
            self.send_response(202)
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(b'{"accepted":true}')

        def log_message(self, *_args: object) -> None:
            pass

    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
    thread = Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    event_file = tmp_path / "events.jsonl"
    event_file.write_text('\n'.join(json.dumps({"event_id": f"e{i}", "estimated_cost_micros": 1}) for i in range(3)), encoding="utf-8")
    endpoint = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}/exact-ingress"
    try:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="confirmation"):
            execute_actual_ingress(event_file, endpoint, IngressBudget(2, 1), confirmation="yes")
        result = execute_actual_ingress(
            event_file,
            endpoint,
            IngressBudget(max_requests=2, max_cost_micros=3),
            confirmation=ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION,
        )
    finally:
        server.shutdown()
        thread.join()

    assert result["requests_attempted"] == 2
    assert result["stopped_reason"] == "request_budget_reached"
    assert [item["event_id"] for item in received] == ["e0", "e1"]
    assert result["spending_limit_enforced"] is False
    assert result["cost_limit_kind"] == "declared_event_estimates"


def test_actual_ingress_rejects_non_loopback_and_nonpositive_limits(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    event_file = tmp_path / "events.jsonl"
    event_file.write_text('{"event_id":"e1"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="loopback"):
        execute_actual_ingress(event_file, "https://production.example/ingress", IngressBudget(1, 1), confirmation=ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="max_requests"):
        IngressBudget(0, 0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="max_cost_micros"):
        IngressBudget(1, 0)


def _complete_contracts() -> tuple[dict[str, object], dict[str, object]]:
    workload = {
        "average_arrivals_per_day": 100,
        "peak_arrivals_per_second": 1,
        "burst_duration_seconds": 1,
        "case_image_distribution": {"p50": 2, "p95": 4, "max": 6},
        "expected_lanes": [{"category_key": "inspiration_image", "workflow_key": "legacy_review", "input_kind": "case"}],
    }
    sla = {
        "minimum_completed_events": 2,
        "p95_ms": 500,
        "p99_ms": 600,
        "max_429_rate": 0.1,
        "max_timeout_rate": 0.1,
        "max_retry_amplification": 0.2,
        "max_outbox_failure_rate": 0.1,
        "max_readback_failure_rate": 0.1,
        "max_backlog_slope_per_second": 0,
        "recovery_window_seconds": 10,
    }
    return workload, sla


def test_report_accepts_only_complete_matching_peak_and_recovery_evidence() -> None:
    workload, sla = _complete_contracts()
    records = [
        _record(0, "arrival", event_id="e1", backlog=2, oldest_backlog_age_seconds=1),
        _record(1, "arrival", event_id="e2", backlog=1, oldest_backlog_age_seconds=2),
        _record(1, "invocation", event_id="e1"),
        _record(2, "invocation", event_id="e2"),
        _record(2, "completion", event_id="e1", latency_ms=100, fanout=2, cost_micros=1),
        _record(3, "completion", event_id="e2", latency_ms=200, fanout=6, backlog=0, cost_micros=1),
        _record(3, "readback", success=True), _record(3, "readback", success=True),
        _record(3, "outbox", success=True), _record(3, "outbox", success=True),
        _record(3, "drained", drain_seconds=3),
    ]
    report = build_capacity_report(records, workload=workload, sla=sla)
    assert report["capacity_accepted"] is True
    assert report["acceptance_blockers"] == []
    assert report["lanes"][0]["errors"]["rate_limited_429"]["denominator"] == 2


@pytest.mark.parametrize("missing", ["p99_ms", "max_429_rate", "max_readback_failure_rate", "recovery_window_seconds"])
def test_report_rejects_incomplete_sla_contract(missing: str) -> None:
    workload, sla = _complete_contracts()
    del sla[missing]
    report = build_capacity_report([_record(0, "arrival", event_id="e1")], workload=workload, sla=sla)
    assert report["capacity_accepted"] is False
    assert "sla_contract_incomplete" in report["acceptance_blockers"]


def test_report_rejects_mismatched_ids_missing_invocations_and_underattained_peak() -> None:
    workload, sla = _complete_contracts()
    records = [
        _record(0, "arrival", event_id="arrival-only", backlog=1),
        _record(10, "completion", event_id="different", latency_ms=10, backlog=0),
        _record(10, "readback", success=True), _record(10, "outbox", success=True),
        _record(10, "drained", drain_seconds=1),
    ]
    report = build_capacity_report(records, workload=workload, sla=sla)
    assert {"event_identity_mismatch", "invocation_evidence_missing", "peak_load_not_attained"} <= set(report["acceptance_blockers"])

def test_actual_ingress_rejects_missing_or_zero_declared_estimate_before_io(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    missing = tmp_path / "missing.jsonl"
    missing.write_text('{"event_id":"e1"}\n', encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="estimated_cost_micros"):
        execute_actual_ingress(missing, "http://127.0.0.1:1/ingress", IngressBudget(1, 1), confirmation=ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION)
    zero = tmp_path / "zero.jsonl"

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="positive"):
