def test_actual_ingress_bounded_concurrency_auth_and_exact_cap(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    state = {"in_flight": 0, "max_in_flight": 0}
    received: list[str] = []
    lock = __import__("threading").Lock()

    class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def do_POST(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
            assert self.headers.get("Authorization") == "Bearer local-secret"
            with lock:
                state["in_flight"] += 1
                state["max_in_flight"] = max(state["max_in_flight"], state["in_flight"])
            time.sleep(.03)
            with lock:
                received.append(json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["content-length"]))) ["event_id"])
                state["in_flight"] -= 1
            self.send_response(202); self.end_headers()
        def log_message(self, *_args: object) -> None: pass

    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
    thread = Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True); thread.start()
    event_file = tmp_path / "events.jsonl"
    event_file.write_text("\n".join(json.dumps({"event_id": f"e{i}", "estimated_cost_micros": 1}) for i in range(6)), encoding="utf-8")
    try:
        result = execute_actual_ingress(event_file, f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_port}/ingress",
            IngressBudget(4, 10), confirmation=ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION, auth_token="local-secret", max_concurrency=3)
    finally:
        server.shutdown(); thread.join()
    assert result["requests_attempted"] == 4
    assert sorted(received) == ["e0", "e1", "e2", "e3"]
    assert 2 <= state["max_in_flight"] <= 3
    assert "local-secret" not in json.dumps(result)
    assert result["authentication"] == "bearer_from_environment"


def test_actual_ingress_rejects_unsafe_auth_and_concurrency(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    event_file = tmp_path / "events.jsonl"
    event_file.write_text('{"event_id":"e1","estimated_cost_micros":1}\n', encoding="utf-8")
    for token in ("bad\ntoken", "bad\rtoken"):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="auth_token"):
            execute_actual_ingress(event_file, "http://127.0.0.1:1/x", IngressBudget(1, 1), confirmation=ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION, auth_token=token)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="max_concurrency"):
        execute_actual_ingress(event_file, "http://127.0.0.1:1/x", IngressBudget(1, 1), confirmation=ACTUAL_CONFIRMATION, max_concurrency=33)


def test_report_cli_is_read_only_by_default_and_records_source(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    evidence = tmp_path / "evidence.jsonl"
    evidence.write_text(json.dumps({**_record(0, "arrival"), "evidence_kind": "synthetic"}) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "scripts/measure_incremental_capacity.py", "report", "--evidence", str(evidence)],
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    report = json.loads(completed.stdout)
    assert report["sources"] == [str(evidence.resolve())]
    assert report["capacity_accepted"] is False
    assert report["execution_performed"] is False

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_capacity_feed.py =====
"""The capacity fixture must use real incremental creation, not baseline rows."""
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import json
import pytest

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker


@pytest.mark.parametrize('rate', [1, 2])
def test_incremental_feed_creates_only_offered_events_and_runs_native_steps(tmp_path, monkeypatch, rate):
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'scripts/incremental_capacity_feed.py'
    assert path.exists(), 'incremental capacity fixture missing'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('inc_capacity', path)
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    from app.database import Base
    from app.migrations import run_migrations
    from app.models import ProductionRun, BaselineRegressionRun, WorkflowModelInvocation, RuntimeOutboxEvent
    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    import app.workflow_model_executor as executor
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path/"fixture.sqlite"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with engine.begin() as c: run_migrations(c)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    args = SimpleNamespace(items=2, slots=4, timeout=60)
    feed = m.prepare(sessions, tmp_path, args, monkeypatch)
    feed.rate = rate
    from sqlalchemy import event
    commits = []
    event.listen(sessions, 'after_commit', lambda session: commits.append(1)
                 if not session.in_nested_transaction() else None)
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(ProductionRun)) == 0
    assert feed.release_tick() == rate
    assert len(commits) == rate, 'each real ingress request must commit independently'
    with sessions() as db:
        runs = list(db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)))
        assert len(runs)==rate
        assert runs[0].source_type == 'content_ingress_event'
        assert runs[0].queue_class == 'production_batch'
        assert json.loads(runs[0].snapshot_json)['runtime_context']['run_mode']=='incremental'
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineRegressionRun))==0
    assert feed.release_tick()==2-rate
    assert feed.release_tick()==0
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker,'SessionLocal',sessions)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor,'SessionLocal',sessions)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor,'get_settings',lambda:SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path/'images',nas_maps_root=None))
    class FakeProvider:
        def __init__(self,config):self.config=config
        async def chat_json(self,*a,**k):
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed={'label':'fixture','score':88},raw_text='{"label":"fixture","score":88}',
                raw_payload={'id':'synthetic'},upstream_status_code=200,request_correlation_id='synthetic',attempt_count=1,
                output_budget=k['output_budget'],reasoning_effort=k['reasoning_effort'],thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode,
                input_tokens=200,output_tokens=30,total_tokens=230)
    processed=0
    while worker.process_runtime_step_once('fixture',model_client_type=FakeProvider):
        processed+=1
        assert processed<=22
    with sessions() as db:
        assert [r.status for r in db.scalars(select(ProductionRun))]==['succeeded','succeeded']
        assert processed==22
        calls=list(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation)))
        assert len(calls)==4 and all(c.status=='succeeded' for c in calls)
        assert sum(c.actual_cost_micros for c in calls)==1040
        assert all((c.reserved_input_tokens, c.reserved_output_tokens, c.estimated_cost_micros)
                   == (12000, 2048, 16096) for c in calls)
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent))==2
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_capacity_observer.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

from sqlalchemy import select

from app.incremental_capacity_observer import _e2e_completion_timing, _paid_call_retry_flags, observe_capacity_records
from app.models import ContentIngressEvent, IncrementalEntityAssignment, IncrementalShadowResult, ProductionRun, WorkflowVersion
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _close, _fixture, _headers, _payload
from scripts.measure_incremental_capacity import parser


def test_observer_emits_source_backed_arrival_and_only_verified_shadow_completion(monkeypatch) -> None:
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission

    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        definition_id = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        policy = configure_policy(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition_id,
            expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="观察", actor="test")
        set_admission(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition_id,
            expected_revision=policy["revision"], paused=False, reason="观察", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        assert client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch),
            json=_payload(event_id="observe-1", asset_id=asset.id)).status_code == 200
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        run.status = "succeeded"
        run.model_execution_mode = "real"
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id)
        run.started_at = event.received_at + timedelta(seconds=3)
        run.finished_at = run.started_at + timedelta(seconds=2)
        db.commit()

        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        first = observe_capacity_records(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition_id,
            since=now - timedelta(days=1), until=now + timedelta(days=1), max_rows=100)
        assert [row["event_kind"] for row in first["records"]] == ["arrival"]
        assert first["records"][0]["observation_source"] == "platform_database"
        assert first["records"][0]["evidence_kind"] == "actual_e2e"

        assignment = db.scalar(select(IncrementalEntityAssignment))
        db.add(IncrementalShadowResult(assignment_id=assignment.id, run_key=run.run_key, step_key="shadow",
            workflow_version_id=run.workflow_version_id, snapshot_hash="a" * 64, event_id="observe-1",
            source_version="v1", policy_revision=assignment.policy_id, result_json="{}", result_hash="b" * 64,
            created_at=run.finished_at + timedelta(seconds=5)))
        db.commit()
        observed = observe_capacity_records(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition_id,
            since=now - timedelta(days=1), until=now + timedelta(days=1), max_rows=100)
        completion = next(row for row in observed["records"] if row["event_kind"] == "completion")
        assert completion["event_id"] == "observe-1"
        assert completion["latency_ms"] == 10_000
        assert completion["destination"] == "shadow"
        assert completion["local_shadow_verified"] is True
        assert "backlog" not in completion and "drain_seconds" not in completion
        assert completion["fanout"] == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_observer_is_bounded_and_marks_truncation(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        for event_id in ("cap-1", "cap-2"):
            assert client.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch),
                json=_payload(event_id=event_id, asset_id=asset.id)).status_code == 200
        first_run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        values = {column.name: getattr(first_run, column.name) for column in ProductionRun.__table__.columns if column.name != "id"}
        values.update(run_key="observer-second-run", idempotency_key="observer-second-idempotency")
        db.add(ProductionRun(**values))
        db.commit()
        definition_id = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        result = observe_capacity_records(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition_id,
            since=now - timedelta(days=1), until=now + timedelta(days=1), max_rows=1)
        assert len(result["records"]) == 1
        assert result["truncated"] is True
        assert "observer_rows_truncated" in result["acceptance_warnings"]
        assert result["records"][0]["observer_truncated"] is True
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_observer_rejects_unbounded_or_invalid_windows(db_session=None) -> None:
    # Validation occurs before querying and therefore needs no database fixture.
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    try:
        observe_capacity_records(None, category_key="x", workflow_definition_id=1,
            since=now, until=now + timedelta(days=32), max_rows=1)
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "31 days" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError("oversized observer window was accepted")


def test_observe_cli_requires_bounded_scope_and_jsonl_output(tmp_path) -> None:
    args = parser().parse_args(["observe", "--category-key", "space_image",
        "--workflow-definition-id", "7", "--since", "2026-09-01T00:00:00+00:00",
        "--until", "2026-09-02T00:00:00+00:00", "--max-rows", "50",
        "--database-url-env", "OBSERVER_TEST_DATABASE_URL", "--output", str(tmp_path / "evidence.jsonl")])
    assert args.command == "observe"
    assert args.database_url_env == "OBSERVER_TEST_DATABASE_URL"
    assert args.max_rows == 50


def test_e2e_latency_includes_queue_run_and_late_formal_readback() -> None:
    ingress = datetime(2026, 9, 6, 8, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    run_finished = ingress + timedelta(minutes=4)
    readback = ingress + timedelta(minutes=10)
    assert _e2e_completion_timing(ingress, run_finished, readback) == (readback, 600_000.0)
    assert _e2e_completion_timing(ingress, run_finished, None) is None
    assert _e2e_completion_timing(ingress, run_finished, ingress - timedelta(seconds=1)) is None


def test_foreach_child_calls_are_not_retries_but_same_paid_call_is() -> None:
    calls = [
        SimpleNamespace(node_instance_id="score_each", invocation_key="image-1"),
        SimpleNamespace(node_instance_id="score_each", invocation_key="image-2"),
        SimpleNamespace(node_instance_id="score_each", invocation_key="image-1"),
    ]
    assert _paid_call_retry_flags(calls) == [False, False, True]


def test_observer_includes_overdue_unfinished_high_item_not_just_completions(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED', '1')
    monkeypatch.setenv('INCREMENTAL_SLA_HIGH_CATEGORIES', 'space_image')
    engine, db, client, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        response = client.post('/api/content-ingress/events', headers=_headers(monkeypatch),
            json=_payload(event_id='unfinished-sla', asset_id=asset.id))
        assert response.status_code == 200
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun))
        event = db.get(ContentIngressEvent, run.source_id)
        first = event.received_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        now = first + timedelta(hours=6)
        # Advance only the observation clock; ingress events are append-only.
        import app.incremental_capacity_observer as observer
        class ObservationClock(datetime):
            @classmethod
            def now(cls, tz=None):
                return now
        monkeypatch.setattr(observer, 'datetime', ObservationClock)
        result = observe_capacity_records(db, category_key='space_image',
            workflow_definition_id=run.workflow_definition_id, since=now - timedelta(hours=7),
            until=now, max_rows=100)
        item = next(row for row in result['records'] if row['event_kind'] == 'sla')
        assert item['event_id'] == 'unfinished-sla' and item['service_class'] == 'high'
        assert item['usable_result_at'] is None and item['sla_violated'] is True
        assert datetime.fromisoformat(item['first_queued_at']) == first
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.incremental_input import (
    IncrementalCaseAwaitingMaterial,
    IncrementalInputError,
    build_frozen_content_ingress_input,
    frozen_input_hash,
    validate_frozen_content_ingress_input,
)
from app.incremental_mechanisms import set_incremental_mechanism
from app.label_governance import ingest_content_event, route_content_event_to_incremental_package

def _case_payload(first: Asset, second: Asset, *, ready: bool = True) -> dict:
    return {
        "content_id": "case-42",
        "content_version": "v2",
        "category_key": "design_case",
        "case": {
            "id": "case-42",
            "ready": ready,
            "expected_image_ids": ["image-a", "image-b"],
            "cloud_id": "case-42",
            "project_id": "project-42",
            "case_name": "双图案例",
            "case_text": "完整案例",
            "quality_score": 10,
            "time_score": 5,
            "filter_threshold": 20,
            "images": [
                {"cloud_id": "image-a", "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/a.jpg", "asset_id": first.id, "asset_sha256": first.sha256},
                {"cloud_id": "image-b", "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/b.jpg", "asset_id": second.id, "asset_sha256": second.sha256},
            ],
        },
    }


def _fixture(payload_builder=_case_payload):
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine)
    first = Asset(original_name="a.jpg", stored_name="a.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1, sha256="a" * 64, category_key="design_case")
    second = Asset(original_name="b.jpg", stored_name="b.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1, sha256="b" * 64, category_key="design_case")
    db.add_all([first, second]); db.flush()
    payload = payload_builder(first, second)
    record = ContentRecord(source_system="stub", source_content_id="case-42", category_key="design_case", source_version="v2", source_occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), asset_id=first.id, status="ready")
    db.add(record); db.flush()
    event = ContentIngressEvent(event_id="case-event-v2", schema_version="content-ingress-v1", event_type="content.updated", source_system="stub", occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), payload_hash=hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(payload).encode()).hexdigest(), payload_json=canonical_json(payload), content_record_id=record.id, status="applied", received_by="test")
    db.add(event); db.flush()
    return engine, db, event, record, first, second


def test_complete_case_freezes_exact_images_and_validates_all_package_items() -> None:
    engine, db, event, record, first, second = _fixture()
    try:
        frozen = build_frozen_content_ingress_input(db, event=event, record=record)
        assert frozen["case"]["id"] == frozen["source_content_id"] == "case-42"
        assert [item["cloud_id"] for item in frozen["case"]["images"]] == ["image-a", "image-b"]
        assert frozen["case"]["expected_image_ids"] == ["image-a", "image-b"]
        validate_frozen_content_ingress_input(db, event=event, input_manifest=frozen, input_manifest_hash=frozen_input_hash(frozen), package_category_key="design_case", package_item_asset_ids=[first.id, second.id])
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("mutation", ["not_ready", "missing_image", "duplicate_image", "wrong_hash", "wrong_case_id"])
def test_case_contract_fails_closed_until_complete_and_immutable(mutation: str) -> None:
    engine, db, event, record, _first, _second = _fixture()
    try:
        payload = json.loads(event.payload_json)
        case = payload["case"]
        if mutation == "not_ready": case["ready"] = False
        elif mutation == "missing_image": case["images"].pop()
        elif mutation == "duplicate_image": case["images"][1]["cloud_id"] = "image-a"
        elif mutation == "wrong_hash": case["images"][1]["asset_sha256"] = "c" * 64
        else: case["id"] = "another-case"
        event.payload_json = canonical_json(payload)
        error = IncrementalCaseAwaitingMaterial if mutation in {"not_ready", "missing_image"} else IncrementalInputError
        with pytest.raises(error):
            build_frozen_content_ingress_input(db, event=event, record=record)
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_validator_rejects_tampered_case_image_set() -> None:
    engine, db, event, record, first, second = _fixture()
    try:
        frozen = build_frozen_content_ingress_input(db, event=event, record=record)
        with pytest.raises(IncrementalInputError):
            validate_frozen_content_ingress_input(db, event=event, input_manifest=frozen, input_manifest_hash=frozen_input_hash(frozen), package_category_key="design_case", package_item_asset_ids=[first.id])
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_complete_case_ingress_packages_every_image_and_freezes_case() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        versions = seed_incremental_versions(db, category_key="design_case", count=1)
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key="design_case", workflow_version_id=versions[0].id, expected_lock_version=None, reason="case test", actor="test")
        first = Asset(original_name="a.jpg", stored_name="a.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1, sha256="a" * 64, category_key="design_case")
        second = Asset(original_name="b.jpg", stored_name="b.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1, sha256="b" * 64, category_key="design_case")
        db.add_all([first, second]); db.flush()
        payload = _case_payload(first, second)
        event, record, duplicate = ingest_content_event(db, event_id="complete-case", schema_version="content-ingress-v1", event_type="content.created", source_system="stub", occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), payload=payload, received_by="test")
        package, created, status = route_content_event_to_incremental_package(db, event=event, record=record, duplicate=duplicate, actor="test")
        assert package is not None and created is True
        assert status == "packaged"
        items = db.scalars(select(MaterialPackageItem).where(MaterialPackageItem.package_id == package.id).order_by(MaterialPackageItem.position)).all()
        assert [item.asset_id for item in items] == [first.id, second.id]
        assert json.loads(package.mechanism_snapshot_json)["input_manifest"]["case"]["images"][1]["cloud_id"] == "image-b"
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_incomplete_case_waits_and_later_complete_source_version_becomes_ready() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        seed_incremental_versions(db, category_key="design_case", count=1)
        first = Asset(original_name="a.jpg", stored_name="a.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1, sha256="a" * 64, category_key="design_case")
        second = Asset(original_name="b.jpg", stored_name="b.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1, sha256="b" * 64, category_key="design_case")
        db.add_all([first, second]); db.flush()
        incomplete = _case_payload(first, second, ready=False)
        event_v1, record, _ = ingest_content_event(db, event_id="case-v1", schema_version="content-ingress-v1", event_type="content.created", source_system="stub", occurred_at=datetime(2026, 9, 6, tzinfo=timezone.utc), payload=incomplete, received_by="test")
        assert event_v1.status == "awaiting_material"
        assert record.status == "awaiting_material"
        from app.incremental_observability import waiting_material_count
        assert waiting_material_count(db, "design_case") == 1
        complete = _case_payload(first, second)
        complete["content_version"] = "v2"
        event_v2, updated, _ = ingest_content_event(db, event_id="case-v2", schema_version="content-ingress-v1", event_type="content.updated", source_system="stub", occurred_at=datetime(2026, 9, 6, 0, 0, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc), payload=complete, received_by="test")
        assert event_v2.status == "applied"
        assert updated.status == "ready"
        assert updated.source_version == "v2"
        assert waiting_material_count(db, "design_case") == 0
    finally:
        db.close(); engine.dispose()


def test_complete_case_runs_real_native_graph_into_shadow_without_formal_outbox(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    """Derived incremental version closes ingress -> foreach -> aggregate -> shadow."""
    from copy import deepcopy
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

    from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
    from app.connector_contracts import create_connector_contract_version
    from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_content_event
    from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, set_admission
    from app.legacy_workflow_release import materialize_legacy_workflows
    from app.models import (
        IncrementalShadowResult,
        ModelConfig,
        ProductionRun,
        RuntimeOutboxEvent,
        WorkflowDefinition,
        WorkflowForeachItem,
        WorkflowModelInvocation,
        WorkflowVersion,
    )
    from app.workflow_registry import create_workflow_version, persisted_workflow_manifest
    from tests.test_legacy_workflow_release import _workflow
    from tests.test_native_workflow_real_regression import _real_jpeg_bytes
    from tests.test_strict_prompt_release import _prepare_database
    from tests.v3_contract_fixtures import add_active_v3_contract
    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    engine = _prepare_database(tmp_path)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    first_bytes = _real_jpeg_bytes(width=64, height=48)
    second_bytes = _real_jpeg_bytes(width=65, height=48)
    (tmp_path / "case-a.jpg").write_bytes(first_bytes)
    (tmp_path / "case-b.jpg").write_bytes(second_bytes)
    with sessions() as db:
        materialize_legacy_workflows(db, actor="case-incremental-test", apply=True)
        add_active_v3_contract(db, "design_case")
        for direction, key in (("upstream", "design-case-input"), ("downstream", "design-case-output")):
            create_connector_contract_version(db, contract_key=key, direction=direction, category_key="design_case", logical_locator=f"logical.design_case.{direction}", adapter_key="api-json-v1", schema_fingerprint=hashlib.sha256(key.encode()).hexdigest(), field_mapping={"content_id": "content_id"}, cursor_contract={"field": "sequence", "kind": "integer", "ordering": "ascending", "out_of_order": "delay"} if direction == "upstream" else {}, idempotency_contract={"field": "event_id" if direction == "upstream" else "business_idempotency_key"}, batch_size=100, timeout_seconds=30, owner="test", status="active", created_by="test")
        released = _workflow(db, "design_case.legacy_doubao_review")
        manifest = deepcopy(persisted_workflow_manifest(released))
        for node in manifest["nodes"]:
            if node["kind"] == "upstream_connector": node["config"]["connector_key"] = "design-case-input"
            if node["kind"] == "downstream_projection": node["config"]["connector_key"] = "design-case-output"
        manifest["run_modes"] = [*manifest["run_modes"], "incremental"]
        manifest["resource_policy"]["incremental_autorun"] = {
            "enabled": True,
            "batch_size": 1,
            "max_wait_seconds": 0,
            "immediate_priorities": ["P0"],
            "model_execution_mode": "real",
        }
        case_schema = manifest["input_schema"]["properties"]["case"]
        case_schema["properties"].update({
            "id": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
            "ready": {"type": "boolean", "const": True},
            "expected_image_ids": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1}, "minItems": 1, "uniqueItems": True},
        })
        case_schema["required"].extend(["id", "ready", "expected_image_ids"])
        definition = db.get(WorkflowDefinition, released.workflow_definition_id)
        derived = create_workflow_version(db, definition=definition, version="case-incremental-test-v1", manifest=manifest, created_by="test")
        derived.status = "active"
        derived.validation_report_json = canonical_json({"ok": True, "errors": []})
        for model in db.scalars(select(ModelConfig)).all():
            model.encrypted_api_key = "opaque-test-secret-reference"
            model.max_input_tokens = 128_000
            model.input_micros_per_million_tokens = 1_000_000
            model.output_micros_per_million_tokens = 2_000_000
            model.benchmark_enabled = True
        monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_model_budget, "secret_reference_available", lambda value: bool(value))
        set_incremental_mechanism(db, category_key="design_case", workflow_version_id=derived.id, expected_lock_version=None, reason="derived case acceptance", actor="test")
        from app.incremental_mechanisms import resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue
        resolve_incremental_mechanism_for_enqueue(db, "design_case")
        policy = configure_policy(db, category_key="design_case", workflow_definition_id=definition.id, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="local shadow acceptance", actor="test")
        set_admission(db, category_key="design_case", workflow_definition_id=definition.id, expected_revision=policy["revision"], paused=False, reason="local acceptance", actor="test")
        first = Asset(original_name="case-a.jpg", stored_name="case-a.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=len(first_bytes), sha256=hashlib.sha256(first_bytes).hexdigest(), category_key="design_case")
        second = Asset(original_name="case-b.jpg", stored_name="case-b.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=len(second_bytes), sha256=hashlib.sha256(second_bytes).hexdigest(), category_key="design_case")
        db.add_all([first, second]); db.flush()
        payload = _case_payload(first, second)
        event, record, duplicate = ingest_content_event(db, event_id="case-real-shadow-v1", schema_version="content-ingress-v1", event_type="content.created", source_system="local-case-stub", occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), payload=payload, received_by="test")
        dispatch = dispatch_content_event(db, event=event, record=record, duplicate=duplicate, actor="test")
        from app.models import AuditEvent
        deferred = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == "incremental_lane_deferred").order_by(AuditEvent.id.desc()))
        assert dispatch.routing_status == "dispatched", json.loads(deferred.payload_json) if deferred else None
        run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key == dispatch.run_key))
        assert run is not None
        assert json.loads(run.snapshot_json)["runtime_context"]["real_execution_budget"]["expected_requests"] == 3
        run_id = run.id
        db.commit()

    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as worker
    import app.workflow_model_executor as model_executor
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    monkeypatch.setattr(model_executor, "get_settings", lambda: SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path, nas_maps_root=None))
    calls: list[str] = []
    image_output = {"title": "案例图", "seo_title": "案例图片", "category": "建筑设计", "sub_category": "住宅", "style": "现代", "tags": ["空间", "建筑", "材质", "光影"], "cons": "无", "design": "空间清晰", "score": 82, "reason": "", "image_defects": "", "trait": "实景照片"}
    case_output = {"score": 70, "reason": "内容完整", "seo_title": "现代住宅案例", "category_one": "建筑设计", "category_two": "住宅", "style": "现代", "matcraft": ["石材"], "designtactic": ["借景"], "spaceshape": ["开放"], "clientInfo": "无", "projattr": "住宅", "designer": "无", "region_type": "国内", "location_detail": "无", "scale": "无", "dtime": "无", "btime": "无"}

    class FakeProvider:
        def __init__(self, config): self.config = config
        def response(self, parsed, options, count):
            calls.append("image" if count else "text")
            return DoubaoResponse(parsed=parsed, raw_text=canonical_json(parsed), raw_payload={"id": f"case-{len(calls)}"}, upstream_status_code=200, request_correlation_id=f"case-{len(calls)}", attempt_count=1, output_budget=options["output_budget"], reasoning_effort=options["reasoning_effort"], thinking_mode=self.config.thinking_mode, input_image_tokens=80 * count, per_image_tokens=tuple(80 for _ in range(count)), input_tokens=200 + 80 * count, output_tokens=60, total_tokens=260 + 80 * count)
        async def chat_json_images(self, system_prompt, image_samples, **options): return self.response(image_output, options, len(image_samples))
        async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options): return self.response(case_output, options, 0)

    processed = 0
    while worker.process_runtime_step_once("case-shadow-worker", model_client_type=FakeProvider):
        processed += 1
        assert processed <= 12
    with sessions() as db:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
        assert run is not None and run.status == "succeeded", (run.error_code, run.error_message)
        assert calls == ["image", "image", "text"]
        children = db.scalars(select(WorkflowForeachItem).where(WorkflowForeachItem.production_run_id == run_id).order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal)).all()
        assert [(item.item_key, item.status) for item in children] == [("image-a", "succeeded"), ("image-b", "succeeded")]
        assert len(db.scalars(select(WorkflowModelInvocation).where(WorkflowModelInvocation.production_run_id == run_id)).all()) == 3
        shadow = db.scalar(select(IncrementalShadowResult).where(IncrementalShadowResult.run_key == run.run_key))
        assert shadow is not None and shadow.result_hash
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id))) == 0

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_continuous.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app.models import ProductionRun, WorkflowVersion
from app.incremental_routing import configure_policy, current_policy
from test_incremental_automation import _operator
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close, _headers, _payload


def test_master_switch_resumes_admission_and_dispatch_with_atomic_revisions(monkeypatch):
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition)
        configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="配置", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        operator = _operator(db)
        path = f"/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{definition}/operation"
        request = dict(enabled=True, expected_revision=0, expected_routing_revision=1, reason="开启")
        response = operator.put(path, json=request)
        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        assert current_policy(db, **scope).admission_paused is False
        rejected = operator.put(path, json={**request, "enabled": False, "expected_revision": 1})
        assert rejected.status_code == 409
        assert current_policy(db, **scope).admission_paused is False
        result = ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="master-live", asset_id=asset.id))
        assert result.status_code == 200, result.text
        assert result.json()["production_run_key"]
        assert operator.put(path, json={**request, "enabled": False, "expected_revision": 1, "expected_routing_revision": 2}).status_code == 200
        assert current_policy(db, **scope).admission_paused is True
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_backlog_source_failure_isolated_and_newer_source_supersedes(monkeypatch):
    from app.models import IncrementalAdmissionBacklog, ContentRecord
    from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_due_incremental_batches
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        configure_policy(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition,
            expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="暂停", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        for i in range(3):
            payload = _payload(event_id=f"backlog-{i}", asset_id=asset.id)
            payload["payload"]["content_id"] = f"entity-{i}"
            response = ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload)
            assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        # A missing record and an advanced source version are independent of
        # the third valid event. Neither may prevent that event from running.
        rows = db.scalars(select(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog).order_by(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.id)).all()
        from app.models import ContentIngressEvent
        import app.label_governance as governance
        original_dispatch = governance.route_content_event_to_incremental_package
        def failing_dispatch(db, **kwargs):
            if kwargs["event"].id == rows[0].event_id:
                raise RuntimeError("injected unavailable dependency")
            return original_dispatch(db, **kwargs)
        monkeypatch.setattr(governance, "route_content_event_to_incremental_package", failing_dispatch)
        newer = db.get(ContentRecord, db.get(ContentIngressEvent, rows[1].event_id).content_record_id)
        newer.source_version = "newer-version"
        db.commit()
        path = f"/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{definition}/operation"
        assert _operator(db).put(path, json=dict(enabled=True, expected_revision=0, expected_routing_revision=1, reason="开始")).status_code == 200
        dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor="worker", now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=1))
        db.commit()
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
        assert rows[0].failure_count == 1
        assert rows[0].status == "pending"
        assert rows[1].status == "superseded"
        assert rows[2].status == "completed"
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_schema_103_upgrade_creates_backlog_without_changing_events():
    from app.models import IncrementalAdmissionBacklog
    from app.migrations import run_migrations
    from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        db.commit()
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            IncrementalAdmissionBacklog.__table__.drop(connection)
            connection.execute(text("DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 104"))
            run_migrations(connection)
            run_migrations(connection)
            assert "incremental_admission_backlog" in inspect(connection).get_table_names()
            assert connection.execute(text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM category_incremental_mechanism_bindings")).scalar() == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_mysql_103_upgrade_reaches_additive_backlog_ddl(monkeypatch):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.migrations import mysql_baseline as migration
    calls = []
    connection = SimpleNamespace(dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="mysql"),
        exec_driver_sql=lambda sql, *args: calls.append((sql, args)) or SimpleNamespace(scalar_one_or_none=lambda: 103))
    table = SimpleNamespace(create=lambda connection, checkfirst: calls.append(("create backlog", checkfirst)))
    monkeypatch.setattr(migration, "_require_mysql", lambda connection: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(migration, "mysql_metadata", lambda: SimpleNamespace(tables={"incremental_admission_backlog": table}))
    migration.upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=104)
    assert ("create backlog", True) in calls
    assert any(args == ((104, "incremental_admission_backlog"),) for _, args in calls)


def test_admission_backlog_obeys_shared_run_cap_and_reports_created_runs(monkeypatch):
    import app.incremental_autorun as autorun
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        configure_policy(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition,
            expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="暂停", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        for i in range(3):
            payload = _payload(event_id=f"capped-{i}", asset_id=asset.id)
            payload["payload"]["content_id"] = f"capped-{i}"

        path = f"/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{definition}/operation"
        assert _operator(db).put(path, json=dict(enabled=True, expected_revision=0, expected_routing_revision=1, reason="开始")).status_code == 200
        monkeypatch.setattr(autorun, "MAX_INCREMENTAL_RUNS_PER_TICK", 1)

            db.commit()

            assert db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(ProductionRun.run_key == created[0])) is not None

def test_admission_pause_backlog_drains_after_resume_without_event_replay(monkeypatch):
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        scope = dict(category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition)
        configure_policy(db, **scope, expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="配置", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        response = ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=_payload(event_id="admission-backlog", asset_id=asset.id))
        assert response.json()["routing_status"] == "routing_admission_paused"
        path = f"/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{definition}/operation"
        result = _operator(db).put(path, json=dict(enabled=True, expected_revision=0, expected_routing_revision=1, reason="开始"))
        assert result.status_code == 200, result.text
        from app.incremental_autorun import dispatch_due_incremental_batches
        dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor="worker", now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=1))
        db.commit()
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
        dispatch_due_incremental_batches(db, actor="worker", now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=2))
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_archived_scope_backlog_is_not_selected_and_cannot_starve_others(monkeypatch):
    # 复审 09-26：SQL 只排除 enabled=False，但 automation_paused 把 archived 也算暂停，
    # 命中后 continue 不推迟 retry_at；超过 100 条就永远占满每一页、饿死其它范围。
    from app.models import IncrementalAdmissionBacklog, IncrementalAutomationControl
    from app import incremental_admission, incremental_automation
    engine, db, ingress, asset = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion)).workflow_definition_id
        configure_policy(db, category_key="space_image", workflow_definition_id=definition,
            expected_revision=0, mode="shadow", ratio_percent=100, reason="暂停", actor="test")
        db.commit()
        for i in range(2):
            payload = _payload(event_id=f"archived-{i}", asset_id=asset.id)
            payload["payload"]["content_id"] = f"archived-entity-{i}"
            assert ingress.post("/api/content-ingress/events", headers=_headers(monkeypatch), json=payload).status_code == 200
        path = f"/api/incremental-mechanisms/automation/space_image/{definition}/operation"
        assert _operator(db).put(path, json=dict(enabled=True, expected_revision=0, expected_routing_revision=1, reason="开始")).status_code == 200
        control = db.scalar(select(IncrementalAutomationControl))
        control.archived = True
        db.commit()
        seen = []
        original = incremental_automation.automation_paused
        monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_automation, "automation_paused",
                            lambda db, **kw: seen.append(kw) or original(db, **kw))
        incremental_admission.resume_due_admissions(db, actor="worker", now=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=1))
        db.commit()
        assert seen == []
        rows = db.scalars(select(IncrementalAdmissionBacklog)).all()
        assert len(rows) == 2 and {row.status for row in rows} == {"pending"}
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 0
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_correction_analysis.py =====
"""增量纠偏分析：结论复用存量那套，候选自己生成，模型改不动结构。

这一组测试最要紧的不是「能不能生成候选」，而是**模型越界时会不会被拦住**。
候选最终要经 create_workflow_version 的 validate_workflow_manifest，但那是最后一道；
在它之前，normalize_edits 必须先把「改了不该改的节点/字段」挡掉，而且是抛错、
不是静默丢弃——静默丢弃会让候选看起来生成成功，实际上模型想改的一个都没落地，
运营还会拿它去跑回归。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from app.incremental_correction_analysis import (
    AnalysisError,
    EDITABLE_BY_KIND,
    GeneratedCandidate,
    NodeEdit,
    apply_edits,
    editable_surface,
    normalize_edits,
)

MANIFEST = {
    "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
    "category_key": "model_3d_su",
    "nodes": [
        {"node_instance_id": "upstream", "kind": "upstream_connector",
         "config": {"connector_key": "factory.prod.upstream"}},
        {"node_instance_id": "prompt_a", "kind": "prompt", "display_name": "提示词 A",
         "config": {"prompt_version_id": 7, "model_snapshot": {"secret": "never-shown"},
                    "component_snapshot": {"version": "A-1", "version_id": 7}}},
        {"node_instance_id": "matcher", "kind": "rule_module", "display_name": "等级撮合",
         "config": {"module_type_key": "score_level_matcher",
                    "component_snapshot": {"content_json": '{"levels":[{"level":"L1","min_score":80}]}'}}},
        {"node_instance_id": "downstream", "kind": "downstream_projection",
         "config": {"connector_key": "factory.prod.downstream"}},
    ],
    "edges": [{"from": "upstream", "to": "prompt_a"}],
}


def test_only_prompts_and_rule_modules_are_editable():
    assert set(EDITABLE_BY_KIND) == {"prompt", "rule_module"}
    assert EDITABLE_BY_KIND["prompt"] == {"system_prompt", "user_prompt"}
    assert EDITABLE_BY_KIND["rule_module"] == {"content"}


def test_a_prompt_edit_is_accepted():
    edits = normalize_edits({"prompt_a": {"system_prompt": "新的系统提示"}}, MANIFEST)
    assert edits == (NodeEdit("prompt_a", "prompt", {"system_prompt": "新的系统提示"}),)


def test_a_rule_module_content_edit_is_accepted():
    content = {"levels": [{"level": "L1", "min_score": 85}]}
    edits = normalize_edits({"matcher": {"content": content}}, MANIFEST)
    assert edits[0].kind == "rule_module" and edits[0].fields["content"] == content


@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw,code", [
    ({"downstream": {"content": {}}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_node_read_only"),
    ({"upstream": {"system_prompt": "x"}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_node_read_only"),
    ({"nope": {"system_prompt": "x"}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_unknown_node"),
    ({"prompt_a": {"model_snapshot": {}}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_field_forbidden"),
    ({"prompt_a": {"prompt_version_id": 99}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_field_forbidden"),
    ({"matcher": {"module_type_key": "x"}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_field_forbidden"),
    ({"prompt_a": {}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_invalid"),
    ({"prompt_a": {"system_prompt": "   "}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_invalid"),
    ({"prompt_a": {"system_prompt": 42}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_invalid"),
    ({"matcher": {"content": "not an object"}}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_invalid"),
    ({}, "incremental_analysis_candidate_empty"),
    (None, "incremental_analysis_candidate_empty"),
])
def test_an_out_of_bounds_edit_is_refused_not_dropped(raw, code):
    with pytest.raises(AnalysisError) as info:
        normalize_edits(raw, MANIFEST)
    assert info.value.code == code


def test_the_model_never_sees_connectors_or_credentials(monkeypatch):
    """可改面只给它能改的东西——看不到就无从改起，也省掉一次必然被拒的往返。"""

    class _Prompt:
        system_prompt, user_prompt = "系统", "用户"

    class _Db:
        def get(self, model, key):
            return _Prompt() if key == 7 else None

    surface = editable_surface(MANIFEST, _Db())
    ids = {item["node_instance_id"] for item in surface}
    assert ids == {"prompt_a", "matcher"}, "只有可改节点会出现在提示里"
    blob = json.dumps(surface, ensure_ascii=False)
    for forbidden in ("connector_key", "factory.prod", "model_snapshot", "never-shown", "prompt_version_id"):
        assert forbidden not in blob, f"可改面泄漏了 {forbidden}"
    matcher = next(item for item in surface if item["node_instance_id"] == "matcher")
    assert matcher["content"] == {"levels": [{"level": "L1", "min_score": 80}]}


def test_applying_edits_inherits_every_structural_field(monkeypatch):
    """候选 manifest 的结构必须逐字继承：节点集合、连线、种类、连接器都不能动。"""

    import app.incremental_correction_analysis as module

    class _Prompt:
        id, version = 99, "A-1+corr1-prompt_a"

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "_candidate_prompt", lambda *a, **k: _Prompt())
    edits = normalize_edits({
        "prompt_a": {"system_prompt": "改过的系统提示"},
        "matcher": {"content": {"levels": [{"level": "L1", "min_score": 85}]}},
    }, MANIFEST)
    candidate = apply_edits(None, MANIFEST, edits, analysis=None, actor="ops")

    assert [n["node_instance_id"] for n in candidate["nodes"]] == \
        [n["node_instance_id"] for n in MANIFEST["nodes"]]
    assert [n["kind"] for n in candidate["nodes"]] == [n["kind"] for n in MANIFEST["nodes"]]
    assert candidate["edges"] == MANIFEST["edges"]
    by_id = {n["node_instance_id"]: n for n in candidate["nodes"]}
    assert by_id["downstream"]["config"]["connector_key"] == "factory.prod.downstream"
    assert by_id["upstream"]["config"]["connector_key"] == "factory.prod.upstream"
    # 提示词换成了新版本，模型快照原样保留
    assert by_id["prompt_a"]["config"]["prompt_version_id"] == 99
    assert by_id["prompt_a"]["config"]["model_snapshot"] == {"secret": "never-shown"}
    assert by_id["matcher"]["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"] == \
        '{"levels":[{"level":"L1","min_score":85}]}'
    # 冻结版本本身一个字节都不能被改到
    assert MANIFEST["nodes"][2]["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_json"] == \
        '{"levels":[{"level":"L1","min_score":80}]}'


def test_a_rule_module_without_frozen_content_cannot_produce_a_candidate():
    manifest = json.loads(json.dumps(MANIFEST))
    del manifest["nodes"][2]["config"]["component_snapshot"]
    edits = normalize_edits({"matcher": {"content": {"levels": []}}}, manifest)
    with pytest.raises(AnalysisError) as info:
        apply_edits(None, manifest, edits, analysis=None, actor="ops")
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_analysis_manifest_invalid"


def test_the_generated_candidate_carries_what_the_panel_needs():
    candidate = GeneratedCandidate(
        edits=(NodeEdit("prompt_a", "prompt", {"system_prompt": "x"}),),
        summary="收紧 L1 阈值", rationale="报告显示 L1 有 62% 被改成 L2")
    assert candidate.summary and candidate.rationale and candidate.trace == []

===== FILE backend/tests/test_incremental_correction_analysis_api.py =====
"""增量纠偏分析的 HTTP 面：案例怎么流转，分析怎么发起，人工确认怎么落地。

发起分析会真实调用调优模型、要花钱，所以这里用一个假生成器，验证的是编排和状态机，
不是模型本身。模型越界的拦截在 test_incremental_correction_analysis.py 里单独测。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.incremental_correction_models import IncrementalCorrectionCase
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import User, WorkflowDefinition, WorkflowVersion


@pytest.fixture
def client():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    user = User(is_admin=True, role="admin", username="ops", password_hash="unused", display_name="运营")
    db.add(user)
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(workflow_key="wf", name="wf", owner="t", created_by="t")
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    db.add(WorkflowVersion(
        id=1, workflow_definition_id=definition.id, version="v1", status="active",
        step_manifest_json="[]", edge_manifest_json="[]", input_schema_json="{}",
        output_schema_json="{}", canonical_hash="a" * 64, created_by="t"))
    db.commit()

    def _db():
        yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = _db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        yield TestClient(app), db
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _case(db, index: int, *, kind="level", status="open", category="model_3d_su", version=1):
    case = IncrementalCorrectionCase(
        correction_key=f"key-{index}", evaluation_id=index, production_run_id=index,
        asset_id=index, category_key=category, workflow_version_id=version, kind=kind,
        node_instance_id="prompt_a" if kind == "field" else "",
        field_path="classification.doc_primary" if kind == "field" else "",
        ai_value_json=json.dumps("L3" if kind == "level" else "建筑"),
        human_value_json=json.dumps("L1" if kind == "level" else "室内"),
        reason=f"理由{index}", corrector="ops", status=status)
    db.add(case)
    db.commit()
    return case


def test_the_case_list_pages_and_filters(client):
    http, db = client
    for index in range(1, 8):
        _case(db, index, kind="level" if index % 2 else "field")
    _case(db, 99, kind="field", category="inspiration_image", version=2)

    body = http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases?limit=3").json()
    assert body["total"] == 8 and len(body["items"]) == 3 and body["has_more"] is True
    # 1..7 里奇数是 level（4 条），偶数和第 8 条是 field（4 条）
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases?kind=level&limit=50").json()["total"] == 4
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases?category_key=inspiration_image&limit=50").json()["total"] == 1
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases?workflow_version_id=2&limit=50").json()["total"] == 1
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases?status=open&limit=50").json()["total"] == 8


def test_an_unknown_case_status_is_refused(client):
    http, _ = client
    response = http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases?status=maybe")
    assert response.status_code == 422


def test_a_case_row_carries_both_sides_of_the_change(client):
    http, db = client
    _case(db, 1, kind="field")
    row = http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases?limit=1").json()["items"][0]
    assert row["kind"] == "field" and row["node_instance_id"] == "prompt_a"
    assert row["field_path"] == "classification.doc_primary"
    assert row["ai_value"] == "建筑" and row["human_value"] == "室内"
    assert row["corrector"] == "ops" and row["status"] == "open"


def test_the_filters_carry_their_own_counts(client):
    http, db = client
    _case(db, 1)
    _case(db, 2, status="dismissed")
    _case(db, 3, category="inspiration_image", version=2)
    options = http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases/filters").json()
    assert {item["category_key"] for item in options["categories"]} == {"model_3d_su", "inspiration_image"}
    assert {item["key"]: item["total"] for item in options["statuses"]}["dismissed"] == 1
    assert [item["key"] for item in options["kinds"]] == ["level", "field"]


def test_dismissing_a_case_keeps_it_but_takes_it_off_the_list(client):
    http, db = client
    case = _case(db, 1)
    response = http.post("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases/dismiss",
                         json={"case_ids": [case.id], "reason": "这条是素材本身有问题，不是机制问题"})
    assert response.status_code == 200 and response.json()["dismissed"] == 1
    db.expire_all()
    stored = db.scalar(select(IncrementalCorrectionCase))
    assert stored.status == "dismissed", "忽略不是删除"
    assert "这条是素材本身有问题" in stored.reason
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases?status=open&limit=50").json()["total"] == 0


def test_dismissing_without_a_reason_is_refused(client):
    http, db = client
    case = _case(db, 1)
    assert http.post("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/cases/dismiss",
                     json={"case_ids": [case.id], "reason": "  "}).status_code == 422


def test_cases_from_different_mechanisms_cannot_share_one_analysis(client):
    http, db = client
    first = _case(db, 1)
    other = _case(db, 2, version=2)
    response = http.post("/api/incremental-correction-analysis",
                         json={"case_ids": [first.id, other.id], "idempotency_key": "k1"})
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "incremental_analysis_cases_mixed"


def test_a_missing_case_is_a_404(client):
    http, _ = client
    response = http.post("/api/incremental-correction-analysis",
                         json={"case_ids": [4242], "idempotency_key": "k1"})
    assert response.status_code == 404
    assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "incremental_analysis_case_not_found"


def test_starting_an_analysis_without_a_tuning_model_says_so_plainly(client):
    """没配调优模型时要给一句人能看懂的话，而不是崩在里面。

    这是配置问题不是分析结果，所以它是 409、不留失败记录；相对地，
    「冻结机制不见了」那类是分析本身的失败，会留一条 failed 记录供运营看到。
    """

    http, db = client
    case = _case(db, 1)
    response = http.post("/api/incremental-correction-analysis",
                         json={"case_ids": [case.id], "idempotency_key": "k1"})
    assert response.status_code == 409
    detail = response.json()["detail"]
    assert detail["code"] == "incremental_analysis_tuning_model_missing"
    assert "调优模型" in detail["message"]
    # 失败不该把案例锁死：它们要能再被选进下一次分析。
    db.expire_all()
    assert db.scalar(select(IncrementalCorrectionCase)).status == "open"


def test_an_analysis_that_does_not_exist_is_a_404(client):
    http, _ = client
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/999").status_code == 404
    assert http.post("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/999/decision",
                     json={"decision": "adopt", "note": "x"}).status_code == 404


@pytest.mark.parametrize("payload", [
    {"decision": "maybe", "note": "x"},
    {"decision": "adopt", "note": ""},
    {"decision": "adopt", "note": "   "},
    {"note": "x"},
])
def test_an_incomplete_decision_is_refused(client, payload):
    http, _ = client
    assert http.post("/api/incremental-correction-analysis/1/decision",
                     json=payload).status_code == 422


def test_the_analysis_list_pages_and_filters(client):
    http, _ = client
    body = http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis?limit=5").json()
    assert body == {"items": [], "total": 0, "offset": 0, "limit": 5, "has_more": False}
    assert http.get("/api/incremental-correction-analysis?status=nope").status_code == 422
